# Siva Purana. Chapter 6

## Section 10 - Kailāsa-saṃhitā

## Chapter 1 - The discussion among Vyāsa, Śaunaka and others

## \|\| 10.1.1 \|\|

*namaḥ śivāya sāmbāya sagaṇāya sasūnave \|*  
*pradhānapuruṣeśāya sargasthitatyantahetave \|\| 1 \|\|*

1\. Obeisance to Śiva accompanied by Ambā, his sons and the Gaṇas, obeisance to the lord of Pradhāna and Puruṣa, obeisance to him who is the cause of creation, sustenance and dissolution.

## \|\| 10.1.2 \|\|

*ṛṣaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*śrutomāsaṃhitā ramyā nānākhyānasamanvitā \|*  
*kailāsasaṃhitāmbrūhi śivatattvavivarddhinīm \|\| 2 \|\|*

The sages said:—

2\. The beautiful Umāsaṃhitā full of various anecdotes we have heard. Now narrate the Kailāsasaṃhitā that propagates Śiva’s tenets.

## \|\| 10.1.3 \|\|

*vyāsa uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇuta prītito vatsāḥ kailāsākhyāṃ hi saṃhitām \|*  
*śivatattvaparāndivyāṃ vakṣye vaḥ snehataḥ parām \|\| 3 \|\|*

Vyāsa said:—

3\. O dear, now listen with pleasure. Out of affection for you I shall narrate the divine Saṃhitā Kailāsa expounding Śiva’s tenets.

## \|\| 10.1.4 \|\|

*himavacchikhare pūrvvaṃ tapasyanto mahaujasaḥ \|*  
*vārāṇasīṅgantukāmā munayaḥ kṛtasamvidaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Formerly the sages of great power and splendour performing penances on the summit of Himavat\[1\] desired to go to Vārāṇasī and decided accordingly.

## \|\| 10.1.5 \|\|

*nirgatya tasmātsamprāpya gireḥ kāśīṃ samāhitāḥ \|*  
*snātavyameveti tadā dadṛśurmaṇikarṇikām \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. They started from the mountain and reached Kāśī. They decided to bathe there and saw Maṇikarṇikā.

## \|\| 10.1.6-8 \|\|

*tatra snātvā susantapya devādīnatha jāhnavīm \|*  
*dṛṣṭvā snātvā munīśāste viśveśaṃ tridaśeśvaram \|\| 6 \|\|*

*namaskṛtyātha sampūjya bhaktyā paramayānvitāḥ \|*  
*śatarudrādibhiḥ stutvā stutibhirvvedapāragāḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*ātmānaṃ menire sarve kṛtārthā vayamityuta \|*  
*śivaprītyā supūrṇārthāśśivabhaktiratāssadā \|\| 8 \|\|*

6-8. The lordly sages bathed there and performed the Tarpaṇa rites to the gods and others and to Gaṅgā. They visited Viśveśa the lord of the gods, bowed and worshipped him with devotion The sages, the masters of the Vedas eulogised him with Śatarudra and other mantras and thought “O, we are blessed. We are content.” Due to Śiva’s pleasure they realised everything. They remained ever engaged in devotion to Śiva.

## \|\| 10.1.9 \|\|

*tasminnavasare sūtaṃ pañcakrośadidṛkṣayā \|*  
*gatvā samāgataṃ vīkṣya mudā te taṃ vavandire\|\| \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. In the meantime Sūta who had left his hermitage to visit Pañcakrośa reached there. On seeing him the joyous sages paid obeisance to him.

## \|\| 10.1.10 \|\|

*sopi viśveśvaraṃ sākṣāddevadevamumāpatim \|*  
*namaskṛtyātha taissākammuktimaṇḍapamāviśat \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. After bowing to Viśveśvara, the lord of gods, the consort of Umā, Sūta entered the Muktimaṇḍapa along with them.

## \|\| 10.1.11 \|\|

*tatrāsīnammahātmānaṃ sūtampaurāṇikottamam \|*  
*arghyādibhistadā sarvve munayassamupācaran \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. When Sūta the great soul, the excellent of the Paurāṇikas took his seat, the sages paid respects to him with Arghya and other ingredients of worship.

## \|\| 10.1.12 \|\|

*tataḥ sūtaḥ prasannātmā munīnālokya suvratān \|*  
*papraccha kuśalāntepi procuḥ kuśalamātmanaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The delighted Sūta saw the sages of auspicious rites and enquired after their health and welfare. They replied suitably.

## \|\| 10.1.13 \|\|

*te tu saṃhṛṣṭahṛdayaṃ jñātvā taṃ vai munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*praṇavārthāvagatyarthamūcuḥ prāstāvikaṃ vacaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. On seeing him pleased at heart the sages spoke in relevance to the context, in order to know the meaning of Praṇava.

## \|\| 10.1.14 \|\|

*munaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*vyāsaśiṣya mahābhāga sūta paurāṇikottama \|*  
*dhanyastvaṃ śivabhakto hi sarvavijñāna sāgaraḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

The sages said:—

14\. O blessed disciple of Vyāsa, O Sūta the best among the Paurāṇikas, you are a great devotee of Śiva, the ocean of perfect knowledge.

## \|\| 10.1.15 \|\|

*bhavantameva bhagavānvyāsassarvajagadguruḥ \|*  
*abhiṣicya purāṇānāṃ gurutve samayojayat \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Lord Vyāsa, the preceptor of the universe has crowned you as the chief of the Paurāṇikas and made you the propounder of the Purāṇas.

## \|\| 10.1.16 \|\|

*tasmātpaurāṇikī vidyā bhavato hṛdi saṃsthitā \|*  
*purāṇāni ca sarvāṇi vedārthampravadanti hi \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. The Purāṇic lore is firmly set in your heart. Indeed the Purāṇas explain the meaning of the Vedas.

## \|\| 10.1.17 \|\|

*vedāḥ praṇavasambhūtāḥ praṇavārtho maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*ato maheśvarasthānaṃ tvayi dhiṣṇyampratiṣṭhitam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Vedas arise from the Praṇava. The Praṇava is lord Śiva. Hence you are the permanent abode of lord Śiva’s stay.

## \|\| 10.1.18 \|\|

*tvanmukhābjaparisyandanmakaraṃde manoharam \|*  
*praṇavārthāmṛtaṃ pītvā bhaviṣyāmo gatajvarāḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. We shall be free from illness after we have drunk the nectar of Praṇava which is as sweet and pleasing as the honey oozing out of your lotus-mouth.

## \|\| 10.1.19 \|\|

*viśeṣato gurustvaṃ hi nānyo'smākaṃ mahāmate \|*  
*paraṃ bhāvaṃ maheśasya parayā kṛpayā vada \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. O intelligent one, you alone are our preceptor, none else. Out of compassion please narrate the nature of lord Śiva.

## \|\| 10.1.20-21 \|\|

*iti teṣāṃ vacaḥ śrutvā sūto vyāsapriyassudhīḥ \|*  
*gaṇeśaṃ ṣaṇmukhaṃ sākṣānmaheśānaṃ maheśvarīm \|\| 20 \|\|*

*śilādatanayaṃ devaṃ nandīśaṃ suyaśāpatim \|*  
*sanatkumāraṃ vyāsaṃ ca praṇipatyedamabravīt \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. On hearing their words, the intelligent Sūta, the beloved disciple of Vyāsa, made obeisance to Gaṇeśa, six-faced Kārttika, Maheśvarī, lord Nandīśa–son of Śilāda and husband of Suyaśā, Sanatkumāra and Vyāsa and said.

## \|\| 10.1.22 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*sādhusādhu mahābhāgā munayaḥ kṣīṇakalmaṣāḥ \|*  
*matirdṛḍhatarā jātā durlabhā sāpi duṣkṛtām \|\| 22 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

22\. Well done, well done, O blessed sages, your sins have been quelled. Your minds are steadied. It is rare in persons of evil deeds.

## \|\| 10.1.23-24 \|\|

*pārāśaryeṇa guruṇā naimiṣāraṇyavāsinām \|*  
*munīnāmupadiṣṭaṃ yadvakṣye tanmunipuṃgavāḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

*yasya śravaṇamātreṇa śivabhaktirbhavennṛṇām \|*  
*sāvadhānā bhavantodya śṛṇvantu parayā mudā \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. O leading sages, I shall narrate to you what was mentioned by my preceptor Vyāsa to the sages in the Naimiṣa forest. On hearing this alone men are blessed with devotion to Śiva. You shall listen with attention and joy.

## \|\| 10.1.25-27 \|\|

*svārociṣentare pūrvaṃ tapasyaṃto dṛḍhavratāḥ \|*  
*ṛṣayo naimiṣāraṇye sarvasiddhaniṣevite \|\| 25 \|\|*

*dīrghasatraṃ vitanvanto rudramadhvaranāyakam \|*  
*prīṇayantaḥ paraṃ bhāvamaiśvaryyaṃ jñātumicchavaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

*nivasanti sma te sarve vyāsadarśanakāṃkṣiṇaḥ \|*  
*śivabhaktiratā nityaṃ bhasmarudrākṣadhāriṇaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

25-27. Formerly in the Svārociṣa Manvantara, sages of steady rites performed penance in the Naimiṣa forest where all the Siddhas live. They conducted a sacrifice of long duration, propitiated Rudra the leader of sacrifices. They desired to know the supreme nature of the lord. They waited for Vyāsa. They were engaged in devotion to Śiva and wore Rudrākṣa after smearing their bodies with the ashes.

## \|\| 10.1.28 \|\|

*teṣāṃ bhāvaṃ samālokya bhagavānbādarāyaṇaḥ \|*  
*prādurbabhūva sarvātmā parāśaratapaḥphalam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. Observing their eagerness, lord Bādarāyaṇa, the soul of all, the fruit of the penance of Parāśara appeared before them.

## \|\| 10.1.29 \|\|

*taṃ dṛṣṭvā munayassarve prahṛṣṭavadanekṣaṇāḥ \|*  
*abhyutthānādibhissarvairupacārairupācaran \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. On seeing him the sages were delighted with beaming eyes and faces. They stood up and welcomed him with reverence.

## \|\| 10.1.30 \|\|

*satkṛtya pradadustasmai sauvarṇaṃ viṣṭaraṃ śubham \|*  
*sukhopaviṣṭaḥ sa tadā tasminsauvarṇaviṣṭare \|*  
*prāha gaṃbhīrayā vācā pārāśaryyo mahāmuniḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. With due hospitality they offered him a golden scat. Seated cosily on the golden seat the sage Vyāsa spoke majestically.

## \|\| 10.1.31 \|\|

*vyāsa uvāca \|*  
*kuśalaṃ kiṃ nu yuṣmākamprabrūtāsminmahāmakhe \|*  
*arcitaṃ kiṃ nu yuṣmābhissamyagadhvaranāyakaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

Vyāsa said:—

31\. Hope you are quite well. Please say. Has the leader of the sacrifices been worshipped well by you in this sacrifice?

## \|\| 10.1.32 \|\|

*kimarthamatra yuṣmābhiradhvare parameśvaraḥ \|*  
*svarcito bhaktibhāvena sāmbassaṃsāramocakaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Why lord Śiva accompanied by Ambā, the lord who releases from worldly bondage has been worshipped with due devotion by you all in this sacrifice.

## \|\| 10.1.33 \|\|

*yuṣmatpravṛttirme bhāti śuśrūṣā pūrvameva hi \|*  
*parabhāve maheśasya muktihetośśivasya ca \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Your activity seems to me to be the outcome of your desire to hear more of the great nature of lord Śiva, the cause of salvation.

## \|\| 10.1.34-35 \|\|

*evamuktā munīndreṇa vyāsenāmitatejasā \|*  
*munayo naimiṣāraṇyavāsinaḥ paramaujasaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

*praṇipatya mahātmānaṃ pārāśaśaryyaṃ mahāmunim \|*  
*śivānurāgasaṃhṛṣṭamānasaṃ ca tamabruvan \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. Thus urged by the sage Vyāsa of great brilliance the great sages of the Naimiṣa forest bowed to him, the noble soul whose mind was delighted by his devotion to Śiva.

## \|\| 10.1.36-37 \|\|

*munaya ūcuḥ \|*  
*bhagavanmuniśārdūla sākṣānnārāyaṇāṃśaja \|*  
*kṛpānidhe mahāprājña sarvavidyādhipa prabho \|\| 36 \|\|*

*tvaṃ hi sarvajagadbharturmahā devasya vedhasaḥ \|*  
*sāmbasya sagaṇasyāsya prasādānāṃ nidhissvayam \|\| 37 \|\|*

The sages said:—

36-37. O holy lord, O leading sage, born of Nārāyaṇa’s part, O storehouse of mercy, O intelligent one, O holy lord of all lores, you are the storehouse of the favours of Mahādeva, lord of the universe, accompanied by Ambā and the Gaṇas.

## \|\| 10.1.38 \|\|

*tvatpādābjarasāsvādamadhupāyitamānasāḥ \|*  
*kṛtārthā vayamadyaiva bhavatpādābjadarśanāt \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Our minds have been yearning for the sight of your lotus feet even as the bees yearn for the touch of lotus flowers. We are now satisfied by the vision of your lotus feet.

## \|\| 10.1.39 \|\|

*tvadīyacaraṇāmbhojadarśanaṃ khalu pāpinām \|*  
*durlabhaṃ labdhamasmābhistvasmātsukṛtino vayam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Inaccessible indeed is the sight of your lotus feet to the sinners. Since now we have obtained it we are really fortunate.

## \|\| 10.1.40-42 \|\|

*asmindeśe mahābhāga naimiṣāraṇyasaṃjñake \|*  
*dīrghasatrānvitāssarve praṇavārthaprakāśakāḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

*śrotavyaḥ parameśāna iti kṛtvā viniścitāḥ \|*  
*parasparaṃ cintayantaḥ paraṃ bhāvaṃ maheśitu \|\| 41 \|\|*

*ajñātavanta evaite vayaṃ tasmādbhavānprabho \|*  
*chettumarhati tānsarvānsaṃśayānalpacetasām \|\| 42 \|\|*

40-42. O blessed one, we are performing this long sacrifice in this Naimiṣāraṇya. We wanted to know the meaning of the Praṇava. We are discussing the greatness of lord Śiva and wish to hear all about him. But we cannot understand the same. Now it behoves you to clear off our doubts. O holy lord, we are only small-minded.

## \|\| 10.1.43-44 \|\|

*tvadanyaḥ saṃśayasyāsyacchettā na hi jagattraye \|*  
*tasmādapāragaṃbhīravyāmohābdhau nimajjataḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*tārayasva śivajñānapotenāsmāndayānidhe \|*  
*śivasadbhaktitattvārthaṃ jñātuṃ śraddhālavo vayam \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. There is none else in the three worlds to clear our doubts. We are immersed in the ocean of delusion, vast and shoreless. O storehouse of mercy, take us across this ocean in the raft of Śiva’s knowledge. We are truly eager to know the principle of Śiva’s devotion.

## \|\| 10.1.45-46 \|\|

*evamabhyarthitasta munibhirvedapāragaiḥ \|*  
*sarvavedārthavinmukhyaḥ śukatāto mahāmuniḥ \|*  
*vedāntasārasarvasvaṃ praṇavaṃ parameśvaram \|\| 45 \|\|*

*dhyātvā hṛtkarṇikāmadhye sāmbaṃ saṃsāramocakam \|*  
*prahṛṣṭamānaso bhūtvā vyājahāra mahāmuni \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. Requested by the sages, the master of the Vedas, the great sage, father of Śuka, chief of the Vedic scholars, meditated within the pericarp of his heart, on lord Śiva who releases from worldly existence and who is Praṇava, the essential substance of Vedanta. Being delighted in his mind, the great sage spoke.

## Chapter 2 - The dialogue between the god and the goddess

## \|\| 10.2.1-3 \|\|

*vyāsa uvāca \|*  
*sādhu pṛṣṭamidaṃ viprā bhavadbhirbhāgyavattamaiḥ \|*  
*durlabhaṃ hi śivajñānaṃ praṇavārthaprakāśakam \|\| 1 \|\|*

*yeṣāṃ prasanno bhagavānsākṣācchūlavarāyudhaḥ \|*  
*teṣāmeva śivajñānaṃ praṇavārthaprakāśakama \|\| 2 \|\|*

*jāyate na hi sandeho netareṣāmiti śrutiḥ \|\| \|*  
*śivabhaktivihīnānāmiti tattvārthaniścayaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

Vyāsa said:—

1-3. O blessed brahmins, the question has been well put by you. The knowledge of Śiva’s principle that illuminates the meaning of Praṇava’s incomprehensible. It can be obtained surely by those with whom the trident-bearing lord is pleased and not by others who are undoubtedly devoid of Śiva’s devotion. This is true.

## \|\| 10.2.4 \|\|

*dīrghasatreṇa yuṣmābhirbhagavānambikāpatiḥ \|*  
*upāsita itīdaṃ me dṛṣṭamadya viniścitam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Lord Śiva, consort of Ambā, has been adored by you by long sacrifices. This I see actually.

## \|\| 10.2.5 \|\|

*tasmādvakṣyāmi yuṣmākamitihāsampurātanam \|*  
*umāmaheśasamvādarūpamadbhutamāstikāḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. O devotees, I shall narrate to you an ancient story containing a wonderful dialogue between Umā and Śiva.

## \|\| 10.2.6-8 \|\|

*purākhilajaganmātā satī dākṣāyaṇī tanum \|*  
*śivanindāprasaṅgena tyaktvā ca janakādhvare \|\| 6 \|\|*

*tataḥ prabhāvātsā devī sutā'bhūddhimavadgireḥ \|*  
*śivārthamatapatsā vai nāradasyopadeśataḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

*tasminbhūdharavaryye tu svayaṃvaravidhānataḥ \|*  
*deveśe ca kṛtodvāhe pārvatī sukhamāpa sā \|\| 8 \|\|*

6-8. Formerly Satī, daughter of Dakṣa, the mother of the universe abandoned her body in the sacrifice of her father in view of the insult offered to Śiva. As a result of the penance the goddess became the daughter of the Himavat. On the advice of Nārada she performed a penance for Śiva on that mountain. When lord Śiva married her with Svayaṃvara rites Pārvatī attained happiness.

## \|\| 10.2.9 \|\|

*tathaikasminmahādevī samaye patinā saha \|*  
*sūpaviṣṭā mahāśaile gaurī devamabhāṣata \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Once she was cosily seated with lord Śiva on that great mountain. She spoke to the lord.

## \|\| 10.2.10-11 \|\|

*mahādevyuvāca \|*  
*bhagavanparameśāna pañcakṛtyavidhāyaka \|*  
*sarvajña bhaktisulabha paramāmṛtavigraha \|\| 10 \|\|*

*dākṣāyaṇīntanuṃ tyaktvā tava nindāprasaṃgataḥ \|*  
*āsamadya maheśāna putrī himavato gireḥ\|\| \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. O omniscient lord who hath ordained to himself the five-fold duties, O lord easily accessible through devotion, O lord of nectarine body, I was the daughter of Dakṣa in my former birth. I abandoned my body because of the insult offered to you. I have now become the daughter of the mountain Himavat.

## \|\| 10.2.12 \|\|

*kṛpayā parameśāna maṃtradīkṣāvidhānataḥ \|*  
*māṃ viśuddhātmatattvasthāṃ kuru nityaṃ maheśvara \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. O lord, please make me merged in the principle of pure Ātman by duly initiating me in the Mantras.

## \|\| 10.2.13 \|\|

*iti samprārthito devyā devaḥ śītāṃśu bhūṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*pratyuvāca tato devīṃ prahṛṣṭenāntarātmanā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Thus requested by the goddess the moon-crested lord replied to the goddess with a delighted mind.

## \|\| 10.2.14 \|\|

*mahādeva uvāca \|*  
*dhanyā tvaṃ devadevaśi yadi jātedṛśī matiḥ \|*  
*kailāsa śikharaṃ gatvā kariṣye tvāṃ ca tādṛśīm \|\| 14 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

14\. If your mind is thus diverted, O goddess, you are blessed indeed. I shall initiate you so after going to the summit of Kailāsa.

## \|\| 10.2.15 \|\|

*tato himavato gatvā kailāsambhūdhareśvaram \|*  
*jagau dīkṣāvidhānena praṇavādīnmanūn kramāt \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Then after leaving Himavat and reaching Kailāsa, the lord of mountains, Śiva, recited mantras Praṇava and others in the proper order after duly initiating her.

## \|\| 10.2.16 \|\|

*uktvā maṃtrāṃśca tāndevīṃ kṛtvā śuddhātmani sthitām \|*  
*sārddhaṃ devyā mahādevo devodyānaṃ gato'bhavat \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Then making the goddess merged in the pure Ātman, lord Śiva went to his garden accompanied by the goddess.

## \|\| 10.2.17-18 \|\|

*tataḥ sumālinīmukhyairdaivyāḥ priyasakhījanaiḥ \|*  
*samāhṛtaiḥ praphullaistaiḥ puṣpaiḥ kalpatarūdbhavaiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

*alaṃkṛtya mahādevīṃ svāṃkamāropya śaṃkaraḥ \|*  
*prahṛṣṭavadanastasthau vilokya ca tadānanam \|\| 18 \|\|*

17-18. Śiva bedecked the goddess with the fullblown kalpa tree flowers brought by the dearest friends of the goddess the chief of whom was Sumālinī. He made her sit on his lap. Looking at her face he sat there with his face beaming with delight.

## \|\| 10.2.19 \|\|

*tataḥ priyakathā jātāḥ pārvatīparameśayoḥ \|*  
*hitāya sarvalokānāṃ sākṣācchrutyarthaṃ sammitā \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Then ensued the delightful discourse conformable to the Vedas between Pārvatī and lord Śiva. It was meant for the welfare of the world.

## \|\| 10.2.20 \|\|

*tadā sarvajaganmātā bharturaṃkaṃ samāśritā \|*  
*vilokya vadanaṃ bharturidamāhaḥ tapodhanāḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. O ascetics, then the mother of the universe seated on the lap of her husband looked at the lord’s face and said.

## \|\| 10.2.21 \|\|

*\|\| śrīdevyuvāca \|*  
*upadiṣṭāstvayā deva maṃtrāssapraṇavā matāḥ \|*  
*tatrādau śrotumicchāmi praṇavārthaṃ viniścitam \|\| 21 \|\|*

The goddess said:—

21\. O lord, you have discoursed on the Mantras including the Praṇava. There at the outset I wish to know the meaning of the Praṇava.

## \|\| 10.2.22 \|\|

*kathampraṇava utpannaḥ kathaṃ praṇava ucyate \|*  
*mātrāḥ kati samākhyātāḥ kathaṃ vedādirucyate \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. How did Praṇava originate? How is Praṇava mentioned? How many Mātrās have been mentioned? How is it that it is called the initial mantra of the Vedas?

## \|\| 10.2.23 \|\|

*devatāḥ kati ca proktāḥ kathaṃ vedādibhāvanā \|*  
*kriyāḥ katividhāḥ proktā vyāpyavyāpakatā katham \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. How many deities are mentioned? What is the concept of the Vedas thereto? What types of rites are mentioned? What is their cause and what is their result?

## \|\| 10.2.24 \|\|

*brahmāṇi paṃca maṃtre'sminkathaṃ tiṣṭhaṃtyanukramāt \|*  
*kalāḥ kati samākhyātāḥ prapaṃcātmakatā katham \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. How do the five Brahmans stay in this mantra in their proper order? How many Kalās are enumerated? How is it the Ātman of the universe?

## \|\| 10.2.25 \|\|

*vācyavācakasambandhasthānāni ca kathaṃ śiva \|*  
*ko'trādhikārī vijñeyo viṣayaḥ ka udāhṛtaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. O Śiva, what is the link between the syllables and their meaning? What are the places of their articulation? Who is authorised in this? What is the theme?

## \|\| 10.2.26 \|\|

*sambandhaḥ kotra vijñeyaḥ kiṃprayojanamucyate \|*  
*upāsakastu kiṃrūpaḥ kiṃ vā sthānamupāsanam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. What is the link between the knower and the known and what is the purpose? What shall be the nature of the practising devotee? What shall be the place for the practice of meditation?

## \|\| 10.2.27 \|\|

*upāsyaṃ vastu kiṃrūpaṃ kiṃ vā phalamupāsituḥ \|*  
*anuṣṭhāna vidhiḥ kovā pūjāsthānaṃ ca kiṃ prabho \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Of what form is the object of devotion? What is the benefit derived from this? What is the procedure? What is the place of worship?

## \|\| 10.2.28 \|\|

*pūjāyāṃ maṇḍalaṃ kiṃ vā kiṃ vā ṛṣyādikaṃ hara \|*  
*nyāsajātavidhiḥ ko vā ko vā pūjāvidhikramaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. What is the mystic diagram of the worship? O Śiva, what are the sages and the rules regarding Nyāsa, Japa etc. What is the order of procedure in this worship?

## \|\| 10.2.29 \|\|

*etatsarvaṃ maheśāna samācakṣva viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*śrotumicchāmi tattvena yadyasti mayi te kṛpā \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. O lord Śiva, please mention these all particularly, if you have mercy on me. I wish to hear everything factually.

## \|\| 10.2.30 \|\|

*iti devyā samāpṛṣṭo bhagavānindubhūṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*sampraśasya maheśānīṃ vaktuṃ samupacakrame \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Thus implored, the moon-crested lord praised the goddess and began to explain.

## Chapter 3 - The way of Sannyāsa

## \|\| 10.3.1 \|\|

*\|\| īśvara uvāca \|*  
*śṛṇu devi pravakṣyāmi yanmāṃ tvampari pṛcchasi \|*  
*tasya śravaṇamātreṇa jīvassākṣācchivo bhavet \|\| 1 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

1\. O goddess, listen. I shall explain what you have asked for. Merely by hearing this the individual soul becomes Śiva himself.

## \|\| 10.3.2 \|\|

*praṇavārthaparijñānameva jñānaṃ madātmakam \|*  
*bījantatsarvavidyānāṃ maṃtra mpraṇavanāmakam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. To understand the meaning of Praṇava is to understand me. Praṇava is the seed of all the lores.

## \|\| 10.3.3 \|\|

*atisūkṣmaṃ mahārthaṃ ca jñeyaṃ tadvaṭabījavat \|*  
*vedādi vedasāraṃ ca madrūpaṃ ca viśeṣataḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. It shall be understood as very subtle but possessed of great meaning even as the seed of the Banyan tree though very small contains a huge tree. It is the initial mantra and the essence of the Vedas. Particularly it has me for its form.

## \|\| 10.3.4 \|\|

*devo guṇatrayātītaḥ sarvajñaḥ sarvakṛtprabhuḥ \|*  
*omityekākṣare maṃtre sthitohaṃ sarvagaśśivaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. I am the lord far beyond the attributes, the omniscient and the omnipotent. I am Śiva pervading all but stationed in the single-syllabled mantra Om.

## \|\| 10.3.5 \|\|

*yadasti vastu tatsarvaṃ guṇaprādhānyayogataḥ \|*  
*samastaṃ vyasta mapi ca praṇavārthaṃ pracakṣate \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. They say that whatever object there is whether???esised into one or analysed in pieces is the meaning of Praṇava, thanks to the combination and importance of the attributes.

## \|\| 10.3.6 \|\|

*sarvārthasādhakaṃ tasmādekaṃ brahmaitadakṣaram \|*  
*tenomiti jagatkṛsnaṃ kurute prathamaṃ śivaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. It is the imperishable Brahman, the means of attaining all objects. Śiva creates universe at the outset saying “Om”.

## \|\| 10.3.7 \|\|

*śivo vā praṇavo hyeṣa praṇavo vā śivaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*vācyavācakayorbhedo nātyaṃtaṃ vidyate yataḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. Since there is not much difference between the sense and the sound Om, this is explained thus. Śiva is Praṇava or Praṇava is Śiva.

## \|\| 10.3.8 \|\|

*tasmādekākṣaraṃ devaṃ māṃ ca brahmarṣayo viduḥ \|*  
*vācyavācakayoraikyaṃ manyamānā vipaścitaḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The brahminical sages, the scholars who realize the identity between the sense and the sound know me as the single-syllabled Om.

## \|\| 10.3.9 \|\|

*atastadeva jānīyātpraṇavaṃ sarvakāraṇam \|*  
*nirvikārī mumukṣurmāṃ nirguṇaṃ parameśvaram \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Hence he who aspires for salvation and is free from aberration shall understand Praṇava as the cause of all and me as the Nirguṇa Parameśvara.

## \|\| 10.3.10 \|\|

*enameva hi deveśi sarvamaṃtraśiromaṇim \|*  
*kāśyāmahaṃ pradāsyāmi jīvānāṃ muktihetave \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. O goddess, I shall give this crest-jewel of mantras at Kāśī for the liberation of all Individual souls.

## \|\| 10.3.11 \|\|

*tatrādau sampravakṣyāmi praṇavoddhārama mbike \|*  
*yasya vijñānamātreṇa siddhiśca paramā bhavet \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. O Ambā, there at the outset I shall explain Praṇavoddhāra, the knowledge of which imparls the greatest achievement.

## \|\| 10.3.12 \|\|

*nivṛttimuddharetpūrvamindhanaṃ ca tataḥ param \|*  
*kālaṃ samuddharetpaścāddaṃḍamī śvarameva ca \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Firstly the devotee shall extricate Nivṛtti, then the the fuel, time, s iff, and the lord.

## \|\| 10.3.13 \|\|

*varṇapaṃcakarūpoyamevaṃ praṇava uddhṛtaḥ \|*  
*trimātrabindunādātmā muktido japatāṃ sadā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Thus the Praṇava of five syllables is explained by extricating the three Mātrās, Bindu and Nāda. It yields liberation to those who perform the Japa in this manner.

## \|\| 10.3.14 \|\|

*brahmādisthāvarāntānāṃ sarveṣāṃ prāṇināṃ khalu \|*  
*prāṇaḥ praṇava evāyaṃ tasmātpraṇava īritaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. Praṇava is the vital breath of all living beings from Brahmā to immobile beings. Being the Prāṇa thus, it is called Praṇava.

## \|\| 10.3.15 \|\|

*ādyamvarṇamakāraṃ ca ukāramuttare tataḥ \|*  
*makāraṃ madhyataścaiva nādāṃtaṃ tasya comiti \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. It consists of A, U and M in the middle, Bindu and Nāda at the end. That is Om.

## \|\| 10.3.16 \|\|

*jalavadvarṇamādyantu dakṣiṇe cottare tathā \|*  
*madhye makāraṃ śucivadoṃkāre munisattama \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. O excellent sage, the first letter (A) is like water in the south, the second letter (U) is in the north; the letter M is like fire in the middle, before Nāda and Bindu.

## \|\| 10.3.17 \|\|

*akāraścāpyukāroyaṃ makārāśca trayaṃ kramāt \|*  
*tisro mātrāssamākhyātā arddhamātrā tataḥ param \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The three Mātrās are thus mentioned in order: A. U. M. Half a mātrā is beyond it.

## \|\| 10.3.18 \|\|

*arddhamātrā maheśāni bindunādasvarūpiṇī \|*  
*varṇanīyā na vai cāddhā jñeyā jñānibhireva sā \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O goddess, this half mātrā is in the form of Bindu and Nāda. This cannot be described directly. It is known only by the wise.

## \|\| 10.3.19 \|\|

*īśānassarvavidyānāmityadyāśśrutayaḥ priye \|*  
*matta eva bhavantīti vedāssatyamvadanti hi \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. O beloved, the Vedic texts beginning with “Īśānaḥ Sarvavidyānām” issue from me. The Vedas indeed speak the truth.

## \|\| 10.3.20 \|\|

*tasmādvedādirevāhaṃ praṇavo mama vācakaḥ \|*  
*vācakatvānmamaiṣo'pi vedādiriti kathyate \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. I am the source of the Vedas. Praṇava expresses me. Since it expresses me the Praṇava too is mentioned as Vedādi.

## \|\| 10.3.21 \|\|

*akārastu mahadbījaṃ rajassraṣṭā caturmukhaḥ \|*  
*ukāraḥ prakṛtiryonissattvaṃ pālayitā hariḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. ‘A’ is the great Bīja, Rajas, the four-faced creator. ‘U’ is the Prakṛti, source, Sattva, the protector Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 10.3.22 \|\|

*makāraḥ puruṣo bījī tamassaṃhārako haraḥ \|*  
*bindurmaheśvaro devastiro bhāva udāhṛtaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. ‘M’ is the Puruṣa, the Bījin, Tamas, the annihilator Śiva. Bindu is Maheśvara the lord, the disappearance.

## \|\| 10.3.23 \|\|

*nādassadāśivaḥ proktassarvānugrahakārakaḥ \|*  
*nādamūrddhani saṃcintya parātparataraḥ śivaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Nāda is Sadāśiva the bestower of blessings on all. On the top of Nāda there is Śiva who is greater than the greatest.

## \|\| 10.3.24 \|\|

*sa sarvajñaḥ sarvakarttā sarveśo nirmalo'vyayaḥ \|*  
*anirdeśyaḥ parabrahma sākṣātsadasataḥ paraḥ \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. He is omniscient, the creator, the lord, free from dirt, the imperishable, the inexpressible, the great Brahman, beyond the existent and the nonexistent.

## \|\| 10.3.25 \|\|

*akārādiṣu varṇeṣu vyāpakaṃ cottarottaram \|*  
*vyāpyantvadhastanaṃ varṇamevaṃ sarvatra bhāvayet \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. In the letters ‘A’ etc. the later one is pervasive of the earlier. The earlier one is the pervaded.

## \|\| 10.3.26 \|\|

*sadyādīśānaparyyaṃtānyakārādiṣu paṃcasu \|*  
*sthitāni paṃca brahmāṇi tāni manmūrttayaḥ kramāt \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. The five Brahmans beginning with Sadya and ending with Īśāna are stationed in the five letters ‘A’ etc. They are my very forms in order.

## \|\| 10.3.27 \|\|

*aṣṭau kalāssamākhyātā akāre sadyajāśśive \|*  
*ukāre vāmarūpiṇyastrayodaśa samīritāḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O Śivā, in the syllable ‘A’ eight Kalās of the form of Sadya exist. “In ‘U’ thirteen Kalās of the form of Vāma exist.

## \|\| 10.3.28 \|\|

*aṣṭāvaghorarūpiṇyo makāre saṃsthitāḥ kalāḥ \|*  
*bindau catasrassaṃbhūtāḥ kalāḥ puruṣagocarāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The eight Kalās of the form of Aghora are stationed in ‘M’. In Bindu four Kalās of the form of Puruṣa exist.

## \|\| 10.3.29 \|\|

*nāde paṃca samākhyātāḥ kalā īśānasaṃbhavāḥ \|*  
*ṣaḍvidhaikyānusaṃdhānātprapaṃcātmakatocyate \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. In Nāda five Kalās born of Īśāna arc present. Thus their state of being the soul of the universe is due to the recognition of their sixfold identity.

## \|\| 10.3.30 \|\|

*mantro yantraṃ devatā ca prapaṃco gurureva ca \|*  
*śiṣyaśca ṣaṭpadārthā nāmeṣāmarthaṃ śṛṇu priye \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The sixfold objects constitute Mantra, Yantra, deity, universe, the preceptor and the disciple. O beloved, listen to the moaning of these objects.

## \|\| 10.3.31 \|\|

*paṃcavarṇasamaṣṭiḥ syānmantraḥ pūrvamudāhataḥ \|*  
*sa eva yaṃtratāṃ prāpto vakṣye tanmaṇḍalakramam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The mantra previously mentioned is a composite of five letters. The same attains the form of a Yantra. I shall mention the details of its mystic diagram.

## \|\| 10.3.32 \|\|

*yantraṃ tu devatārūpaṃ devatā viśvarūpiṇī \|*  
*viśvarūpo guruḥ proktaśśiṣyo guruvapustvataḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Yantra is in the form of the deity; the deity is in the form of the universe; the preceptor too is in the form of the universe and the disciple is of the body of the preceptor.

## \|\| 10.3.33 \|\|

*omitīdaṃ sarvamiti sarvaṃ brahmeti ca śruteḥ \|*  
*vācyavācakasambandhopyayamevārtha īritaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Om is everything, everything is Brahman. (Om Itīdam Sarvam Iti Sarvam Brahma) This Vedic Text establishes the link between the word and its meaning.

## \|\| 10.3.34-35 \|\|

*ādhāro maṇipūraśca hṛdayaṃ tu tataḥ param \|*  
*viśuddhirājñā ca tataḥ śaktiḥ śāntiriti kramāt \|\| 34 \|\|*

*sthānānyetāni deveśi śāntyatītaṃ parātparam \|*  
*adhikārī bhavedyasya vairāgyaṃ jāyate dṛḍham \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. The places of articulation, O goddess are: Ādhāra (support) Maṇipūra (jewel-refill), Hṛdaya (heart), Viśuddhi (purity), Ājñā (order), Śakti (power), Śānti. Beyond Śānti is the lord who is greater than the greatest. The authorised person is he whose non-attachment is firm and stable.

## \|\| 10.3.36-37 \|\|

*viṣayaḥ syāmahaṃ devi jīvabrahmaikyabhāvanāt \|*  
*sambandhaṃ śṛṇu deveśi viṣayaḥ samyagīritaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

*jīvātmanormayā sārddhamaikyasya praṇavasya ca \|*  
*vācyavācakabhāvotra samvandhassamudīritaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

36-37. O goddess, I shall be the theme, thanks to the concept of the identity of the individual and the universal soul. The subject has been clearly mentioned. O goddess, now listen. The link of the Jīva and the Ātman with me and the Praṇava is as between the word and its sense.

## \|\| 10.3.38-42 \|\|

*vratādinirataḥ śāntastapasvī vijitendriyaḥ \|*  
*śaucācārasamāyukto bhūdevo vedaniṣṭhitaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

*viṣayeṣu viraktaḥ sannaihikāmuṣmikeṣu ca \|*  
*devānāṃ brāhmaṇo'pīha lokajeṣu śivavratī \|\| 39 \|\|*

*sarvaśāstrārtha tattvajñaṃ vedāntajñānapāragam \|*  
*ācāryyamupasaṃgamya yatiṃ matimatāṃ varam \|\| 40 \|\|*

*dīrghadaṇḍapraṇāmādyaistoṣayedyatnatassudhīḥ \|*  
*śāntyādiguṇasaṃyuktaḥ śiṣyassauśīlyavānvaraḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

*yo guruḥ sa śivaḥ prokto yaśśivassa guruḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*iti niścitya manasā svavicāraṃ nivedayet \|\| 42 \|\|*

38-42. A brahmin with the following characteristics shall approach the preceptor with the requisite qualifications. He shall be regularly engaged in holy rites, be calm, perform penance, shall have self-control, be a brahmin well-versed in the Vedas and endowed with cleanliness and good conduct, unattached to the pleasures of this world and of the next and of the worlds of the gods. He shall perform the holy rites of Śiva. The preceptor shall be one who has understood the tenets and the meanings of sacred texts; who has mastered Vedānta; who is an ascetic and the most excellent of intelligent men. After approaching him the intelligent brahmin shall prostrate before him and propitiate him by reverential deeds. That disciple is the most excellent who possesses good behaviour and the qualities of peaceful calmness. The disciple shall realise that preceptor is Śiva and Śiva is the preceptor. Then he shall reveal his thought.

## \|\| 10.3.43-52 \|\|

*labdhānujñastu guruṇā dvādaśāhaṃ payovatī \|*  
*samudratīre nadyāṃ ca parvate vā śivālaye \|\| 43 \|\|*

*śuklapakṣe tu paṃcamyāmekādaśyāṃ tathāpi vā \|*  
*prātaḥ snātvā tu śuddhātmā kṛtanitya kriyassudhīḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

*gurumāhūya vidhinā nāndīśrāddhaṃ vidhāya ca \|*  
*kṣauraṃ ca kārayitvātha kakṣopasthavivarjitam \|\| 45 \|\|*

*keśaśmaśrunakhānāṃ vai snātvā niyatamānasaḥ \|*  
*saktuṃ prāśyātha sāyāhne snātvā sandhyāmupāsya ca \|\| 46 \|\|*

*sāyamaupāsanaṃ kṛtvā guruṇā sahito dvijaḥ \|*  
*śāstroktadakṣiṇāndattvā śivāya gururūpiṇe \|\| 47 \|\|*

*homadravyāṇi saṃpādya svasūtroktavidhānataḥ \|*  
*agnimādhāya vidhivallaukikādivibhedataḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

*āhitāgnistu yaḥ kuryātprājāpatye ṣṭināhite \|*  
*śraute vaiśvānare samyak sarvavedasadakṣiṇam \|\| 49 \|\|*

*athāgnimātmanyāropya brāhmaṇaḥ pravrajedgṛhāt \|*  
*śrapayitvā caruṃ tasminsamidannājyabhedataḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

*pauruṣeṇaiva sūktena hutvā pratyṛcamātmavān \|*  
*hutvā ca sauviṣṭakṛtīṃ svasūtroktavidhānataḥ \|\| 51 \|\|*

*hutvopariṣṭāttantraṃ ca tenāgneruttare budhaḥ \|*  
*sthitvāsane japenmaunī cailājinakuśottare \|*  
*yāvadbrāhmamuhūrttaṃ tu gāyatrīṃ dṛḍhamānasaḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

43-52. After being permitted by the preceptor the brahmin devotee shall perform holy rites without taking anything except milk, for twelve days on the sea-shore, or on the banks of a river, or on a mountain or in a temple of Śiva. On the fifth or the eleventh day of the bright half he shall take his bath early in the morning. The intelligent devotee pure in mind shall perform his daily duty. He shall invite the preceptor duly and perform Nāndīśrāddha. He shall get himself shaved of the hair on the head and the moustache and the beards. The hair in the armpit and the private parts must not be shaved. The nails shall be cut. He shall perform his ceremonial ablution with restrained mind. He shall cat only powdered fried grain. In the evening he shall take his bath and perform Sandhyā. In the presence of the preceptor he shall perform the Aupāsana rites in the evening. To Śiva in the form of the preceptor he shall give Dakṣiṇā according to the scriptures. He shall collect articles for Homa. place the holy fires, laukika and others, with the rules\[?\] of his Gṛhya Sūtra. A brahmin who consecrates the sacred fire shall perform this Aupāsana in the sacrificial fire consecrated by the Prājāpatya sacrifice, reciting mantras from the Vedas and offering due Dakṣiṇās. Then the brahmin shall superimpose the fire\[?\] in the Ātman and formally renounce the world. The food-offering shall be cooked with the fire-wood and cooked rice and ghee. He shall perform the sacrifice repeating the Puruṣa Sūkta. The ghee shall be offered at the end of every stanza. The Sauviṣṭakṛti shall be performed in accordance with the rules of his own Sūtra. After the sacrifice he shall perform Tāntric activities. The learned devotee shall sit on a doth, deer-skin or Kuśa grass to the north of the fire. He shall repeat the Gāyatrī mantra silently till the Brāhma Muhūrta, and stabilise his mind firmly.

## \|\| 10.3.53 \|\|

*tataḥ snātvā yathā pūrvaṃ śrapayitvā caruṃ tataḥ \|*  
*pauruṣaṃ sūktamārabhya virajāntaṃ hunedbudhaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Then he shall take bath and cook the Caru. He shall perform the sacrifice beginning with Puruṣa and ending with the Virāja sūktas.

## \|\| 10.3.54 \|\|

*vāmadevamatenāpi śaunakādimatena vā \|*  
*tatra mukhyaṃ vāmadevyaṃ garbhayukto yato muniḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. The procedure may follow the opinion of Vāmadeva or of Sanaka and others. But the important one is that of Vāmadeva because he was a liberated soul even while he was in the womb.

## \|\| 10.3.55 \|\|

*homaśeṣaṃ samāpyātha hunet \|*  
*tatognimātmanyāropya prātassandhyamupāsya ca \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. After performing the remaining rites of the sacrifice he shall perform the Aupāsana rites of the morning. Then he shall superimpose Agni in the Ātman and perform the morning prayers.

## \|\| 10.3.56 \|\|

*savitaryudite paścātsāvitrīṃ prāviśetkramāt \|*  
*eṣaṇānāṃ trayaṃ tyaktvā preṣamuccārya ca kramāt \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. When the sun has arisen he shall recite Sāvitrī. He shall eschew the three Eṣaṇās and repeat the Preṣa mantras.

## \|\| 10.3.57 \|\|

*śikhopavīte saṃtyajya kaṭisūtrādikaṃ tataḥ \|*  
*visṛjya prāṅmukho gaccheduttarāśāmukhopi vā \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. He shall abandon the tuft, the sacred thread and the girdle too. He shall start to the east or to the north.

## \|\| 10.3.58 \|\|

*gṛhṇīyāddaṇḍakaupīnādyucitaṃ lokavartane \|*  
*viraktaścena gṛhṇīyāllokavṛttivicāraṇe \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. He shall lake with him a staff and a loin-cloth and such other things as are necessary for life. If he is totally detached he need not take these things too.

## \|\| 10.3.59 \|\|

*guroḥ samīpaṃ gatvātha daṇḍavatpraṇamettrayam \|*  
*samutthāya tatastiṣṭhedgurupādasamīpataḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. He shall approach the preceptor and prostrate before him thrice. Standing up he shall sit at the feet of the preceptor.

## \|\| 10.3.60-61 \|\|

*tato guruḥ samādāya virajānalajaṃ śitam \|*  
*bhasma tenaiva taṃ śiṣyaṃ samuddhṛtya yathāvidhi \|\| 60 \|\|*

*agnirityādibhirmantraistripuṇḍraṃ dhārayettataḥ \|*  
*hṛtpaṃkaje samāsīnaṃ māṃ tvayā saha cintayet \|\| 61 \|\|*

60-61. The teacher shall take the white ashes from the Virāja fire and smear it over the body of the disciple. Repeating the mantra “Agniriti” etc. he shall make the three marks Tripuṇḍra on his forehead. He shall think of me stationed in the lotus of the heart along with you.

## \|\| 10.3.62-63 \|\|

*hastaṃ nidhāya śirasi śiṣyasya prītamānasaḥ \|*  
*ṛṣyādisahitaṃ tasya dakṣakarṇe samuccaret \|\| 62 \|\|*

*praṇavaṃ triḥprakāraṃ tu tatastasyārthamādiśet \|*  
*ṣaḍvidhārthaṃ parijñānasahitaṃ gurusattamaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

62-63. Then the teacher shall place his hand on the head of the disciple. With a delighted mind, the preceptor shall utter the Praṇava mantra thrice in the right ear of the disciple mentioning the sage etc. Then he shall tell him the sixfold meaning.

## \|\| 10.3.64 \|\|

*dviṣaṭprakāraṃ sa guruṃ praṇamya bhuvi daṇḍavat \|*  
*tadadhīno bhavennityaṃ vedāntaṃ samyagabhyaset \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. The disciple shall bow prostrate before the precr-tor on the ground twelve times. He shall remain subservient to him and practise the study of the Vedānta strenuously.

## \|\| 10.3.65 \|\|

*māmeva ciṃtayennityaṃ paramātmānamātmani \|*  
*viśuddhe nirvikāre vai brahmasākṣiṇamavyayam \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. He shall ponder and meditate in the pure Ātman without aberrations, upon me the great Ātman, the Sākṣin of Brahman, the unchanging.

## \|\| 10.3.66 \|\|

*śamādidharmanirato vedāntajñānapāragaḥ \|*  
*atrādhikārī sa prokto yatirvigatamatsaraḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Here the person authorised is the ascetic engaged in the pious activities of self-restraint, the master of Vedāntic knowledge and free from rivalry.

## \|\| 10.3.67 \|\|

*hṛtpuṇḍarīkaṃ virajaṃ viśokaṃ viśadamparam \|*  
*aṣṭapatraṃ keśarāḍhyaṃ karṇikopari śo bhitam \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. The lotus of the heart is free from dust, devoid of sorrow, clear and great. It has eight petals with filaments and it shines above the pericarp.

## \|\| 10.3.68 \|\|

*ādhāraśaktimārabhya tritatvāṃtamayaṃ padam \|*  
*vicintya madhyatastasya daharaṃ vyoma bhāvayet \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. After thinking about the region from the Ādhāra-Śakti to the three Tattvas the Dahara Vyoman (the ether-heart) shall be imagined and conceived in the middle.

## \|\| 10.3.69 \|\|

*omityekākṣaraṃ brahma vyāharanmāṃ tvayā saha \|*  
*ciṃtayenmadhyatastasya nityamudyuktamānasaḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. Repeating the single-syllabled Brahman Om, he shall with alert mind think of me along with you in the middle.

## \|\| 10.3.70 \|\|

*evaṃvidhopāsakasya mallokagatimeva ca \|*  
*matto vijñānamāsādya matsāyujyaphalaṃ priye \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. O beloved, he who performs the rite of this type attains my world. After securing the knowledge of my principles he will attain Sāyujya salvation as the fruit.

## Chapter 4 - The daily conduct of a Sannyāsin

## \|\| 10.4.1 \|\|

*īśvara uvāca \|*  
*ataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi saṃnyāsāhnikakarma ca \|*  
*tava snehānmahādevi saṃpradāyānurodhataḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

1\. O goddess, after this I shall mention the daily routine during the Sannyāsa according to the convention. It is out of my affection for you that I am narrating the same.

## \|\| 10.4.2 \|\|

*brāhme muhūrtta utthāya śirasi śvetapaṃkaje \|*  
*sahasrāre samāsīnaṃ guruṃ saṃcitayedyatiḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. The ascetic shall get up in the Brāhma Muhūrta and think about the preceptor Śiva seated in the thousand-petalled lotus on the head.

## \|\| 10.4.3 \|\|

*śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśaṃ dvinetraṃ varadābhaye \|*  
*dadhānaṃ śivasadbhāvamevātmani manoharam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. The preceptor resembling pure crystal, with two eyes and holding the mystic gestures of boon and protection in the hand is conceived as Śiva of beautiful form in the soul.

## \|\| 10.4.4 \|\|

*bhāvopanītaiḥ saṃpūjya gandhādibhiranukramāt \|*  
*baddhāṃjalipuṭo bhūtvā namaskuryādguruṃ tataḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. He shall bow to the preceptor with palms joined in reverence and worship him with scents, fragrant flowers etc. created by his imagination.

## \|\| 10.4.5 \|\|

*prātaḥprabhṛti sāyānte sāyādiprātaraṃ tataḥ \|*  
*yatkaromi mahādeva tadastu tava pūjanam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. He shall pray thus—“O lord, let whatever I do from morning till sunset and from sunset till daybreak be your worship.”

## \|\| 10.4.6 \|\|

*prativijñāpya gurave labdhānujñastato guroḥ \|*  
*niruddhaprāṇa āsīno vijitātmā jitendriyaḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. After intimating to the preceptor thus and securing the permission he shall retain the breath and sit down, with his mind and the sense-organs under full control.

## \|\| 10.4.7-8 \|\|

*mūlādibrahmaraṃdhrāṃtaṃ ṣaṭcakraṃ pariciṃtayet \|*  
*vidyutkoṭisamaprakhyaṃ sarvatejomayaṃ param \|\| 7 \|\|*

*tanmadhye ciṃtayenmāṃ ca saccidānandavigraham \|*  
*nirguṇaṃ paramaṃ brahma sadāśivamanāmayam \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. He shall then meditate on the six-fold wheel from the root to the Brahma Randhra. In the middle he shall think of me, the Nirguṇa Sadāśiva, free from ailment, the great Brahman, with the lustre of crores of lightning, identical with splendour, with the body of existence, knowledge and bliss.

## \|\| 10.4.9 \|\|

*sohamasmīti matimānma daikyamanubhūya ca \|*  
*bahirnirgatya ca tato dūraṃ gacchedyathāsukham \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. He shall realize identity with me in the form “I am he”. The intelligent ascetic then shall go out far according to convenience.

## \|\| 10.4.10 \|\|

*vastreṇācchādya matimāñchiro nāsikayā saha \|*  
*viśodhya dehaṃ vi dhivattṛṇamādhāya bhūtale \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. The intelligent devotee shall cover the head along with the nose with a piece of cloth. He shall spread some grass on the ground and evacuate his bowels there duly.

## \|\| 10.4.11 \|\|

*gṛhītaśiśna utthāya tato gacchejjalāśayam \|*  
*uddhṛtya vāryathānyāyaṃ śaucaṃ kuryādatandritaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. He shall stand up holding the penis and go to the water-pond. He shall take out water and use it for cleansing carefully.

## \|\| 10.4.12-13 \|\|

*hastau pādau ca saṃśodhya dvirācamyomiti smaran \|*  
*uttarābhimukho maunī dantadhāvanamācaret \|\| 12 \|\|*

*tṛṇaparṇaiḥ sadā kuryādamāmekādaśī vinā \|*  
*apāṃ dvādaśagaṇḍūṣairmukhaṃ saṃśodhayettataḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

12-13. He shall wash hands, feet and perform Ācamana twice remembering Om. He shall face the north and clean his teeth always with leaves or grass except on the eleventh and the New-moon day. He shall rinse the mouth by gargling twelve times.

## \|\| 10.4.14 \|\|

*dvirācamya mṛdā toyaiḥ kaṭiśaucaṃ vidhāya ca \|*  
*aruṇodayakāle tu snānaṃ kuryānmṛdā saha \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. After performing Ācamana twice he shall clean the hips with water and clay. At the time of dawn he shall take his bath using clay.

## \|\| 10.4.15 \|\|

*guruṃ saṃsmṛtya māṃ caiva snānasaṃdhyādyamācaret \|*  
*vistārabhayato noktamatra draṣṭavyamanyataḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The bath and the Sandhyā prayers shall be performed with thoughts on the preceptor and me. The procedure of bath is not detailed here for fear of prolixity. That shall be seen elsewhere.

## \|\| 10.4.16 \|\|

*ābadhya śaṃkhamudrāṃ ca praṇavenābhiṣecayet \|*  
*śirasi dvādaśāvṛttyā tadardhaṃ vā tadardhakam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Joining the palms so as to from the Śaṅkhamudrā water shall be poured over the head repeating the Praṇava, twelve times, six times or three times.

## \|\| 10.4.17 \|\|

*tīramāgatya kaupīnaṃ prakṣālyācamya ca dvidhā \|*  
*prokṣayetpraṇavenaiva vastramaṃgopamārjanam \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. He shall go to the bank and wash the loin-cloth. He shall perform the Ācamana twice. Repeating the Praṇava he shall sprinkle water over the towel and wipe off the body.

## \|\| 10.4.18 \|\|

*mukhamprathamato mṛjya śira ārabhya sarvataḥ \|*  
*tenaiva mārjayeddehaṃ sthitvā ca gurusannidhau \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. He shall wipe off his face first and then the head and other parts of the body standing by the side of the preceptor.

## \|\| 10.4.19 \|\|

*ābadhyādvāmataḥ śuddhaṃ kaupīnaṃ ca saḍorakam \|*  
*tataḥ saṃdhārayedbhasma tadvidhiḥ procyate'drije \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. A pure loin-cloth shall be tied with its string with the knot on the left side. Then he shall smear his body with the ashes. O daughter of the mountain, now I tell you the procedure thereof.

## \|\| 10.4.20 \|\|

*dvirācamya samādāya bhasma sadyādimaṃtrataḥ \|*  
*agnirityādibhirmaṃtrairabhimaṃtrya spṛśettanum \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. After performing the Ācamana twice he shall take the ashes repeating the Sadyādi mantra\[2\]. Then repeating the mantras “Agniriti” he shall touch the body.

## \|\| 10.4.21-22 \|\|

*āpovetyabhimaṃtryātha jalaṃ tenaiva secayet \|*  
*omāpojyotirityuktvā mānastoketi maṃtrataḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*samadya kamaladvandvaṃ kuryā kekaṃ tu paṃcadhā \|*  
*śirovadanahṛdguhyapādeṣu parameśvari \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. Repeating the mantra “Āpo vai\[4\]”— he shall mix the ashes with water. He shall make two balls of the pasted ashes with the mantras “Om Āpo Jyotī”\[5\] and “Mā Nastok\[?\]”—He shall divide one of the balls into five, O goddess, and apply each respectively to the head, face, chest, private parts and the feet.

## \|\| 10.4.23-25 \|\|

*īśānādisamārabhya sadyāntaṃ paṃcabhiḥ kramāt \|*  
*uddhūlya kavalaṃ paścātpraṇavenābhiṣecayet \|\| 23 \|\|*

*sarvāṃgaṃ ca tato hastau prakṣālyānyatsamāharet \|*  
*samarcya pūrvattattu tripuṇḍrāṃstena dhārayet \|\| 24 \|\|*

*triyāyuṣaistryambakaiśca praṇavena śivena ca \|*  
*śirasyatha lalāṭe ca vakṣasi skandha eva ca \|\| 25 \|\|*

23-25. He shall repeat the mantra beginning with “Īśāna\[7\]”— and ending with “Sadya.” After applying the ashes he shall repeat Om touching all the parts of the body. He shall wash his hands, feet and take the other ball. Adoring as before he shall apply three parallel lines on the forehead repeating the mantra ‘Tryāyuṣa’\[8\] and the ‘Tryambaka’. He shall apply the same on the chest with the Praṇava and on the shoulders with “Oṃ Namaḥ Śivāya.”

## \|\| 10.4.26 \|\|

*nābhau bāhvauḥ saṃdhiṣu ca pṛṣṭha caiva yathākramam \|*  
*prakṣālya hastau ca tato dvirācamya yathāvidhi \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Uttering Pañcīkaraṇa mantra, the scholarly ascetic shall think of his preceptor. In the manner as explained hereafter he shall perform six Prāṇāyāmas.

## \|\| 10.4.27-28 \|\|

*paṃcīkaraṇamuccārya bhāvayetsvaguruṃ budhaḥ \|*  
*vakṣyamāṇaprakāreṇa prāṇāyāmānṣaḍācaret \|\| 27 \|\|*

*dakṣahastena saṃgṛhya jalaṃ vāmena pāṇinā \|*  
*samācchādya dviṣaḍvāraṃ praṇave nābhimaṃtrayet \|\| 28 \|\|*

27-28. Touching the navel, the joints of the arms and the back in order he shall wash both the hands duly and perform Ācamana twice. He shall take some water in the right hand and cover it with the left hand. Praṇava is then repeated twelve times.

## \|\| 10.4.29-31 \|\|

*evaṃ trivāraṃ saṃprokṣya śirasi triḥ pibettataḥ \|*  
*samāhitena manasā dhyāyannoṃkāramīśvaram \|\| 29 \|\|*

*sauramaṇḍalamadhyasthaṃ sarvatejomayaṃ param \|*  
*aṣṭabāhuṃ caturvaktramarddhanārīkamadbhutam \|\| 30 \|\|*

*sarvāścaryyaguṇopetaṃ sarvālaṃkāraśobhitam \|*  
*evaṃ dhyātvātha vidhivaddadyādarghyatrayaṃ tataḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

29-31. Sprinkling this water thrice on the head he shall drink water thrice meditating on Oṅkāra—Śiva with pure mind—Śiva who is stationed in the middle of the solar disc, the god consisting of all splendour, possessing eight arms, four faces, the wonderful form half-female, endowed with wonderful qualities and bedecked in ornaments. After meditating thus duly he shall offer three Arghyas.

## \|\| 10.4.32 \|\|

*aṣṭottaraśataṃ japtvā dviṣaḍvāraṃ tu tarpayet \|*  
*punarācamya vidhivatprāṇāyāmatrayaṃ caret \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. After performing the Japa of Gāyatrī hundred and eight times, he shall offer Tarpaṇa twelve times. After performing the Ācamana he shall perform Prāṇāyāma thrice.

## \|\| 10.4.33 \|\|

*pūjāsadanamāgacchenmanasā saṃsmarañcchivam \|*  
*dvāramāsādya prakṣālya pādau maunī dvirācamet \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Then he shall go to the hall of worship thinking on Śiva. After reaching the door he shall wash his feet silently and perform Ācamana twice.

## \|\| 10.4.34 \|\|

*praviśedvidhinā tatra dakṣapādapurassa ram \|*  
*maṇḍapāntassudhīstatra maṇḍalaṃ racayetkramāt \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. He shall enter it duly placing the right foot at first. Inside the Maṇḍapa there, he, the intelligent ascetic, shall draw the mystic diagram in due order.

## Chapter 5 - The rules governing the mystic diagram of the ascetic

## \|\| 10.5.1-2 \|\|

*īśvara uvāca \|*  
*parīkṣya vidhivadbhūmiṃ gaṃdhavarṇarasādibhiḥ \|*  
*manobhilaṣite tatra vitānavitatāmbare \|\| 1 \|\|*

*supralipte mahīpṛṣṭhe darpaṇodarasannibhe \|*  
*aratniyugmamānena caturasraṃ prakalpayet \|\| 2 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

1-2. The ground shall be tested duly on the basis of smell, colour and taste. In a spot pleasing to the mind the space above shall be covered with a canopy, the ground below shall be scrubbed and cleaned so much as to appear like the surface of a mirror. A square of sides two Aratnis each shall be drawn.

## \|\| 10.5.3 \|\|

*tālapatraṃ samādāya tatsamāyāmavistaram \|*  
*tasminbhāgānprakurvīta trayodaśasamāṃ kalām \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Make small squares within the width of a palm-leaf each. Thus there shall be thirteen equal squares in each column.

## \|\| 10.5.4-6 \|\|

*tatpatraṃ tatra nikṣipya paścimābhimukhaḥ sthitaḥ \|*  
*tatpūrvabhāge sudṛḍhaṃ sūtramādāya raṃjitam \|\| 4 \|\|*

*prākpratyagdakṣiṇodak ca caturdiśi nipātayet \|*  
*sūtrāṇi devadeveśi navaṣaṣṭyuttaraṃ śatam \|\| 5 \|\|*

*koṣṭhāni syustatastasya madhyakoṣṭhaṃ tu karṇikā \|*  
*koṣṭhāṣṭakaṃ bahistasya dalāṣṭakamihocyate \|\| 6 \|\|*

4-6. The ascetic shall put the piece of palm leaf inside the square and sit facing the west. Towards the east a strong coloured thread shall be fixed. Threads shall be put in all the four directions. Thus there will be one hundred and sixty-nine squares. The middle square is the pericarp. The squares adjoining it around in the outside, eight in number, are called eight petals.

## \|\| 10.5.7-8 \|\|

*dalāni śvetavarṇāni dalāṣṭakamihocyate \|*  
*dalāni śvetavarṇātri samagrāṇi prakalpayet \|*  
*pītarūpāṃ karṇikāṃ ca kṛtvā raktaṃ ca vṛttakam \|\| 7 \|\|*

*vanabhiddaladakṣaṃ tu samārabhya sureśvari \|*  
*raktakṛṣṇāḥ krameṇaiva dalasandhīnvicitrayet \|\| 8 \|\|*

7-8. All the petals shall be made white completely. The pericarp shall be made yellow in colour and a red circle shall be drawn within. O goddess, beginning with the petal of Indra to the right hand side the bordering lines shall be coloured alternatively black and red.

## \|\| 10.5.9-10 \|\|

*karṇikāyāṃ likhedyaṃtraṃ praṇavārthaprakāśakam \|*  
*adhaḥ pīṭhaṃ samālikhya śrīkaṇṭhaṃ ca tadūrdhvataḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*taduparyyamareśaṃ ca mahākālaṃ ca madhyataḥ \|*  
*tanmastakasthaṃ daṇḍaṃ ca tata īśvaramālikhet \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. The Yantra illuminating the meaning of the Praṇava shall be written inside the pericarp. The pedestal shall be drawn beneath. Śrīkaṇṭha shall be drawn above it. Amareśa shall be drawn above it and Mahākāla in the middle. On the top of it the staff and beyond that Īśvara shall be drawn.

## \|\| 10.5.11 \|\|

*śyāmena pīṭhaṃ pītena śrīkaṇṭhaṃ ca vicitrayet \|*  
*amareśaṃ mahākālaṃ raktaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ca tau kramāt \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The Pīṭha shall be coloured blue. Śrīkaṇṭha in yellow, Amareśa in red, and Mahākāla in black.

## \|\| 10.5.12 \|\|

*kuryātsudhūmraṃ daṇḍaṃ ca dhavalaṃ ceśvaraṃ budhaḥ \|*  
*evaṃ yaṃtraṃ samālikhya raktaṃ sadyena veṣṭayet \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. The scholarly ascetic shall make the staff smoky coloured and Īśvara white in colour. After drawing the Yantra the red one (Amareśa) shall be encompassed by Sadya.

## \|\| 10.5.13 \|\|

*tadutthenaiva nādena vidyādīśānamīśvari \|*  
*tadvāsapaṃktīrgṛhṇīyādāgneyādikrameṇa vai \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. O goddess, only through the Nāda Īśāna shall be known. The rows of its residence shall be taken in order from the south-east one.

## \|\| 10.5.14-15 \|\|

*koṣṭhāni koṇabhāgeṣu catvāryetāni sundari \|*  
*śuklenāpūryya varṇādi catuṣkaṃ raktadhātubhiḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*āpūryya tāni catvāri dvārāṇi parikalpayet \|*  
*tatastatpārśvayordvaṃdvaṃ pītenaiva prapūrayet \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. The four squares at the four corners, O beautiful lady, shall be painted white. The first four letters “a”, “ā”, “i” and “ī” shall be written with red minerals and these four are conceived as doors. The two squares adjacent to each of these four shall be painted yellow.

## \|\| 10.5.16 \|\|

*āgneyakoṣṭhamadhye tu pītābhe caturasrake \|*  
*aṣṭapatraṃ likhetpadmaṃ raktābhaṃ pītakarṇikam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. In the yellow square in between the south-east squares a lotus of eight petals shall be drawn red in colour with yellow pericarp.

## \|\| 10.5.17-19 \|\|

*hakāraṃ vilikhenmadhye vinduyuktaṃ samāhitaḥ \|*  
*padmasya nairṛte koṣṭhe caturasrantadā likhet \|\| 17 \|\|*

*padmamaṣṭadalaṃ raktaṃ pītakiṃjalkakarṇikam \|\| \|*  
*śavargasya tṛtīyantu ṣaṣṭhasvarasamanvitam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*caturdaśasvaropetaṃ bindunādavibhūṣitam \|*  
*etadbījavaraṃ bhadre padmamadhye samālikhet \|\| 19 \|\|*

17-19. With purity of mind he shall draw the letter “ha” in the middle along with the Bindu. In the south-west square of this lotus he shall draw another red lotus with yellow filaments and pericarp. The third letter of the class of “śa” along with the sixth vowel and the fourteenth vowel decorated with Bindu and Nāda is, O gentle lady, the excellent Bīja which shall be written in the middle of the lotus.

## \|\| 10.5.20-22 \|\|

*padmasyeśānakoṣṭhe tu tathā padmaṃ samālikhet \|*  
*kavargasya tṛtīyaṃ tu paṃcamasvarasaṃyutam \|\| 20 \|\|*

*vilikhenmadhyatastasya bindukaṇṭhe svalaṃkṛtam \|*  
*tadbāhyapaṃktitritaye pūrvādiparitaḥ kramāt \|\| 21 \|\|*

*koṣṭhāni paṃca gṛhrīyādgirirājasute śive \|*  
*madhye tu karṇikāṃ kuryātpītāṃ raktaṃ ca vṛttakam \|\| 22 \|\|*

20-22. In the north-cast square of the lotus another lotus shall be drawn with the third letter of the class of “ka” along with the fifth vowel. In its middle Bindu and Kaṇṭha shall be decoratively written. In the three columns outside it, beginning with the eastern one five squares shall be taken, O Śivā, daughter of the lord of mountains. The pericarp shall be taken in the middle painted yellow with a red circle.

\[? note for one of verses 7-22 ?\]: The mystical syllables as mentioned in the verses indicate the following incantations bhū (bhūryāya namaḥ), guṃ (gurave namaḥ).

## \|\| 10.5.23 \|\|

*dalāni raktavarṇāni kalpayetkalpavittamaḥ \|*  
*dalabāhye tu kṛṣṇena raṃdhrāṇi paripūrayet \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. The most excellent of the knowers of the rules shall make the leaves red in colour. Outside the leaves the cavities shall be filled with black colour.

## \|\| 10.5.24 \|\|

*āgneyādīni catvāri śuklenaiva prapūrayet \|*  
*pūrve ṣaḍbindusahitaṃ ṣaṭkoṇaṃ kṛṣṇamālikhet \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. The south-east square and others, four in number, shall be filled with white paint. With six Bindus a six-sided figure shall be drawn in black in the east.

## \|\| 10.5.25 \|\|

*raktavarṇaṃ dakṣiṇatastrikoṇaṃ cottare tataḥ \|*  
*śvetābhamarddhacandraṃ ca pītavarṇaṃ ca paścime \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. To the south it shall be painted red. In the north a triangle is painted in white. In the west a crescent moon is painted in yellow.

## \|\| 10.5.26-27 \|\|

*caturasraṃ kramātteṣu likhedbījacatuṣṭayam \|*  
*pūrve binduṃ samālikhya śubhraṃ kṛṣṇaṃ tu dakṣiṇe \|\| 26 \|\|*

*ukāramuttare raktaṃ makāraṃ paścime tataḥ \|*  
*akāraṃ pītamevaṃ tu kṛtvā varṇacatuṣṭayam \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. The four Bījas shall be written in the squares in order. The Bindu shall be drawn in the cast in white. In the south is painted in black the letter “u”; in the north, letter “ma” is painted in red; in the west letter “a” is painted yellow; thus the four letters are written.

## \|\| 10.5.28-29 \|\|

*sarvorddhvapaṃktyadhaḥ paṃktau samārabhya ca sundari \|*  
*pītaṃ śvetaṃ ca raktaṃ ca kṛṣṇaṃ ceti catuṣṭayam \|\| 28 \|\|*

*tadadho dhavalaṃ śyāmaṃ pītaṃ raktaṃ catuṣṭayam \|*  
*adhastrikoṇake raktaṃ śuklaṃ pītaṃ varānane \|\| 29 \|\|*

28-29. In the second row from the top O beautiful lady, one shall begin. The first four squares are respectively painted yellow, white, red and black. Below that the four are painted white, blue, yellow and red respectively. O good-faced lady, below that in the triangle it shall be painted red white and yellow.

## \|\| 10.5.30 \|\|

*evandakṣiṇamārabhya kuryātsomāntamīśvari \|*  
*tadbāhyapaṃktau pūrvādimadhyamāntaṃ vicitrayet \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. O goddess, from the south to the north the same procedure shall be adopted. In its external row it shall be painted from the east to the middle.

## \|\| 10.5.31-36 \|\|

*pītaṃ raktaṃ ca kṛṣṇaṃ ca śyāmaṃ śvetaṃ ca pītakam \|*  
*āgreyyādi samārabhya raktaṃ śyāmaṃ sitaṃ priye \|\| 31 \|\|*

*raktaṃ kṛṣṇaṃ ca raktaṃ ca ṣaṭkameva prakīrtitam \|*  
*dakṣiṇādyaṃ maheśāni pūrvāvadhi samīritam \|\| 32 \|\|*

*nairṛtādyantu vijñeyamāgneyāvadhi ceśvari \|*  
*vāruṇaṃ tu samārabhya dakṣiṇāvadhi ceritama \|\| 33 \|\|*

*vāyavyādyaṃ mahādevi nairṛtāvadhi ceritam \|*  
*somārthaṃ parameśāni vāruṇāvadhi ceritam \|\| 34 \|\|*

*īśānādyaṃ tu vijñeyaṃ vāyavyāvadhi cāmbike \|*  
*ityukto maṇḍalavidhirmayā tubhyaṃ ca pārvati \|\| 35 \|\|*

*evaṃ maṇḍalamālikhya niyatātmā yatissvataḥ \|*  
*saurapūjāṃ prakurvīta sa hi tadvastutatparaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

31-36. The colours are yellow, red, black, blue, white and yellow. O beloved, from the south-east onwards colours are red, blue, white, red, black and red. Thus the colours of the six squares are mentioned: from the south to the cast; from the south-west to the south-east; from the west to the south; from the north-west to the south-west; from the north to the west; from the north-east, to the north-west. O goddess, thus the procedure of the diagram has been mentioned to you. Having drawn the diagram thus the ascetic with restrained mind shall worship the sun with devotion.

## Chapter 6 - Rules of Nyāsa in the path of Renunciation

## \|\| 10.6.1-4 \|\|

*\|\|īśvara uvāca \|*  
*dakṣiṇe maṃḍalasyātha vaiyāghraṃ carmaśobhanam \|*  
*āstīryya śuddhatoyena prokṣayedastramaṃtrataḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

*praṇavaṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya paścādādhāra muddharet \|*  
*tatpaścācchaktikamalaṃ caturthyaṃtaṃ namontakam \|\| 2 \|\|*

*manumevaṃ samuccārya sthitvā tasminnudaṅmukhaḥ \|*  
*prāṇānāyamya vidhivatpra ṇavoccārapūrvakam \|\| 3 \|\|*

*agnirityādibhirmaṃtrairbhasma saṃdhārayettataḥ \|*  
*śirasi śrīguruṃ natvā maṇḍalaṃ racayetpunaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

1-4. The ascetic shall spread a beautiful tiger’s hide to the south of the diagram and sprinkle pure water over it repeating the Astra mantra. After uttering the Praṇava at the outset he shall utter the Ādhāra and the Śaktikamala. He shall sit on the tiger’s hide repeating the mantra with the dative case ending with Namaḥ.\[1\] He shall face the north. After performing the Prāṇāyāma duly preceded by the utterance of the Praṇava he shall smear the body with the ashes with the mantras “Agniriti” After bowing to lord Śiva he shall arrange the diagram again.

## \|\| 10.6.5-7 \|\|

*trikoṇavṛttaṃ bāhye tu caturasrātmakaṃ kramāt \|*  
*abhyarcyomiti sādhāraṃ sthāpya śaṃkhaṃ samarcayet \|\| 5 \|\|*

*āpūrya śuddhatoyena praṇavena sugaṃdhinā \|*  
*abhyarcya gaṃdhapuṣpādyaiḥ praṇavena ca saptadhā \|\| 6 \|\|*

*abhimaṃtrya tatastasmindhenumudrāṃ pradarśayeta \|*  
*śaṃkhamudrāṃ ca tenaiva prokṣayedastramaṃtrataḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

5-7. Outside he shall adore in order the diagrams of triangle circle and square repeating Om. He shall fill the conch with fragrant pure water repeating the Praṇava. He shall place the conch on the diagrams and worship it with scents, flowers etc. repeating the Praṇava seven times. After repeating the mantra he shall show the Dhenumudrā and Śaṅkhamudrā. He shall sprinkle it with the Astra mantra.

## \|\| 10.6.8 \|\|

*ātmānaṃ gaṃdhapuṣpādipūjopakaraṇāni ca \|*  
*prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kṛtvā ṛṣyādikamathācaret \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. He shall sprinkle himself and the scents, flowers and other materials of worship. After performing Prāṇāyāma thrice he shall utter the names of the sages etc.

## \|\| 10.6.9-10 \|\|

*asya śrīsauramaṃtrasya devabhāga ṛṣistataḥ \|*  
*chando gāyatramityuktaṃ devassūryo maheśvaraḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*devatā syātṣaḍaṃgāni hrāmityādīni vinyaset \|*  
*tatassaṃprokṣayetpadmamastreṇāgneragocaram \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. Of this mantra of the glorious sun the sage is Devabhāga, the metre is Gāyatrī, the deity is the sun. The six-organed Nyāsa shall be made with “hrām” etc. Then he sprinkles the Padma not within the purview of Agni.

## \|\| 10.6.11-14 \|\|

*tasminsamarcayedvidvān prabhūtāṃ vimalāmapi \|*  
*sārāṃ cātha samārādhya pūrvādiparataḥ kramāt \|\| 11 \|\|*

*atha kālāgnirudraṃ ca śaktimādhārasaṃjñitām \|*  
*anantaṃ pṛthivīṃ caiva ratnadvīpaṃ tathaiva ca \|\| 12 \|\|*

*saṃkalpavṛkṣodyānaṃ ca gṛhaṃ maṇimayaṃ tataḥ \|*  
*raktapīṭhaṃ ca saṃpūjya pādeṣu prāgupakramāt \|\| 13 \|\|*

*dharmaṃ jñānaṃ ca vairāgyamaiśvaryaṃ ca catuṣṭayam \|*  
*adharmādyagnikoṇādikoṇeṣu ca samarcayet \|\| 14 \|\|*

11-14. The learned ascetic shall worship in it Prabhūtā, Vimalā, Sārā from the former to the latter in order. Then he shall worship Kālāgnirudra, Ādhāra, Śakti, Ananta, Pṛthivī, Ratnadvīpa, imaginary trees and garden a house of jewels and the gemset scat at the feet; beginning with the east the gods of virtue, knowledge, detachment and supremacy shall be worshipped. He shall also worship the gods of evil etc. in the corner quarters such as south-east and others.

## \|\| 10.6.15-20 \|\|

*māyādhaśchadanaṃ paścādvidyordhvacchadanaṃ tataḥ \|*  
*sattvaṃ rajastamaścaiva samabhyarcya yathākramam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*pūrvādidikṣu madhye ca dīptāṃ sūkṣmāṃ jayāmapi \|*  
*bhadrāṃ vibhūti vimalāmamoghāṃ vaidyutāmapi \|\| 16 \|\|*

*sarvatomukhasaṃjñāṃ ca kandanālaṃ tathaiva ca \|*  
*suṣiraṃ ca tatastaṃ tu kaṃṭakāṃstadanaṃtaram \|\| 17 \|\|*

*mūlacchadanakiṃjalkaprakāśasakalātmanaḥ \|*  
*paṃcagraṃthikarṇikāṃ ca dalāni tadanaṃtaram \|\| 18 \|\|*

*keśarānbrahmaviṣṇū ca rudramātmānameva ca \|*  
*antarātmānamapi ca jñānātmaparamātmani \|\| 19 \|\|*

*sampūjya paścātsaurākhyaṃ yogapīṭhaṃ samarcayet \|*  
*pīṭhopari samākalpya mūrttiṃ mūlena mūlavit \|\| 20 \|\|*

15-20. He shall worship the lower lid of Māyā and and the upper lid of Vidyā. Thereafter Sattva, Rajas and Tamas shall be worshipped in order. In the quarters beginning with the east and in the middle he shall worship the following:—Dīptā, Sūkṣmā, Jayā, Bhadrā, Vibhūti, Vimalā, Amoghā, Vaidyutā, Sarvatomukhasaṃjñā, Kandanāla, Suṣira, then Tantukaṇṭakas, then the root lid, filament, (light) and Sakalātman, the five-knotted pericarp and the petals, then the filaments, Brahmā, Viṣṇu, Rudra and Ātman. After worshipping the inner Ātman in the great Ātman of knowledge he shall worship the Yogapīṭha called the Saura. The knower of the root shall conceive the idol as stationed on the pedestal.

## \|\| 10.6.21-24 \|\|

*niruddhaprāṇa āsīno mūlenaiva svamūlataḥ \|*  
*śaktimutthāpya tattejaḥ prabhāvātpiṃgalādhvanā \|\| 21 \|\|*

*puṣpāṃjalau nirgamayya maṇḍalasthasya bhāsvataḥ \|*  
*sindūrāruṇadehasya vāmārddhadayitasya ca \|\| 22 \|\|*

*akṣasrakpāśakhaṭvāṃgakapālāṃkuśapaṃkajam \|*  
*śaṃkhaṃ cakraṃ dadhānasya caturvaktrasya locanaiḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

*rājitasya dvādaśabhistasya hṛtpaṃkajodare \|*  
*praṇavaṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya hrāṃhrīṃsastadanantaram \|\| 24 \|\|*

21-24. The ascetic sits checking the vital breath with the root from the root. With the power of its splendour he raises the Śakti through the path of the Piṅgalā nerve of the sun stationed in the circle. He causes it to come out with the handful of flowers. Of Śiva whose body is as red as the vermillion, who has his wife as his left half, who is wearing Rudrākṣa garland and holding a noose, a club called Khaṭvāṅga, a skull, a goad, a lotus a conch and a discus; who has four faces, twelve eyes and a spear within the lotus of his heart, he utters the Praṇava first and then Hrām, Hrīm and Saḥ.

## \|\| 10.6.25-27 \|\|

*prakāśaśaktisahitaṃ mārtaṇḍaṃ ca tataḥ param \|*  
*āvāhayāmi nama ityāvāhyā vāhanākhyayā \|\| 25 \|\|*

*mudrayā sthāpanādyāśca mudrāssaṃdarśayettataḥ \|*  
*vinyasyāṃgāni hrāṃ hrīṃ hrūmaṃtena manunā tataḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

*paṃcopacārānsaṃkalpya mūlenābhyarcayettridhā \|*  
*keśareṣu ca padmasya ṣaḍaṃgāni maheśvari \|\| 27 \|\|*

25-27. Thereafter he invokes the sun along with the light and power saying “I invoke obeisance”. He shows the mystic signs of Sthāpanā etc. After performing the Nyāsa of the limbs with the mantra Hrām, Hrīm, Hrum and conceiving the five services O great goddess, he shall worship thrice the six organs in the filaments of the lotus.

## \|\| 10.6.28-30 \|\|

*vahnīśarakṣovāyūnāṃ paritaḥ kramataḥ sudhīḥ \|*  
*dvitīyāvaraṇe pūjyāścatasro mūrtayaḥ kramāt \|\| 28 \|\|*

*pūrvādyuttaraparyaṃtaṃ dalamūleṣu pārvati \|*  
*ādityo bhāskaro bhānū raviścetyanupūrvaśaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

*arko brahmā tathā rudro viṣṇuśceti punaḥ priye \|*  
*īśānādiṣu saṃpūjyāstṛtīyāvaraṇe punaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

28-30. In the second covering the four deities shall be worshipped in the south-east, north-east south-west and the north-west. O Pārvatī, from east to north at the roots of the petals Āditya, Bhāskara, Bhānu and Ravi shall be worshipped in order. O beloved, in the third covering, Arka, Brahmā, Rudra and Viṣṇu shall be worshipped in the north-east and other quarters.

## \|\| 10.6.31 \|\|

*somaṃ kujaṃ budhaṃ jīvaṃ kaviṃ maṃdaṃ tama stamaḥ \|*  
*samaṃtato yajedetānpūrvādidalamadhyataḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. In the middle of the leaves from the east onwards he shall worship the Moon, Mars, Mercury, Jupiter, Venus, Saturn and Rāhu.

## \|\| 10.6.32 \|\|

*athavā dvādaśādityāndvitīyāvaraṇe yajeta \|*  
*tṛtīyāvaraṇe caiva rāśīrdvādaśa pūjayet \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Or he shall worship the twelve Ādityas in the second covering and worship the twelve signs of zodiac in the third covering.

## \|\| 10.6.33-34 \|\|

*saptasāgaragaṃgāśca bahirasya samaṃtataḥ \|*  
*ṛṣīndevāṃśca gaṃdharvānpannagānapsarogaṇān \|\| 33 \|\|*

*grāmaṇyaśca tathā yakṣānyātudhānāṃstathā hayān \|*  
*saptacchandomayāṃścaiva vālakhilyāṃśca pūjayet \|\| 34 \|\|*

33-34. He shall worship the seven oceans\[3\] and seven Gaṅgās on its outer side and then the Sages, Gods, Gandharvas, Serpents, Celestial damsels, the village chiefs, the Yakṣas, Yātudhānas, the seven horses in the form of the Vedas and the Bālakhilyas.

## \|\| 10.6.35-37 \|\|

*evaṃ tryāvaraṇaṃ devaṃ samabhyarcya divākaram \|*  
*viracya maṃḍalaṃ paścāccaturasraṃ samāhitaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

*sthāpya sādhārakaṃ tāmrapātraṃ prasthodavistṛtam \|*  
*pūrayitvā jalaiḥ śuddhairvāsitaiḥ kusumādibhiḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

*abhyarcya gaṃdhapuṣpādyairjānubhyāmavanīṃ gataḥ \|*  
*arghyapātraṃ samādāya bhūmadhyāntaṃ samuddharet \|\| 37 \|\|*

35-37. After worshipping the sun of three coverings and then arranging the square diagram with pious attention he shall place a copper vessel that can hold a prastha of water on its stand. He shall fill it with pure water rendered fragrant with flowers. He shall worship it with scents, flowers and kneel on the ground. The arghya vessel shall be lifted upto the middle of the eyebrows.

## \|\| 10.6.38 \|\|

*tato brūyādimaṃ maṃtraṃ sāvitraṃ sarvasiddhidam \|*  
*śṛṇu tacca mahādevi bhaktimuktipradaṃ sadā \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. He shall recite the hymn to the sun-god yielding all accomplishments. O goddess, it yields worldly pleasures and salvation always.

## \|\| 10.6.39-40 \|\|

*sindūravarṇāya sumaṇḍalāya namo'stu vajrābharaṇāya tubhyam \|*  
*padmābhanetrāya supaṃkajāya brahmendranārāyaṇakāraṇāya \|\| 39 \|\|*

*saraktacūrṇaṃ sasuvarṇatoyaṃ srakkuṃkumāḍhyaṃ sakuśaṃ sapuṣpam \|*  
*pradattamādāya sahemapātraṃ praśastamarghyaṃ bhagavanprasīda \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. Obeisance to you of the colour of vermillion, of good disc wearing diamond for the ornament, having eyes resembling lotus, of good lotus and cause of Brahmā, Indra and Viṣṇu. O lord, be pleased to accept this holy arghya offered by me along with the red powder, coloured\[?\] water, garland, vermilion, kuśa grass, flowers and a golden pot.

## \|\| 10.6.41 \|\|

*evamuktvā tato dattvā tadarthaṃ sūryamūrttaye \|*  
*namaskuryādimaṃ maṃtraṃ paṭhitvā susamāhitaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. He shall recite this and offer the arghya to the sun-god and perform obeisance. He shall recite the following piously.

## \|\| 10.6.42 \|\|

*namaśśivāya sāmbāya sagaṇāyādihetave \|*  
*rudrāya viṣṇave tubhyaṃ brahmaṇe ca trimūrtaye \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. “Obeisance to Śiva accompanied by Pārvatī and the Gaṇas. Obeisance to you the primordial cause, of the form of Trinity, Rudra, Viṣṇu and Brahmā”.

## \|\| 10.6.43-44 \|\|

*evamuktvā namaskṛtya svāsane samavasthitaḥ \|*  
*ṛṣyādikaṃ punaḥ kṛtvā karaṃ saṃśodhya vāriṇā \|\| 43 \|\|*

*punaśca bhasma saṃdhārya pūrvoktenaiva vartmanā \|*  
*nyāsajātamprakurvīta śivabhāvavivṛddhdhaye \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. After saying this and bowing he shall seat himself. He shall mention the names of the sages etc. again and wash his hands with water. He shall apply the ashes again as mentioned before. He shall then perforin Nyāsas expressive of his devotion to Śiva.

## \|\| 10.6.43 \|\|

*evamuktvā namaskṛtya svāsane samavasthitaḥ \|*  
*ṛṣyādikaṃ punaḥ kṛtvā karaṃ saṃśodhya vāriṇā \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. After worshipping lord Śiva with bent head and fivefold services he shall bow to the Praṇava in the dative case ending with Namaḥ.

## \|\| 10.6.46-47 \|\|

*paṃcātmakaṃ binduyutaṃ paṃcamasvarasaṃyutam \|*  
*tadeva bindusahitaṃ paṃcamasvaravarjitam \|\| 46 \|\|*

*paṃcamasvarasaṃyuktaṃ maṃtrīśaṃ ca sabindukam \|*  
*uddhṛtya bindusahitaṃ saṃvartakamathoddharet \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. He shall meditate upon the Praṇava consisting of five letters including the Bindu and fifth vowel “u” without the fifth vowel and with the Bindu. After uttering this with Bindu he shall utter the composite unit.

## \|\| 10.6.48 \|\|

*etaireva kramādbījairuddhṛtaiḥ praṇamedbudhaḥ \|*  
*bhujayorūruyugme ca guruṃ gaṇapatintathā \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. With these Bījas duly uttered the scholarly ascetic shall bow to the preceptor and the lord of Gaṇas stationed on the arms and thighs.

## \|\| 10.6.49 \|\|

*durgāṃ ca kṣetrapālaṃ ca baddhāṃjalipuṭaḥ sthitaḥ \|*  
*omastrāya phaḍityuktvā karau saṃśodhya ṣaṭ kramāt \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. He shall bow to Durgā and Kṣetrapāla also, with palms joined in reverence. He shall repeat “Om Astrāya Phat” six times and wipe oíí his hands.

## \|\| 10.6.50-51 \|\|

*apasarppantviti procya praṇavaṃ tadanaṃtaram \|*  
*astrāya phaḍiti procya pārṣṇighātatrayeṇa tu \|\| 50 \|\|*

*uddhṛtya vighnānbhūyiṣṭhānkara tālatrayeṇa tu \|*  
*antarikṣagatā ndṛṣṭvā vilokya divi saṃsthitān \|\| 51 \|\|*

50-51. He shall recite the mantra “Apasa?pantu” and repeat “Astrāya Phaṭ”. Clapping the palms thrice and kicking thrice he shall remove the obstacles; he shall gaze at the heaven-dwellers, the skyfarers.

## \|\| 10.6.52 \|\|

*niruddhaprāṇa āsīno haṃsamaṃtramanusmaran \|*  
*hṛdisthaṃ jīvacaitanyaṃ brahmanāḍyā samāna yet \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. He shall restrain the vital breath and remember the Haṃsa mantra. Through the Brahma Nāḍī he shall then bring about the living consciousness in the heart.

## \|\| 10.6.53 \|\|

*dvādaśāṃtassthaviśade sahasrāramahāmbuje \|*  
*ciccandramaṇḍalāntasthaṃ cidrūpaṃ parameśvaram \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. He shall meditate on the supreme god of the form of cit in the middle of lunar sphere of cit within the great lotus of thousand petals, full-blown and stationed in the twelve (?)

## \|\| 10.6.54 \|\|

*śoṣadāhaplavānkuryādrecakādi krameṇa tu \|*  
*saṣoḍaśacatuṣṣaṣṭidvātriṃśadgaṇanāyutaiḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. For sixteen, sixty-four and thirty-two seconds he shall perform Pūraka, Kumbhaka and Recaka through Śoṣa Dāha and Plava.

## \|\| 10.6.55-58 \|\|

*vāyvagnisalilādyaistaisstavedādyairanukramāt \|*  
*prāṇānāyamya mūlasthāṃ kuṇḍalīṃ brahmaraṃdhragām \|\| 55 \|\|*

*ānīya dvādaśāṃtasthasahasrārāmbujodare \|*  
*ciccandramaṇḍalodbhūtaparamāmṛtadhārayā \|\| 56 \|\|*

*saṃsiktāyāṃ tanau bhūyaśśuddhadehassubhāvanaḥ \|*  
*sohamityavatīryātha svātmānaṃ hṛdayāmbuje \|\| 57 \|\|*

*ātmanyāveśya cātmānamamṛtaṃ sṛtidhārayā \|*  
*prāṇapratiṣṭhāṃ vidhivatkuryādatra samāhitaḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

55-58. After retaining the breath as mentioned in his particular Veda through wind, íìre, water etc. he shall bring the nerve which is at the Mūla to the cerebral aperture. When the body is bathed in the exquisite nectarine current issuing from the lunar sphere of Cit, within the lotus of thousand petals within the twelve, it is purified and thrilled with good emotions. He makes the Ātman descend to the lotus of the heart realising “I am he”. He thrusts the Ātman into the Ātman through the nectarine current. Thus he shall piously stabilise the vital breath duly.

## \|\| 10.6.59-60 \|\|

*ekāgramānaso yogī vimṛśyāttāṃ ca mātṛkām \|*  
*puṭitāṃ praṇavenātha nyasedbāhye ca mātṛkām \|\| 59 \|\|*

*punaśca saṃyataprāṇaḥ kuryāddṛṣṭyādikaṃ budhaḥ \|*  
*śaṃkaraṃ saṃsmaraṃścitte saṃnyasecca vimatsaraḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

59-60. The Yogin with mental concentration shall ponder over the Mātṛkā. He shall fix the developed Mātṛkā through the Praṇava externally. He shall carry out nourishment etc. with restricted vital breath. Thinking upon Śiva in the mind he shall dedicate himself quietly.

## \|\| 10.6.61 \|\|

*praṇavasya ṛṣirbrahmā devi gāyatramīritam \|*  
*chandotra devatāhaṃ vai paramātmā sadāśivaḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. O goddess, the sage of Praṇava is Brahmā, the metre is Gāyatrī and the deity is the great Ātman, Sadāśiva that is I myself.

## \|\| 10.6.62 \|\|

*akāro bījamākhyātamukāraḥ śaktirucyate \|*  
*makāraḥ kīlakaṃ proktaṃ mokṣārthe viniyujyate \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. The letter “A” is the seed; the letter “U” is Śaktī; the letter “M” is Kīlaka. It is used for the sake of liberation.

## \|\| 10.6.63 \|\|

*aṃguṣṭhadvayamārabhya talāṃtaṃ parimārjayet \|*  
*omityuktvātha deveśi karanyāsaṃ samārabhet \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Beginning with the two thumbs and ending with the palms, the hands are wiped off. O goddess, śaying “Om” he shall begin the Nyāsa of the hands.

## \|\| 10.6.64 \|\|

*dakṣahastasthitāṃguṣṭhaṃ samārabhya yathākramam \|*  
*vāmahastakaniṣṭhāṃtaṃ vinyasetpūrvavatkramāt \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. He shall perform the Nyāsa starting with the thumb of the right hand and ending with the little finger of the left hand.

## \|\| 10.6.65 \|\|

*akāramapyukāraṃ ca makāraṃ bindusaṃyutam \|*  
*namontaṃ procya sarvatra hṛdayādau nyasedatha \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. Everywhere “A”, “U”, “M” with the Bindu, and the mantra ending with Namaḥ shall be uttered and the Nyāsa of the hand in the heart etc. be performed.

## \|\| 10.6.66 \|\|

*akāraṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya brahmātmānamathācaret \|*  
*ṅeṃtaṃ namoṃtaṃ hṛdaye viniyujyāttathā punaḥ \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. After uttering “A” at the outset the identity of Brahman and Ātman shall be practised. The mantras ending with “De” and “Namaḥ” shall be utilised in the heart.

## \|\| 10.6.67 \|\|

*ukāraṃ viṣṇusahitaṃ śirodeśe pravinyaset \|*  
*makāraṃ rudrasahitaṃ śikhāyāntu pravinyaset \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. The Nyāsa of “U” along with Viṣṇu is made on the head; that of “M” along with Rudra in the tuft.

## \|\| 10.6.68 \|\|

*evamuktvā munirmaṃtrī kavacaṃ netramastake \|*  
*vinyaseddevadeveśi sāvadhānena cetasā \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. O Goddess, the sage practising the mantra shall make the Nyāsa of the Kavaca in the eyes and the forehead with an attentive mind.

## \|\| 10.6.69-74 \|\|

*aṃgavaktrakalābhedātpaṃca brahmāṇi vinyaset \|*  
*śirovadanahṛdaguhyapādeṣvetāni vinyaset \|\| 69 \|\|*

*īśānyasya kalāḥ paṃca paṃcasveteṣu ca kramāt \|*  
*tataścaturṣu vaktreṣu puruṣasya kalā api \|\| 70 \|\|*

*catasraḥ praṇidhātavyāḥ pūrvādikramayogataḥ \|*  
*hṛtkaṃṭhāṃseṣu nābhau ca kukṣau pṛṣṭhe ca vakṣasi \|\| 71 \|\|*

*aghorasya kalāścāṣṭau pūjanīyā yathākramam \|*  
*paścāttrayodaśakalāḥ pāyumeḍhrorujānuṣu \|\| 72 \|\|*

*jaṃghāsphikkaṭipārśveṣu vāmadevasya bhāvayet \|*  
*sadyasyāpi kalā cāṣṭau netreṣu ca yathākramam \|\| 73 \|\|*

*kīrtitāstāḥ kalāścaiva pādayorapi hastayoḥ \|*  
*prāṇe śirasi bāhvośca kalpayetkalpavittamaḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

69-74. The different organs, faces and digits shall be fixed in the five Brahmans. Īśāna’s five digits shall be fixed in the head, face, heart, private parts and the feet. The four digits of the Puruṣa shall be fixed in the four faces. Aghora’s eight digits shall be worshipped in the heart, neck, shoulders, navel, belly, back and the chest. Vāmadeva’s thirteen digits shall be fixed in the anus, penis, thighs, knees, calves, buttocks, waist and the sides. Sadya’s eight digits shall be fixed in eight eyes. The most excellent of the knowers of kalpa shall fix these digits in the feet, hand, vital breath, head and arms.

## \|\| 10.6.75 \|\|

*aṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsamevaṃ kṛtvā tu sarvaśaḥ \|*  
*paścātpraṇavaviddhīmānpraṇavanyāsamācaret \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. Thus making the Nyāsa of the thirty-eight Kalās the intelligent knower of Praṇava shall proceed with the Nyāsa of Praṇava.

## \|\| 10.6.76-77 \|\|

*bāhudvaye kūrparayostathā ca maṇibandhayoḥ \|*  
*pārśvatodarajaṃgheṣu pādayoḥ pṛṣṭhatastathā \|\| 76 \|\|*

*itthaṃ praṇavavinyāsaṃ kṛtvā nyāsavicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*haṃsanyāsaṃ prakurvīta paramātmavibodhini \|\| 77 \|\|*

76-77. After making the Nyāsa in the arms, elbows, wrists, sides, belly, calves, feet and the back, the devotee, an expert in the Nyāsa, shall perform Haṃsanyāsa, O lady enlightener of the great Ātman.

## Chapter 7 - The worship of Śiva

## \|\| 10.7.1-6 \|\|

*īśvara uvāca \|*  
*svavāme caturasraṃ tu maṇḍalaṃ parikalpayet \|*  
*omityabhyarcya tasmiṃstu śaṃkhamastropaśobhitam \|\| 1 \|\|*

*sthāpya sādhārakaṃ taṃ tu praṇavenārcayettataḥ \|*  
*āpūryya śuddhatoyena candanādisugaṃdhinā \|\| 2 \|\|*

*abhyarcya gandhapuṣpādyaiḥ praṇavena ca saptadhā \|*  
*abhimaṃtrya tatastasmindhenumudrāṃ pradarśayet \|\| 3 \|\|*

*śaṃkhamudrāṃ ca purataścaturasraṃ prakalpayet \|*  
*tadantarerddhacandraṃ ca trikoṇaṃ ca tadantare \|\| 4 \|\|*

*ṣaṭkoṇaṃ vṛttamevedaṃ maṇḍalaṃ parikalpayet \|*  
*abhyarcya gaṃdhapuṣpādyaiḥ praṇavenātha madhyataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*sādhāramarghyapātraṃ ca sthāpya gaṃdhādinārcayet \|*  
*āpūryya śuddhatoyena tasminpātre viniḥkṣipet \|\| 6 \|\|*

Lord Śiva said:—

1-6. The ascetic devotee shall make the square diagram to his left and worship it with Om. He shall place the conch and the missile, with its stand and worship it with the Praṇava after filling it with the water scented with the sandal paste. He shall worship it with the scents and flowers and repeat Praṇava seven times. He shall show the mystic sign of the cow and the conch making a square diagram in front and a semicircle in the middle, a triangle, hexagon and a circle within each in order.
