40\. The group of sense-organs consists of the eye, etc; that of the Bhūtas is of four types. In the Pitṛ Śrāddha the father, grandfather and greatgrandfather are included.

## \|\| 10.12.41-42 \|\|

*mātṛśrāddhe mātṛpitāmahyau ca prapitāmahī \|*  
*ātmaśrāddhe tu catvāra ātmā pitṛpitāmahau \|\| 41 \|\|*

*prapitāmahanāmā ca sapatnīkāḥ prakīrttitāḥ \|*  
*mātāmahātmakaśrāddhe trayo mātāmahādayaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

41-42. In the Mātṛśrāddha, the mother, grandmother and the greatgrandmother are mentioned. In the Śrāddha of the Ātman the four, viz. himself father, grandfather and greatgrandfather and their wives are mentioned. In the Śrāddha of mother’s father the three ancestors of the maternal grandfather are mentioned.

## \|\| 10.12.43 \|\|

*pratiśrāddhaṃ brāhmaṇānāṃ yugmaṃ kṛtvopakalpitān \|*  
*āhūya pādau prakṣālya svayamācamya yatnataḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. In every Śrāddha a pair of brahmins shall be invited and their feet washed. He shall then perform the Ācamana assiduously.

## \|\| 10.12.44-46 \|\|

*samastasaṃpatsamavāptihetavaḥ samutthitāpatkuladhūmaketavaḥ \|*  
*apārasaṃsārasamudrasetavaḥ punantu māṃ brāhmaṇapādareṇavaḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

*āpaddhanadhvāntasahasrabhānavaḥ samīhitā rthārpaṇakāmadhenavaḥ \|*  
*samastatīrthāṃbupavitramūrttayo rakṣaṃtu māṃ brāhmaṇapādapāṃsavaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

*iti japtvā namaskṛtya sāṣṭāṃgaṃ bhuvi daṇḍavat \|*  
*sthitvā tu prāṅmukhaḥ śambhoḥ pādābjayugalaṃ smaran \|\| 46 \|\|*

44-46. He shall pray thus:—“May the dust-particles from the feet of the brahmins sanctify me. They are the cause of prosperity. They destroy all adversities and constitute the bridge on the vast ocean of worldly existence. Like suns they remove the pitch darkness of distress. Like divine cows they meet the desired objects. They are the embodied holy waters in every pilgrim centre.” After praying thus he shall bow and prostrate with eight limbs touching the ground. He shall then face the cast and remember the lotus-feet of Śiva.

## \|\| 10.12.47-49 \|\|

*sapavitrakaraśśuddha upavītī dṛḍhāsanaḥ \|*  
*prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ kuryyā cchrutvātithyādikaṃ punaḥ \|\| 47 \|\|*

*matsaṃnyāsāṃgabhūtaṃ yadviśvedevādikaṃ tathā \|*  
*śrāddhamaṣṭavidhaṃ mātāmahagataṃ pārvaṇena vai \|\| 48 \|\|*

*vidhānena kariṣyāmi yuṣmadājñāpurassaram \|*  
*evaṃ vidhāya saṃkalpaṃ darbhānuttaratastyajet \|\| 49 \|\|*

47-49. Wearing the Pavitra in the finger and the sacred thread on the shoulders, being pure and steady he shall perform the Prāṇāyāma thrice. Mentioning the Tithi etc. he shall continue—“As an ancillary rite to my formal renunciation I have to perform eight Śrāddhas beginning with the Śrāddha of Viśvedevas and ending with the Śrāddha of Mātāmaha. I shall perform these with the rules governing the festive rites, with your permission.” After proclaiming the intention he shall cast off the Darbha grass to the north.

## \|\| 10.12.50 \|\|

*upaspṛśyāpa utthāya varaṇakramamārabhet \|*  
*pavitrapāṇiḥ saṃspṛśya pāṇī brāhmaṇayorvadet \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. He shall touch water and stand up to begin the formal invitation to the brahmins. With the Pavitra in the hand he shall touch the hands of the brahmins and proclaim:

## \|\| 10.12.51-53 \|\|

*viśvedevārtha ityādi bhavadbhyāṃ kṣaṇa ityapi \|\| 51 \|\|*

*prasādanīya ityantaṃ sarvva traivaṃ vidhikramaḥ \|*  
*evaṃ samāpya varaṇaṃ maṇḍalāni prakalpayet \|\| 52 \|\|*

*udagārabhya daśa ca kṛtvābhyarcanamakṣataiḥ \|*  
*teṣu krameṇa saṃsthāpya brāhmaṇānpādayoḥ punaḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

51-53. “For the sake of Viśvedevas I invite you both and conclude. You shall favour me.” This is the procedure throughout. After this he shall draw ten Maṇḍalas beginning from the north. He shall worship these with Akṣatas and ask the Brahmins to stand there.

## \|\| 10.12.54-55 \|\|

*viśvedevādināmāni sasaṃvabodhanamuccaret \|*  
*idaṃ vaḥ pādyamiti sakuśapuṣpākṣatodakaiḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

*pādyaṃ dattvā svayamapi kṣālitāṃghrirudaṅmukhaḥ \|*  
*ācamya yugmaklṛptāṃstānāsaneṣūpaveśya ca \|\| 55 \|\|*

54-55. He shall utter the names of Viśvedevas in the vocative case. “Here is water for washing the feet”. Saying this, he shall offer water from the Darbha grass, flowers and Akṣata as Pādya. He shall wash his own feet too faring the north. He shall perform Ācamana and ask them to sit in pairs on their seats.

## \|\| 10.12.56-57 \|\|

*viśvedevasvarūpasya brāhmaṇasyedamāsanam \|*  
*iti darbhāsanaṃ dattvā darbhapāṇissvayaṃ sthitaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

*asminnāndīmukhaśrāddhe viśvedevārtha ityapi \|*  
*bhavadbhyāṃ kṣaṇa ityuktvā kriyatāmiti saṃvadet \|\| 57 \|\|*

56-57. “Here is the seat to the brahmin in the form of Visvedevas.” Saying this he shall offer a few Darbha grass blades. He himself shall stand holding the Darbha grass and say, “In this Nāndīmukhaśrāddha for the sake of Visvedevas I invite both of you to be present.”

## \|\| 10.12.58 \|\|

*prāpnutāmiti samprocya bhavantāviti saṃvadet \|*  
*vadetāṃ prāpnuyāveti tau ca brāhmaṇapuṃgavau \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. Then he shall say “Both of you shall accept my invitation.” The two brahmins shall say “We accept.”

## \|\| 10.12.59 \|\|

*saṃpūrṇamastu saṃkalpasiddhirastviti tānprati \|*  
*bhavanto'nugṛhṇaṃtviti prārthayeddvijapuṃgavān \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. He shall request the brahmins—“May you bless me. Let the rite be complete. Let me attain my desires.”

## \|\| 10.12.60-63 \|\|

*tataśśuddhakadalyādipātreṣu kṣāliteṣu ca \|*  
*annādibhojyadravyāṇi dattvā darbhaiḥ pṛthakpṛthak \|\| 60 \|\|*

*paristīryya svayaṃ tatra pariṣicyodakena ca \|*  
*hastābhyāmavalaṃbyātha pātraṃ pratyekamādarāt \|\| 61 \|\|*

*pṛthivī te pātramityādi kṛtvā tatra vyavasthitān \|*  
*devādīṃśca caturthyantānanūdyākṣatasaṃyutān \|\| 62 \|\|*

*udaggṛhītvā svāheti devārthe'nnaṃ yajetpunaḥ \|*  
*na mameti vadedante sarvatrāyaṃ vidhikramaḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

60-63. He shall then place cooked rice and other foodstuffs in well washed plantain leaf cups. They shall be covered with Darbha grass. He shall sprinkle wain over them. The leafcups shall be lifted up by the hands. He shall offer the food-stuffs into fire saying “Svāhā.” The names of the deities shall be used in the dative case. The Mantras “the earth is your vessel,” etc. shall be repeated. After worship the devotee shall say “Na Mama” (not for me). Everywhere this is the procedure.

## \|\| 10.12.64 \|\|

*yatpādapadmasmaraṇādyasya nāmajapādapi \|*  
*nyūnaṃ karma bhavetpūrṇantaṃ vande sāmbamīśvaram \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. “I salute lord Śiva accompanied by Ambā, the recollection of whose feet and the repctition of whose names makes deficient rites perfect and complete.

## \|\| 10.12.65-67 \|\|

*iti japtvā tato brūyānmayā kṛta midaṃ punaḥ \|*  
*nāndīmukhaśrāddhamiti yathoktaṃ ca vadettataḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

*asviti brūteti ca tānprasādya dvijapuṃgavān \|*  
*visṛjya svakarasthodaṃ praṇamya bhuvi daṇḍavat \|\| 66 \|\|*

*utthāya ca tato brūyādamṛtambhavatu dvijān \|*  
*prārthayecca paraṃ prītyā kṛtāṃjalirudāradhīḥ \|\| 67 \|\|*

65-67. After repeating the prayer ho shall say. I have completed Nandīmukhaśrāddha. Confirm that I have done it. He shall propitiate the leading brahmins, let some water drop from his hand, bow to them, stand up and say to them. let the food be even as nectar. Then with palms joined in reverence he shall pray with pleasure.

## \|\| 10.12.68 \|\|

*śrīrudraṃ camakaṃ sūktaṃ pauruṣaṃ ca yathāvidhi \|*  
*citte sadāśivandhyātvā japedbrahmāṇi pañca ca \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. He shall recite Śrīrudra, Camaka, and Puruṣa sūktas duly. After meditating on Sadāśiva he shall repeat the Japas of the five Brahmans.

## \|\| 10.12.69 \|\|

*bhojanānte rudrasūktaṃ kṣamā payya dvijānmunaḥ \|*  
*tanmantreṇa tato dadyāduttarāpośaṇaṃ puraḥ \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. At the end of the meal he shall recite Rudra Sūkta. He shall ask forgiveness of the brahmins. After food he shall offer water.

## \|\| 10.12.70 \|\|

*prakṣālitāṃghrirācamya piṇḍasthānaṃ vrajettataḥ \|*  
*āsīnaḥ prāṅmukho maunī prāṇāyāmatrayaṃ caret \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. Washing his feet and performing the Ācamana he shall approach the Piṇḍas. Facing the cast he shall sit silently and perform Prāṇāyāma thrice.

## \|\| 10.12.71-72 \|\|

*nāndīmukhoktaśrāddhāṃgaṃ kariṣye piṇḍadānakam \|*  
*iti saṃkalpya dakṣādisamārabhyodakānti kam \|\| 71 \|\|*

*nava rekhāḥ samālikhya prāgagrāndvādaśa kramāt \|*  
*saṃstīryya darbhāndakṣādidevādisthānapañcakam \|\| 72 \|\|*

71-72. “I shall perform the rite of offering the Piṇḍas as an ancillary to the Nāndīmukhaśrāddha.” After deciding thus he shall draw nine lines from south to north. He shall place twelve Darbhas with their tips to the east over the lines. These are the five places for the gods Dakṣa and others.

## \|\| 10.12.73-75 \|\|

*tūṣṇīṃ dadyātsākṣatodaṃ triṣu sthāneṣu ca kramāt \|*  
*sthāneṣvanyeṣu mātṛṣu mārjjayantāstataḥ param \|\| 73 \|\|*

*atreti pitaraḥ paścātsākṣatodaṃ samarcya ca \|*  
*dadyāttataḥ krameṇaiva devādisthānapañcake \|\| 74 \|\|*

*tattaddevādināmāni caturthyantānyudīryya ca \|*  
*piṇḍatrayaṃ tato dadyātpratyekaṃ sthānapañcake \|\| 75 \|\|*

73-75. In these places the akṣata grains and water shall be offered silently. In the other places he shall sprinkle water with the mantra “Atra Pitaraḥ”, and offer the Akṣata grains and water for worship. The names of different gods shall be mentioned with the dative case ending. Three Piṇḍas shall be offered to each in the five places.

## \|\| 10.12.76 \|\|

*svagṛhyoktena mārgeṇa dadyātpiṇḍānpṛthakpṛthak \|*  
*dadyādidaṃ sākṣataṃ ca pitṛsāṅguṇyahetave \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. The Piṇḍas shall be given separately along with Akṣatas for the full efficacy of the Pitṛs as explained in the Gṛhyasūtra.

## \|\| 10.12.77-79 \|\|

*dhyāyetsadāśivaṃ devaṃ hṛdayāmbhojamadhyataḥ \|*  
*tatpādapadmasmaraṇāditi ślokaṃ paṭhanpunaḥ \|\| 77 \|\|*

*namaskṛtya brāhmaṇebhyo dakṣiṇāṃ ca svaśa ktitaḥ \|*  
*dattvā kṣamāpayya ca tānvisṛjya ca tataḥ kramāt \|\| 78 \|\|*

*piṇḍānutsṛjya gogrāsaṃ dadyānnocejjale kṣipet \|*  
*puṇyāhavācanaṃ tvāṃ bhuṃjīta svajanaissaha \|\| 79 \|\|*

77-79. He shall meditate on lord Sadāśiva in the lotus of the heart reciting the verse. By remembering his lotus-like feet he shall give fees to the Brahmins according to his ability. He shall ask forgiveness and bid them farewell. He shall give Piṇḍas to the cows or cast off into running water. After performing “Puṇyāhavācana” he shall take meals with his kinsmen.

## \|\| 10.12.80-81 \|\|

*anyedyuḥ prātarutthāya kṛtanityakriyassudhīḥ \|*  
*upoṣya kṣaurakarmādi kakṣopasthavivarjitam \|\| 80 \|\|*

*keśaśmaśrunakhāneva karmmāvadhi visṛjya ca \|*  
*samāṣṭakeśānvidhivatkārayitvā vidhānataḥ \|\| 81 \|\|*

80-81. After getting up early in the morning next day the devotee shall perform his routine. He shall observe fast, shave hair except in the armpits and the private parts. He shall cut the moustache, the nails and shave his head.

## \|\| 10.12.82 \|\|

*snātvā dhautapaṭaśśuddho dvirācamyātha vāgyataḥ \|*  
*bhasma saṃdhāryya vidhinā kṛtvā puṇyāhavācanam \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. He shall take bath and wear a washed and dried cloth. He shall be pure in thought and body. Silently he shall perform Ācamana twice and apply ashes duly and perform the Puṇyāhavācana.

## \|\| 10.12.83 \|\|

*tena saṃprokṣya saṃprāpya śuddhadehasvabhāvataḥ \|*  
*homadravyārthamācāryya dakṣiṇārthaṃ vihāya ca \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. Except the materials for the homa and the articles of gift for the preceptor—because they are naturally pure, every other article shall be sprinkled with holy water.

## \|\| 10.12.84 \|\|

*dravyajātaṃ maheśāya dvijebhyaśca viśeṣataḥ \|*  
*bhaktebhyaśca pradāyātha śivāya gururūpiṇe \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. He shall make gift to brahmins especially to devotees and to Śiva in the form of preceptor.

## \|\| 10.12.85-87 \|\|

*vastrādi dakṣiṇāṃ dattvā praṇamya bhuvi daṇḍavat \|*  
*dorakaupīnavasanaṃ daṇḍācca kṣālitambhuvi \|\| 85 \|\|*

*ādāya homadravyāṇi samidhādīni ca kramāt \|*  
*samudratīre nadyāṃ vā parvvate vā śivālaye \|\| 86 \|\|*

*araṇye cāpī goṣṭhe vā vicāryya sthānamuttamam \|*  
*sthitvācamya tataḥ pūrvvaṃ kṛtvā mānasamañjarīm \|\| 87 \|\|*

85-87. He shall give cloths and the fee also. He shall prostrate before him. He shall take the loin-cloth, its string, staff and the articles for homa—sacrificial twigs etc. He shall go to sea-shore or a mountain or a river-bank or a Śiva temple or a forest or a cowpen. He shall sit on a good place and perform Ācamana. He shall make his mind pure like a flower bunch.

## \|\| 10.12.88-90 \|\|

*brāhmamoṃkārasahitaṃ namo brahmaṇa ityapi \|*  
*japitvā tristato brūyādagnimīḷe purohitam \|\| 88 \|\|*

*atha mahāvratamiti agnirvai devā nāmataḥ \|*  
*tathaitasya samāmnāyamiṣetvorjje tvā veti tat \|\| 89 \|\|*

*agna āyāhi vītaye śanno devīrabhiṣṭaye \|*  
*paścātprocya mayarasatajabhanalagaiḥ saha \|\| 90 \|\|*

88-90. He shall repeat Vedic mantras along with Oṃ and recite “Namo Brahmaṇe”\[4\] etc. thrice. Then he shall repeat the mantra “Agnimīle purohitam”,\[5\] perform the great rite and repeat. “Agnirvai”\[6\] “Iṣe tvorje tvā”, then repeat the Mantras”Agna āyāhi vītaye”,\[7\] “Śanno devīrabhiṣṭaye” along with Ma, Ya, Ra, Sa, Ta, Ja, Bha, Na, La, and Ga.

## \|\| 10.12.91-92 \|\|

*sammitaṃ ca tataḥ pañcasaṃvatsaramayaṃ tataḥ \|*  
*samāmnāyassamāmnātaḥ atha śikṣāṃ vadetpunaḥ \|*  
*pravakṣyāmītyudīryātha vṛddhirādaicca samvadet \|\| 91 \|\|*

*athāto dharmajijñāsetyuccārya punaraṃjasā \|*  
*athāto brahmajijñāsā vedādīnapi saṃjapet \|\| 92 \|\|*

91-92. This shall continue for five years. This is the tradition. Then he shall repeat the text of grammar, Mīmāṃsā and Vedanta. He shall repeat the names of the gods as well.

## \|\| 10.12.93-96 \|\|

*brahmāṇamindraṃ sūryyañca somaṃ caiva prajāpatim \|*  
*ātmānamantarātmānaṃ jñānātmānamataḥ param \|\| 93 \|\|*

*paramātmānamapi ca praṇavādyaṃ namoṃtakam \|*  
*caturthyantaṃ japitvā'tha saktumuṣṭiṃ pragṛhya ca \|\| 94 \|\|*

*prāśyātha praṇavenaiva dvirācamyātha saṃspṛśet \|*  
*nābhiṃ mantrānvakṣyamāṇanpraṇavādyānnamontakān \|\| 95 \|\|*

*ātmānamantarātmānaṃ jñānātmānaṃ puraṃ punaḥ \|*  
*ātmānaṃ ca samuccārya prajāpatimataḥ param \|\| 96 \|\|*

93-96. He shall perform the Japa of Brahmā, Indra, Sun, moon, Prajāpati Jñānātman and Paramatman with Praṇava in the beginning, namaḥ in the end using the dative case. He shall take some flour of fried grain, cat it with Praṇava, perform Ācamana twice, wash his hands, repeat the mantras mentioned hereafter. He shall repeat the names of Ātman, Antarātman, Jñānātman beginning with Praṇava and ending with Namaḥ. After repeating the mantra of Ātman he shall repeat the mantra of Prajāpati.

## \|\| 10.12.97-98 \|\|

*svāhāṃtānprajapetpaścātpayodadhighṛtaṃ pṛthak \|*  
*trivāraṃ praṇavenaiva prāśyācamya dvidhā punaḥ \|\| 97 \|\|*

*prāgāsya upaviśyātha dṛḍhacittaḥ sthirāsanaḥ \|*  
*yathoktavidhinā samyakprāṇāyāmatrayañcaret \|\| 98 \|\|*

97-98. He shall then perform the Japa ending with Svāha. Repeating Praṇava he shall take in milk, curd and ghee separately thrice and perform Ācamana twice. With the mind steady he shall sit firmly facing the east and perform prāṇāyāma thrice in accordance with the rules.

## Chapter 13 - The Procedure of Renunciation

## \|\| 10.13.1 \|\|

*\|\| subrahmaṇya uvāca \|*  
*atha madhyāhnasamaye snātvā niyatamānasaḥ \|*  
*gandhapuṣpākṣatādīni pūjādravyāṇyupāhareta \|\| 1 \|\|*

Subrahmaṇya said:—

1\. Then at midday he shall take bath. With the mind fully controlled he shall gather materials for worship such as fragrant flowers, Akṣatas etc.

## \|\| 10.13.2 \|\|

*nairṛtye pūjayeddevaṃ vighreśaṃ devapūjitam \|*  
*gaṇānāṃ tveti mantreṇāvāhayetsuvidhānataḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. With the mantra, “Gaṇānām tvā” he shall as enjoined invoke lord Gaṇapati worshipped by the gods. He shall worship hīm in the south-west.

## \|\| 10.13.3-5 \|\|

*raktavarṇaṃ mahākāyaṃ sarvvābharaṇabhūṣitam \|*  
*pāśāṃkuśākṣābhīṣṭañca dadhānaṃ karapaṃkajaiḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*evamāvāhya sandhyāya śaṃbhuputraṃ gajānanam \|*  
*abhyarcya pāyasāpūpanālikeraguḍādibhiḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

*naivedyamuttamaṃ dadyāttāmbūlādimathāparam \|*  
*paritoṣya namaskṛtya nirvighnamprārthayettataḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

3-5. After invoking the elephant-faced lord Gaṇapati son of Śivā, of red colour, huge body, bedecked in ornaments and holding noose, goad, Rudrākṣa and boon in his lotus-hand he shall worship him at dusk. He shall offer milk pudding (sweet pie), coconut jaggery and other things as Naivedya and also the leaf. After propitiating and bowing to him he shall pray for warding off evil.

## \|\| 10.13.6 \|\|

*aupāsanāgnau karttavyaṃ svagṛhyoktavidhānataḥ \|*  
*ājyabhāgāntamāgneyaṃ makhatantramataḥ param \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Thereafter the rites of sacrifice concluding with the sharing of clarified butter shall be performed in the Aupāsana fire according to the rules of one’s Gṛhyasūtra.

## \|\| 10.13.7 \|\|

*bhūḥ svāheti tryṛcā pūrṇāhutiṃ hutvā samāpya ca \|*  
*gāyatrīṃ prajapedyāvadaparāhṇamataṃdritaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. After offering the Pūrṇāhūti with the three Ṛks “Bhūḥ Svāhā and concluding the rites he shall repeat Gāyatrī assiduously till late in the afternoon.

## \|\| 10.13.8 \|\|

*atha sāyantanīṃ sandhyāmupāsya snānapūrvakam \|\| \|*  
*sāyamaupāsanaṃ hutvā maunī vijñāpayedgurum \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. He shall perform the evening prayers after bath and the sacrificial rites and inform his preceptor accordingly.

## \|\| 10.13.9-10 \|\|

*śrapayitvā caruntasminsamidannājyabhedataḥ \|*  
*juhuyādraudrasūktena sadyojātādi pañcabhiḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*brahmabhiśca mahādevaṃ sāṃbaṃ vahnau vibhāvayet \|*  
*gaurīrmimāya mantreṇa hutvā gaurīmanusmaran \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. After offering the Caru therein e shall perform Homa with different faggots of sticks, cooked rice and ghee repeating Rudrasūkta. He shall conceive Lord Śiva in the fire along with Ambā and the five Brahmans—Sadyojāta etc. He shall remember Gaurī and perform homa with the mantra “Gaurīrmimāya etc.

## \|\| 10.13.11-12 \|\|

*tato'gnaye sviṣṭakṛte svāheti juhuyātsakṛt \|*  
*hutvopariṣṭāttantrantu tato'gneruttare budhaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

*sthitvāsane japenmaunī cailājinakuśottare \|*  
*ābrāhmaṃ ca muhūrte tu gāyatrī dṛḍhamānasaḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. He shall perform sacrifice, repeat the mantra “Agnaye sviṣṭakṛṭe svāhā” and then show mystic gestures. Being of steady mind he shall sit on the,seat of cloth, deerskin or Darbha grass to the north of the fire and perform the Gāyatrī japa silently till the brāhma muhūrta.

## \|\| 10.13.13-19 \|\|

*tataḥ snātvā tvaśaktaścedbhasmanā vā vidhānataḥ \|*  
*śrapayitvā caruṃ tasminnagnāve vābhidhāritam \|\| 13 \|\|*

*udagudvāsya barhiṣyāsādyājyena caruṃ tataḥ \|*  
*abhighāryya vyāhṛtīśca raudrasūktañca pañca ca \|\| 14 \|\|*

*japedbrahmāṇi sandhāryya cittaṃ śivapadāṃbuje \|*  
*prajāpatimathendrañca viśvedevāstataḥ param \|\| 15 \|\|*

*brahmāṇaṃ sacaturthyantaṃ svāhāṃtānpraṇavā dikān \|*  
*saṃjapya vācayitvā'tha puṇyāhaṃ ca tataḥ param \|\| 16 \|\|*

*parastāttaṃtramagnaye svāhetyagnimukhāvadhi \|*  
*nirvartya paścātprāṇāya svāhetyārabhya pañcabhiḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

*sājyena caruṇā paścādagniṃ sviṣṭakṛtaṃ hunet \|*  
*punaśca prajapetsūktaṃ raudraṃ brahmāṇi pañca ca \|\| 18 \|\|*

*maheśādicaturvyūhamantrāṃśca prajapetpunaḥ \|*  
*hutvopariṣṭāttantrantu svaśākhoktena vartmanā \|\| 19 \|\|*

13-19. He shall then take bath. If he is feeble he shall smear Bhasma only. He shall cook Caru in the fire and pour ghee over it. Thus sanctifying it he shall consign it to the fire. He shall perform the Japa of the Vyāhṛtis, Rudra Sūkta and the five Brahmans. He shall fix his mind in the lotus-feet of Śiva. He shall perform the Japa of Prajāpatī, Indra, Viśvedevas and Brahma also, with dative case-endings. Praṇava and other mantras shall be repeated ending with Svāhā. He shall perform Puṇyāhavācana, say Agnye svāhā, show mystic gesture in front of the fire. Then he shall repeat Prāṇāya svāhā and offer five Āhutis to Sviṣṭakṛt fire. He shall repeat Rudrasūkta, the five Brahmans, the Caturvyūha mantras, Homa and mystic gestures in accordance with his Vedic branch.

## \|\| 10.13.20-21 \|\|

*tattaddevānsamuddiśya sāṃgaṃ kuryyādvicakṣaṇaḥ \|*  
*evamagnimukhādyaṃ yatkarmatantrampravarttitam \|\| 20 \|\|*

*ataḥ paraṃ prajuhuyādvirajāhomamātmanaḥ \|*  
*ṣaḍviṃśatattvarūpesmindehe līnasya śuddhaye \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. The same procedure he shall adopt for the other gods, along with their ancillary rites. After these rites beginning with the fire, the Virajā homa of the Ātman shall be performed for the purification of the self in the form of twenty-six principles.

## \|\| 10.13.22-23 \|\|

*tattvānyetāni maddehe śudhyantāmityanusmaran \|*  
*tatrātmatattvaśuddhyarthaṃ mantrairāruṇaketukaiḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

*paṭhyamānaiḥ pṛthivyādipuruṣāṃtaṃ kramānmune \|*  
*sājyena caruṇā maunī śivapādāmbujaṃ smaran \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. He shall say “Let these Tattvas be purified.” For the purity of the principle—Ātman, the mantras ‘Āruṇaketukas’ shall be repeated. He shall silently perform the Homa with the Caru and ghee and remember the lotus-feet of Śiva. O sage, the principles begin with Pṛthivī and end with Puruṣa.

## \|\| 10.13.24-27 \|\|

*pṛthivyādi ca śabdādi vāgādyaṃ pañcakaṃ punaḥ \|*  
*śrotrādyañca śiraḥ pārśvapṛṣṭhodaracatuṣṭayam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*jaṃghāṃ ca yojayetpaścāttvagādyaṃ dhātusaptakam \|*  
*prāṇādyaṃ pañcakaṃ paścādannādyaṃ kośapañcakam \|\| 25 \|\|*

*manāścittaṃ ca buddhiścāhaṃkṛtiḥ khyātireva ca \|*  
*saṃkalpantu guṇāḥ paścātprakṛtiḥ puruṣastataḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

*puruṣasya tu bhoktṛtvaṃ pratipannasya bhojane \|*  
*antaraṃgatayā tattvapaṃcakaṃ parikīrtitam \|\| 27 \|\|*

24-27. Pṛthivī, Sound, Speech, Ear—these are five each. The four head, sides, back, belly and the calves shall be added afterwards in the group—skin etc. The Dhātu group consists of seven. Prāṇas are five in number. Anna etc. are the five sheaths. Mind, Citta, intellect, ego, Khyāti come under Saṅkalpa. Guṇas come after; Prakṛti and Puruṣa follow these. Puruṣa is the only enjoyer and the five principles are the objects of his enjoyment.

## \|\| 10.13.28 \|\|

*niyatiḥ kālarāgaśca vidyā ca tadanantaram \|*  
*kalā ca paṃcakamidaṃ mayotpannammunīśvara \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O great sage, destiny, time, attachment, Vidyā, Kāla—this set of five is born of Māyā.

## \|\| 10.13.29 \|\|

*māyāntu prakṛtiṃ vidyāditi māyā śrutīritā \|*  
*tajjānyetāni tattvāni śrutyuktāni na saṃśayaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The Vedas declare: “Māyā is identical with Prakṛti.” The principles as mentioned in the Śruti are born of that.

## \|\| 10.13.30 \|\|

*kālasvabhāvo niyatiriti ca śrutitabravīt \|*  
*etatpañcakamevāsya pañcakañcakramucyate \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. The Vedas declare “Destiny is the nature of Kāla.” This set of five is also called “The wheel of five.”

## \|\| 10.13.31-33 \|\|

*ajānanpañcatattvāni vidvānapi ca mūḍhadhīḥ \|*  
*nipatyādhastātprakṛterupariṣṭātpumānayam \|\| 31 \|\|*

*kākākṣinyāyamāśritya varttate pārśvatonvaham \|*  
*vidyātattvamidaṃ proktaṃ śuddhavidyāmaheśvarau \|\| 32 \|\|*

*sadāśivaśca śaktiśca śivaścedaṃ tu pañcakam \|*  
*śiva tattvamidambrahmanprajñānabrahmavāgyataḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

31-33. Though sentient, the Puruṣa is confused in thought, not knowing the five principles. Though superior to nature he falls below Prakṛti as the eye of the crow. This set of five—the pure Vidyā Maheśvara, Sadāśiva, Śakti and Śiva is called Vidyātattva or Śivatattva. This is implied in the Vedic text “Prajñānam Brahma.”

## \|\| 10.13.34 \|\|

*pṛthivyādiśivāṃtaṃ yattattvajātaṃ munīśvara \|*  
*svakāraṇalayadvārā śuddhirasya vidhīyatām \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O Sage, the purity of the principles from Pṛthivī to Śiva is effected through the dissolution of its cause.

## \|\| 10.13.35 \|\|

*ekādaśānāṃ mantrāṇāmparasmaipada pūrvakam \|*  
*śivajyotiścaturthyantamidampadamathoccaret \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. With the eleven mantras he shall mention the god. The word Śivajyolis shall be used in the dative case.

## \|\| 10.13.36-38 \|\|

*na mameti vadetpaścāduddeśatyāga īritaḥ \|*  
*ataḥ paraṃ vividyaiti kapotakāyeti mantrayoḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

*vyāpakāya padasyānte paramātmana ityapi \|*  
*śivajyotiścaturthyantaṃ viśvabhūtapadampunaḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

*ghasanotsukaśabdañca caturthyaṃtamatho vadet \|*  
*parasmaipadamuccāryya devāya padamuccaret \|\| 38 \|\|*

36-38. After Svāhā” he shall say “Na mama” (not to me). By this, renunciation of motive is indicated. In the mantras “Vividyā” “Kaṣṭapotā” after the word Vyāpakāva the word Paramātmane shall be used. The words “Śivajyotis” “Viśvabhūta” and “Ghasanotsuka” shall be mentioned in dative ease. After mentioning the god by name the word Devāya shall be uttered.

## \|\| 10.13.39 \|\|

*uttiṣṭhasveti mantrasya viśvarūpāya śabdataḥ \|*  
*puruṣāya padambrūyādosvāhetyasya saṃvadet \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. After “Viśvarūpāya” in the mantra “Uttiṣṭhasva"’ the word “Puruṣāya” shall be uttered and the words “Om Svāhā” shall be added.

## \|\| 10.13.40 \|\|

*lokatrayapadasyānte vyāpine paramātmane \|*  
*śivāyedaṃ na mama ca padambrūyādataḥ param \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. At the end of the word “Lokatraya” the words “Vyāpine Paratnātmane Śivāyedam Na Mama” shall be added.

## \|\| 10.13.41-42 \|\|

*sva śākhoktaprakāreṇa purastāttantrakarmma ca \|*  
*nirvartya sarpiṣā miśraṃ carumprāśya purodhase \|\| 41 \|\|*

*pradadyāddakṣiṇāntasmai hemādiparibṛṃhitām \|*  
*brahmāṇamudvāsya tataḥ prātaraupāsanaṃ hunet \|\| 42 \|\|*

41-42. He shall conclude the rite according to his own branch of the Veda. He shall eat Caru mixed with ghee and then give fee of gold etc. to the priest. Alter the ritualistic dismissal of Brahmā he shall perform the morning worship.

## \|\| 10.13.43-45 \|\|

*saṃ māṃ siñcantu maruta iti mantrañjapennaraḥ \|*  
*yāte agna ityanena mantreṇāgnau pratāpya ca \|\| 43 \|\|*

*hastamagnau samāropya svātmanyadvaitadhāmani \|*  
*prābhātikīṃ tataḥ sandhyāmupāsyādityamapyatha \|\| 44 \|\|*

*upasthāya praviśyāpsu nābhidaghnaṃ praveśayan \|*  
*tanmantrānprajapetprītyā niścalātmā samutsukaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

43-45. He shall recite the mantra “Saṃ māṃ siñcantu Maruta.”\[4\] He shall warm his hand over the fire repeating the mantra “Yā te Agne” and superimpose the fire in his own Ātman, abode of the universal soul. He shall perform the morning prayers and pay respects to the sun. He shall enter water upto the navel, perform the Japa of the mantras with pleasure, devotion and steadiness.

## \|\| 10.13.46 \|\|

*āhitāgnistu yaḥ kuryyātprājāpatyeṣṭimāhite \|*  
*śraute vaiśvānare samyaksarvavedasadakṣiṇām \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The devotee who had been maintaining sacrificial fires regularly shall perform the Prājāpatya rite in the sacrificial fire and give his entire possession as gift.

## \|\| 10.13.47-51 \|\|

*athāgnimātmanyāropya brāhmaṇaḥ pravrajedgṛhāt \|*  
*sāvitrīprathamaṃ pādaṃ sāvitrīmityudīrya ca \|\| 47 \|\|*

*praveśayāmi śabdānte bhūromiti ca saṃvadet \|*  
*dvitīyampādamuccāryya sāvitrīmiti pūrvvavat \|\| 48 \|\|*

*praveśayāmi śabdānte bhuvaromiti saṃvadet \|*  
*tṛtīyampādamuccāryya sāvitrīmityataḥ param \|\| 49 \|\|*

*praveśayāmi śabdānte suvaromityudīrayet \|*  
*tripādamuccaretpūrvaṃ sāvitrīmityataḥ param \|\| 50 \|\|*

*praveśayāmi śabdānte bhūrbhuvassuvaromiti \|*  
*udīrayetparamprītyā niścalātmā munīśvara \|\| 51 \|\|*

47-51. The brahmin shall superimpose fire in his Ātman and leave the house. In the Sāvitrī mantra he shall utter the first foot and say “I enter Sāvitrī Bhūḥ Om”. Then he shall utter the second foot and say “I enter Sāvitrī Bhuvaḥ Om”. He shall utter the third foot and say “I enter Sāvitrī Suvaḥ Om”. Then he shall repeat all the three feet and say “Sāvitrīm Praveśayāmi”, at the end of which he shall say “Bhūḥ Bhuvaḥ Suvaḥ Om” with great pleasure and steady mind.

## \|\| 10.13.52-56 \|\|

*iyambhagavatī sākṣācchaṃkarārddhaśarīriṇī \|*  
*paṃcavaktrā daśabhujā vipañcanayanojjvalā \|\| 52 \|\|*

*navaratnakirīṭodyaccandra lekhāvataṃsinī \|*  
*śuddhasphaṭikasaṃkāśā dayāyudhadharā śubhā \|\| 53 \|\|*

*hārakeyūrakaṭakakiṃkiṇīnūpurādibhiḥ \|*  
*bhūṣitāvayavā divyavasanā ratnabhūṣaṇā \|\| 54 \|\|*

*viṣṇunā vidhinā devaṛṣigaṃdharvvanāyakaiḥ \|*  
*mānavaiśca sadā sevyā sarvvātmavyāpinī śivā \|\| 55 \|\|*

*sadāśivasya devasya dharmapatnī manoharā \|*  
*jagadambā trijananī triguṇā nirguṇāpyajā \|\| 56 \|\|*

52-56. He shall then think—“This is Goddess herself having half the body of Śiva. She has five faces, ten arms and fifteen eyes. She is bedecked with a down set with nine gems over which the crescent moon rises. She is auspicious, pure as crystal and bears ten weapons. Her body is adorned with necklaces, shoulderlets, bangles and anklets. Her ornaments are studded with gems. She wears brilliant garments. She is served by Viṣṇu, Brahmā the leader of the gods, sages, Gandharvas and human beings. She is Śivā pervading all Ātmans. She is charming wife of lord Sadāśiva. She is the mother of the universe and the trio. She is unborn. She is Saguṇa and Nirguṇa as well.”

## \|\| 10.13.57 \|\|

*ityevaṃ saṃvicāryyātha gāyatrīṃ prajapetsudhīḥ \|*  
*ādidevīṃ ca tripadāṃ brāhmaṇatvādidāmajām \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. After thinking thus the intelligent devotee shall perform the Japa of Gāyatrī the primordial goddess comprising three metrical feet. She is unborn and the bestower of brahminhood

## \|\| 10.13.58 \|\|

*yo hyanyathā japetpāpo gāyatrī śivarūpiṇīm \|*  
*sa pacyate mahāghore narake kalpasaṃkhyayā \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. The sinner who repeats Gāyatrī in any other manner is tortured in the terrible hell for the period of kalpa.

## \|\| 10.13.59 \|\|

*sā vyāhṛtibhyaḥ saṃjātā tāsveva vilayaṃ gatā \|*  
*tāśca praṇavasambhūtāḥ praṇave vilayaṃ gatā \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. She is horn of and merged into Vyāhṛtis. Vyāhṛtis are born of the Praṇava and are merged in Praṇava.

## \|\| 10.13.60 \|\|

*praṇavassarvavedādiḥ praṇavaḥ śivavācakaḥ \|*  
*mantrādhirājarājaśca mahābījaṃ manuḥ paraḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

60\. Praṇava is the initial of all mantras of the Vedas. It expresses Śiva. It is the emperor of all mantras. It is the great Bīja, the greatest mantra.

## \|\| 10.13.61 \|\|

*śivo vā praṇavo hyeṣa praṇavo vā śivaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*vācyavācakayorbhedo nātyantaṃ vidyate yataḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. Śiva is Praṇava, Praṇava is Śiva since there is no difference between Śiva and Praṇava that expresses Śiva.

## \|\| 10.13.62 \|\|

*enameva mahāmantrañjīvānāñca tanutyajām \|*  
*kāśyāṃ saṃśrāvya maraṇe datte muktiṃ parāṃ śivaḥ \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. It is this very mantra that Śiva speaks to individual souls who leave off their bodies at Kāśī to attain liberation hereafter.

## \|\| 10.13.63 \|\|

*tasmādekākṣarandevaṃ śivaṃ paramakāraṇam \|*  
*upāsate yatiśreṣṭhā hṛdayāmbhojamadhyagam \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. Hence good ascetics worship Śiva the single-syllabled lord who being the cause of the universe is stationed in the lotus of the heart.

## \|\| 10.13.64 \|\|

*mumukṣavo'pare dhīrā viraktā laukikā narāḥ \|*  
*viṣayānmanasā jñātvopāsate paramaṃ śivam \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Men desirous of salvation and unattached to worldly pleasures worship Śiva after realising the futility of worldly pleasures.

## \|\| 10.13.65 \|\|

*evaṃ vilāpya gāyatrīṃ praṇave śivavācake \|*  
*ahaṃ vṛkṣasya rerivetyanuvākaṃ japetpunaḥ \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. After dissolving Gāyatrī in the Praṇava identical with Śiva he shall recite the hymn “ahaṃ vṛkṣasya reriva.”

## \|\| 10.13.66 \|\|

*yaśchandasāmṛṣabha ityanuvākamupakramāt \|*  
*gopāyāṃtaṃ japanpaścādutthitohamitīrayet \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. He shall repeat the hymn beginning with “Yaḥ Chandasām Ṛṣabhaḥ” and ending with “gopāya.” Then he shall say I have got up.

## \|\| 10.13.67 \|\|

*vadejjayettridhā mandamadhyocchrāyakramānmune \|*  
*praṇavampūrvvamuddhatya sṛṣṭisthitilayakramāt \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. O sage, he shall recite the mantras in the low, medium and high tone beginning with the Praṇava in the order of creation, sustenance and dissolution.

## \|\| 10.13.68-69 \|\|

*teṣāmatha kramādbhūyādbhūssaṃnyastambhuvastathā \|*  
*saṃnyastaṃ suvarityuktvā saṃnyastaṃ padamuccaram \|\| 68 \|\|*

*sarvamaṃtrādyaḥ pradeśe mayeti ca padaṃ vadet \|*  
*praṇavaṃ pūrvamuddhṛtya samaṣṭiṃ vyāhṛtīrvadet \|\| 69 \|\|*

68-69. In this order he shall say Om Bhūḥ I have renounced. Om Bhuvaḥ I have renounced. Om Suvaḥ I have renounced. Then he shall say the composite Vyāhṛtis Om Bhūḥ, Bhuvaḥ, Suvaḥ.

## \|\| 10.13.70 \|\|

*samastamityato brūyānmayeti ca samabravīt \|*  
*sadāśivaṃ hṛdi dhyātvā maṃdādīti tato mune \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. O sage, he shall say “I have renounced after meditating on Sadāśiva in the heart in the low, medium and high pitch gradually.

## \|\| 10.13.71 \|\|

*praiṣamaṃtrāṃstu japtvaivaṃ sāvadhānena cetasā \|*  
*abhayaṃ sarvabhūtebhyo mattaḥ svāheti saṃjapan \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. After muttering the Preṣa mantras with attentive mind he shall say “Let all living beings have fearlessness from me, Svāhā.”

## \|\| 10.13.72 \|\|

*prācyāṃ diśyapa uddhṛtya prakṣipedajaliṃ tataḥ \|*  
*śikhāṃ yajñopavītaṃ ca yatrotpāṭya ca pāṇinā \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. He shall take water in the cupped palms and pour it to the east. He shall uproot the tuft and take out the sacred thread.

## \|\| 10.13.73-74 \|\|

*gṛhītvā praṇavaṃ bhūśca samudraṃ gaccha samvadet \|*  
*vahnijāyāṃ samuccāryya sodakāñjalinā tataḥ \|\| 73 \|\|*

*apsu hūyādatha preṣairabhimaṃtrya tridhā tvapaḥ \|*  
*prāśya tīre samāgatya bhūmau vastrādikaṃ tyajet \|\| 74 \|\|*

73-74. Taking the two in the cupped palms along with water he shall say Om Bhūḥ “Go thou to the ocean” after uttering Suāhā, the name of the wife of the fire. Along with the water in the palms he shall put the two in the water uttering Preṣa mantras. After performing Ācamana thrice he shall come to the bank and cast off all the clothes.

## \|\| 10.13.75-76 \|\|

*udaṅmukhaḥ prāṅmukho vā gacchessaptapadādhikam \|*  
*kiñciddūramathācāryastiṣṭha tiṣṭheti saṃvadet \|\| 75 \|\|*

*lokasya vyavahārārthaṃ kaupīnaṃ daṇḍameva ca \|*  
*bhagavansvīkuruṣveti dadyātsvenaiva pāṇinā \|\| 76 \|\|*

75-76. He shall then foot a short distance more than seven paces facing north or the east. Then the preceptor shall ask him to stop saying, “for your stay and activity in the world, O holy one, accept this loin-cloth and staff.” He shall hand them over to him with his own hands.

## \|\| 10.13.77-78 \|\|

*dattvā sudoraṃ kaupīnaṃ kāṣāyavasanaṃ tataḥ \|*  
*ācchādyācamya ca dvedhā ta śiṣyamiti saṃvadet \|\| 77 \|\|*

*indrasya vajro'si tata iti mantramudāharet \|*  
*samprārthya daṇḍaṃ gṛhṇīyātsakhāya iti saṃjapan \|\| 78 \|\|*

77-78. After giving the loin-cloth with its string, and the ochre-coloured robe he shall ask him to wear them. After performing Ācamana twice he shall tell the disciple thus “You are the thunderbolt of Indra.”. After praying and saying “A good companion”, he shall take up the staff.

## \|\| 10.13.79 \|\|

*atha gatvā guroḥ pārśvaṃ śivapādāṃbujaṃ smaran \|*  
*praṇameddaṇḍavadbhūmau trivāraṃ saṃyatātmavān \|\| 79 \|\|*

79\. He shall approach the preceptor remembering the lotus-like feet of Śiva and fall at his feet thrice with fully restrained mind.

## \|\| 10.13.80 \|\|

*punarutthāya ca śanaiḥ premṇā paśyanguruṃ nijam \|*  
*kṛtāñjalipuṭastiṣṭhedgurupāda samīpataḥ \|\| 80 \|\|*

80\. Standing up again he shall approach the feet of the preceptor with palms joined in reverence, glancing at his preceptor with love.

## \|\| 10.13.81-82 \|\|

*karmmārambhātpūrvameva gṛhītvā gomayaṃ śubham \|*  
*sthūlāmalakamātreṇa kṛtvā piṇḍānviśoṣayeta \|\| 81 \|\|*

*sauraistu kiraṇaireva homārambhāgnimadhyagān \|*  
*nikṣipya homasampūrttau bhasma saṃgṛhya gopayet \|\| 82 \|\|*

81-82. Before the rites are begun he should collect cowdung and make them into balls of the size of a myrobalan fruit and dry them in sunshine. The balls shall be placed in the sacrificial fire at the advent of homa. At the conclusion of the homa the ashes shall be collected and preserved.

## \|\| 10.13.83-85 \|\|

*tato gurussamādāya virajānalajaṃ sitam \|*  
*bhasma tenaiva taṃ śiṣyamagnirityādibhiḥ kramāt \|\| 83 \|\|*

*maṃtrairaṃgāni saṃspṛśya mūrddhādicaraṇāntataḥ \|*  
*īśānādyaiḥ pañcamaṃtrai śira ārabhya sarvataḥ \|\| 84 \|\|*

*samuddhṛtya vidhānena tripuṇḍraṃ dhārayettataḥ \|*  
*triyāyuṣaistryambakaiśca mūrdhna ārabhya ca kramāt \|\| 85 \|\|*

83-85. The preceptor shall take the white ashes of the Viraja fire. Repeating the mantras “Agniriti Bhasma”\[10\] he shall apply the sacred ashes over the body of the disciple from head to foot. Repeating the five mantras “Īśāna”\[11\] Tryāyuṣa”\[12\] “Tryambakam Yajāmahe he shall apply the Tripuṇḍra marks too beginning with head.

## \|\| 10.13.86 \|\|

*tatassadbhaktiyuktena cetasā śiṣyasattamaḥ \|*  
*hṛtpaṃkaje samāsīnaṃ dhyāyecchivamumāsakham \|\| 86 \|\|*

86\. Then the disciple shall meditate devotedly on Śiva, the consort of Umā in the lotus of his heart.

## \|\| 10.13.87 \|\|

*hastaṃ nidhāya śirasi śiṣyasya sa gururvadet \|*  
*trivāraṃ praṇavaṃ dakṣakarṇe ṛṣyādisaṃyutam \|\| 87 \|\|*

87\. Putting his hand on the head of the disciple the preceptor shall utter the Praṇava thrice into the right ear of the disciple along with the names of the sage etc.

## \|\| 10.13.88 \|\|

*tataḥ kṛtvā ca karuṇāṃ praṇavasyārtha mādiśet \|*  
*ṣaḍvidhārtthapari jñānasahitaṃ gurusattamaḥ \|\| 88 \|\|*

88\. The excellent preceptor shall be sympathetic and explain Praṇava with the knowledge of the six-fold topics.

## \|\| 10.13.89 \|\|

*dviṣaṭprakāraṃ sa guruṃ praṇamedbhuvi daṇḍavat \|*  
*tadadhīno bhavennityaṃ nānyatkarmma samācaret \|\| 89 \|\|*

89\. The disciple shall make obeisance to the preceptor by prostrating twelve times. He shall remain subservient to him always. He shall not do anything else.

## \|\| 10.13.90 \|\|

*tadājñayā tataḥ śiṣyo vedāntārthānusārataḥ \|*  
*śivajñānaparo bhūyātsaguṇāguṇabhedataḥ \|\| 90 \|\|*

90\. At his bidding the disciple shall pursue Śaivitc knowledge in conformity with the Upaniṣadic doctrine and based on the difference of Saguṇa and Nirguṇa.

## \|\| 10.13.91 \|\|

*tatastenaiva śiṣyeṇa śravaṇādyaṃgapūrvvakam \|*  
*prabhātikādyanuṣṭhānaṃ japānte kārayedguruḥ \|\| 91 \|\|*

91\. The preceptor shall make the disciple perform all the rites in the morning up to the Japa including the practice of hearing etc.

## \|\| 10.13.92 \|\|

*pūjāṃ ca maṇḍale tasminkailāsaprastarāhvaye \|*  
*śivoditena mārgeṇa śiṣyastatraiva pūjayet \|\| 92 \|\|*

92\. The disciple shall perform the worship in the maṇḍala Kailāsaprastara as enjoined by Śiva.

## \|\| 10.13.93 \|\|

*devannityamaśaktaścetpūjituṃ guruṇā śubham \|*  
*sphāṭikaṃ pīṭhikopetaṃ gṛhṇīyālliṃgamaiśvaram \|\| 93 \|\|*

93\. If he cannot worship the deity worshipped by his preceptor he shall take up Śivaliṅga of crystal along with the pedestal.

## \|\| 10.13.94 \|\|

*varaṃ prāṇaparityāgaśchedanaṃ śiraso'pi me \|*  
*na tvanabhyarcya bhuñjīyāṃ bhagavantaṃ trilocanam \|\| 94 \|\|*

94\. “I shall rather cast life and cut off my head than take meals without worshipping Śiva.”

## \|\| 10.13.95 \|\|

*evantrivāramuccāryya śapathaṃ gurusannidhau \|*  
*kuryyāddṛḍhamanāśśiṣyaḥ śivabhaktisamudvahan \|\| 95 \|\|*

95\. Thus shall the disciple devoted to Śiva take vow in the presence of the preceptor which he shall repeat thrice with a steady mind.

## \|\| 10.13.96 \|\|

*tata eva mahādevaṃ nityamudyuktamānasaḥ \|*  
*pūjayetparayā bhaktyā pañcāvaraṇamārgataḥ \|\| 96 \|\|*

96\. Thus the disciple shall worship Lord Śiva every day with devoted and diligent mind, following the path of five Āvaraṇas.

## Chapter 14 - The Praṇava in the form of Śiva

## \|\| 10.14.1-2 \|\|

*vāmadeva uvāca \|*  
*bhagavanṣaṇmukhāśeṣa vijñānāmṛtavāridhe \|*  
*viśvāmareśvarasuta praṇatārttiprabhañjana \|\| 1 \|\|*

*ṣaḍvidhārtthaparijñānamiṣṭadaṃ kimudāhṛtam \|*  
*ke tatra ṣaḍvidhā arthāḥ parijñānañca kiṃ prabho \|\| 2 \|\|*

Vāmadeva said:—

1-2. O Kārttikeya, the nectarine ocean of perfect knowledge, son of Śiva the lord of gods and the dispeller of distress of the devotees what is perfect knowledge of the sixfold topics that bestows all desires? What are the six topics? What is the perfect knowledge?

## \|\| 10.14.3 \|\|

*pratipādyaśca kastasya parijñāne ca kiṃ phalam \|*  
*etatsarvaṃ samācakṣva yadyatpṛṣṭaṃ mayā guha \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. What is explained therein? What is the fruit of that knowledge? Please mention all that I have asked.

## \|\| 10.14.4 \|\|

*etamartthamavijñāya paśuśāstravimohitaḥ \|*  
*adyāpyahammahāsena bhrāntaśca śivamāyayā \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Without knowing this I have been deluded by Śiva’s Māyā. I am ignorant of the Pāśupata Śāstra.

## \|\| 10.14.5-6 \|\|

*ahaṃ śivapadadvaṃdvajñānāmṛtarasāyanam \|*  
*pīttvā vigatasammoho bhaviṣyāmi yathā tathā \|\| 5 \|\|*

*kṛpāmṛtārdrayā dṛṣṭyā vilokya suciraṃ mayi \|*  
*karttavyo'nugrahaḥ śrīmatpādābjaśaraṇāgate \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. I have sought refuge in your lotus-feet. Glancing at me for a long time with your eyes drenched with the nectar of sympathy you shall bless me so that I shall I become free from delusion, drink in the nectarine potion of the knowledge of the feet of Śiva.

## \|\| 10.14.7 \|\|

*iti śrutvā munīndroktaṃ jñānaśaktidharo vibhuḥ \|*  
*prāhānyadarśanamahāsaṃtrāsajanakaṃ vacaḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. On hearing these words of the sage the lord bearing the trident of knowledge said words causing fears to other systems of theology.

## \|\| 10.14.8-9 \|\|

*subrahmaṇya uvāca \|*  
*śrūyatāmmuniśārdūla tvayā yatpṛṣṭamādarāt \|*  
*samaṣṭivyaṣṭibhāvena parijñānammaheśituḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

*praṇavārtthaparijñānarūpaṃ tadvistarādaham \|*  
*vadāmi ṣaḍvidhārthaikya parijñānena suvrata \|\| 9 \|\|*

Subrahmaṇya said:—

8-9. O revered sage, you have enquired about the perfect knowledge of Śiva in his composite and individual state. O performer of good rites, I shall explain that in detail Perfect knowledge comprises the meaning of Praṇava by realising the unity of six topics.

## \|\| 10.14.10-11 \|\|

*prathamo maṃtrarūpaḥ syāddvitīyo maṃtrabhāvitaḥ \|*  
*devatārtthastṛtīyo'rthaḥ prapañcārthastataḥ param \|\| 10 \|\|*

*caturthaḥ pañcamārthassyādgururūpapradarśakaḥ \|*  
*ṣaṣṭhaśśiṣyātmarūpo'rthaḥ ṣaḍvidhārthāḥ prakīrttitāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

10-11. The first topic is the mantra; the second Yantra; the third deity; the fourth the cosmos; the fifth the preceptor; the sixth the Ātman of the disciple.

## \|\| 10.14.12 \|\|

*tatra mantrasvarūpante vadāmi munisattama \|*  
*yena vijñātamātreṇa mahājñānī bhavennaraḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. O excellent sage, I shall explain to you the form of the mantra which makes a man possessed of perfect knowledge.

## \|\| 10.14.13 \|\|

*ādyassvaraḥ paṃcamaśca pañcamāntastataḥ paraḥ \|*  
*bindunādau ca pañcārṇāḥ proktā vedairna cānyathā \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. The first and the fifth vowels, the fifth letter of the fifth class of consonants, Bindu and Nāda these five letters are mentioned by the Vedas.

## \|\| 10.14.14-15 \|\|

*etatsamaṣṭirūpo hi vedādissamudāhṛtaḥ \|*  
*nādassarvvasamaṣṭiḥ syādbiṃdvāḍhyaṃ yaccatuṣṭayam \|\| 14 \|\|*

*vyaṣṭirūpeṇa saṃsiddhaṃ praṇave śivavācake \|*  
*yaṃtrarūpaṃ śṛṇu prājña śivaliṃgaṃ tadeva hi \|\| 15 \|\|*

14-15. The initial mantra of the Vedas i.e. Oṃ is of the composite form. Nāda too is the composite of all. The set of four with Bindu as the fifth is established as Vyaṣṭi in the Praṇava that expresses Śiva. O intelligent one, now listen to the form of Yantra that is Śivaliṅga.

## \|\| 10.14.16-17 \|\|

*sarvvādhastāllikhetpīṭhaṃ tadūrdhvamprathamaṃ svaram \|*  
*uvarṇaṃ ca tadūrddhvaṃ sthampavargāntaṃ tadūrdhvagam \|\| 16 \|\|*

*tanmastakasthaṃ biṃduṃ ca tadūrddhvaṃ nādamālikhet \|*  
*yaṃtre saṃpūrṇatāṃ yāti sarvakāmaḥ prasidhyati \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. Beneath all, the pedestal shall be drawn; then the first vowel—A; then the letter U, the final letter of the class of Pa i.e. Ma followed by Bindu and Nāda. When the Yantra becomes perfect, all desires are achieved.

## \|\| 10.14.18 \|\|

*etaṃ yaṃtraṃ samālikhya praṇave nava veṣṭayet \|*  
*tadutthenaiva nādena vidyannādāvasānakam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. The Yantra is encompassed by the Praṇava itself. The Nāda shall be known through the Nāda arising therefrom.

## \|\| 10.14.19 \|\|

*devatārtthampravakṣyāmi gūḍhaṃ sarvvatra yanmune \|*  
*tava snehādvāmadeva yathā śaṃkarabhāṣitam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. O sage, I shall now explain the secret topic of the deity. I shall mention it, out of my affection to you, in the manner explained by Śiva.

## \|\| 10.14.20 \|\|

*sadyojātamprapadyāmītyupakramya sadāśivom \|*  
*iti prāha śrutistāraṃ brahmapaṃcakavācakam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Beginning with “I resort to the lord born all of a sudden” and concluding with “Sadā Śivam” the Vedic text clearly expresses the five Brahmans.

## \|\| 10.14.21 \|\|

*vijñeyā brahmarūpiṇyassūkṣmāḥ paṃcaiva devatāḥ \|*  
*etā eva śivasyāpi mūrtitve nopabṛṃhitāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. The five deities shall be known comprising the subtle form of Brahman. These are enlarged in the form of the idol of Śiva too.

## \|\| 10.14.22 \|\|

*śivasya vācako mantraśśivamūrtteśca vācakaḥ \|*  
*mūrttimūrtimatorbhedo nātyantaṃ vidyate yataḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. The Mantra that expresses Śiva expresses his idol too, since there is not much of difference between the idol and the original.

## \|\| 10.14.23 \|\|

*īśānamukuṭopeta ityārabhya puroditaḥ \|*  
*śivasya vigrahaḥ pañcavaktrāṇi śṛṇu sāṃpratam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. I have already referred to the form of Śiva with Īśāna at the head. Now listen about his five faces.

## \|\| 10.14.24 \|\|

*paṃcamādi samārabhya sadyojātādyanukramāt \|*  
*urddhvāṃtamīśānāṃtaṃ ca mukhapaṃcakamīritam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. Beginning with the fifth one the Sadyojāta and proceeding till Īśāna upwards are the five faces.

## \|\| 10.14.25-26 \|\|

*īśānasyaiva devasya caturvyūhapade sthitam \|*  
*puruṣādyaṃ ca sadyāṃtaṃ brahmarūpaṃ catuṣṭayam \|\| 25 \|\|*

*paṃca brahmasamaṣṭissyādīśānaṃ brahma viśrutam \|*  
*puruṣādyaṃ tu tadvyaṣṭissadyojātāntikaṃ mune \|\| 26 \|\|*

25-26. The four Brahmans constitute a Caturvyūha for lord Īśāna, the famous Brahman. These five together constitute the composite. O sage, from Puruṣa to Sadyojāta the four constitute the individual.

## \|\| 10.14.27 \|\|

*anugrahamayaṃ cakramidaṃ paṃcārtthakāraṇam \|*  
*parabrahmātmakaṃ sūkṣmaṃ nirvikāramanāmayam \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. This set of five is the ‘wheel of blessing’. It is the same as Brahman. It is subtle; free from aberration and ailment.

## \|\| 10.14.28 \|\|

*anugraho'pi dvividhastirobhāvādigocaraḥ \|*  
*prabhuścānyastu jīvānāṃ parāvaravimuktidaḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The Anugraha or blessing is twofold: appearance and disappearance. The lord who bestows the greater and the lesser salvation to the souls is another.

## \|\| 10.14.29 \|\|

*etatsadāśivasyaiva kṛtyadvayamudāhṛtam \|*  
*anugrahe'pi sṛṣṭyādikṛtyānāṃ paṃcakaṃ vibhoḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. This is the twofold function of Śiva for ever. The five functions of creation etc. come under Anugraha.

## \|\| 10.14.30 \|\|

*mune tatrāpi sadyādyā devatāḥ parikīrttitāḥ \|*  
*parabrahmasvarūpāstāḥ paṃca kalyāṇadāssadā \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. O sage, there too the deities Sadya etc. are of the form of the great Brahman. The five always bestow happiness.

## \|\| 10.14.31 \|\|

*anugrahamayaṃ cakraṃ śāṃtyatītakalāmayam \|*  
*sadāśivādhiṣṭhitaṃ ca paramaṃ padamucyate \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. The Anugraha cakra is the greatest bliss presided over by Sadāśiva. It consists of the digit beyond calmness.

## \|\| 10.14.32 \|\|

*etadeva padaṃ prāpyaṃ yatīnāṃ bhavitātmanām \|*  
*sadāśivopāsakānāṃ praṇavāsaktacetasām \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. This region is attainable by the pious ascetics who worship Sadāśiva, with the minds fixed on the Praṇava.

## \|\| 10.14.33-34 \|\|

*etadeva padaṃ prāpya tena sākaṃ munīśvarāḥ \|*  
*bhuktvā suvipulānbhogāndevena brahmarūpiṇā \|\| 33 \|\|*

*mahāpralayasaṃbhūtau śivasāmyaṃ bhajaṃti hi \|*  
*na pataṃti punaḥ kvāpi saṃsārābdhau janāśca te \|\| 34 \|\|*

33-34. After attaining this region and enjoying vast pleasures along with the lord in the form of Brahman the sages attain equality with Śiva at the advent of the great dissolution. They never fall again in the ocean of the universe.

## \|\| 10.14.35 \|\|

*te brahmaloka iti ca śrutirāha sanātanī \|*  
*aiśvaryyaṃ tu śivasyāpi samaṣṭiridameva hi \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The eternal Veda says “Te brahmaloke” etc. The glory of Śiva is the composite form itself.

## \|\| 10.14.36 \|\|

*sarvaiśvaryeṇa sampanna ityāhātharvvaṇī śikhā \|*  
*sarvaiśvaryyapradātṛtvamasyaiva pravadanti hi \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The Vedic text Atharvaśīrṣa says “Equipped with glory and prosperity”. The ability to bestow glory shall be mentioned of him alone.

## \|\| 10.14.37 \|\|

*camakasya padānnānya dadhikaṃ vidyate padam \|*  
*brahmapaṃcakavistāraprapaṃcaḥ khalu dṛśyate \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. There is no higher region than that of Śiva. This universe is only an extension of the five Brahmans.

## \|\| 10.14.38 \|\|

*brahmabhya evaṃ saṃjātā nivṛttyādyāḥ kalā matāḥ \|*  
*sūkṣmabhūtasvarūpiṇyaḥ kāraṇatvena viśrutāḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. Nivṛtti and the Kalās are the offshoots of five Brahmans. They are in the form of subtle elements well known as causes.

## \|\| 10.14.39 \|\|

*sthūlarūpasvarūpasya prapaṃcasyāsya suvrata \|*  
*paṃcadhāvasthitaṃ yattadbrahmapaṃcakamiṣyate \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. O sage of good rites, the set of five Brahmans is the cause of gross cosmos.

## \|\| 10.14.40 \|\|

*puruṣaḥ śrotravāṇyau ca śabdakāśau ca paṃcakam \|*  
*vyāptamīśānarūpeṇa brahmaṇā munisattama \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Puruṣa, ear, speech, sound and ether, this set of five is pervaded, O excellent sage, by the Brahman in the form of Īśāna.

## \|\| 10.14.41 \|\|

*prakṛtistvakca pāṇiśca sparśo vāyuśca paṃcakam \|*  
*vyāptaṃ puruṣarūpeṇa brahmaṇaiva munīśvara \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Prakṛti, skin, hand, sense of touch and wind, this set of five, O great sage, is pervaded by Brahman as Puruṣa.

## \|\| 10.14.42 \|\|

*ahaṃkārastathā cakṣuḥ pādo rūpaṃ ca pāvakaḥ \|*  
*aghoravrahmaṇā vyāptametatpaṃcakamaṃcitam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. The ego, eye, foot, colour and fire, this set of five is pervaded by Aghora Brahman.

## \|\| 10.14.43 \|\|

*buddhiśca rasanā pāyū rasa āpaśca paṃcakam \|*  
*brahmaṇā vāmadevena vyāptaṃ bhavati nityaśaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. The intellect, tongue, arms, taste and water, this iet of five is pervaded by the Brahman Vāmadeva.

## \|\| 10.14.44 \|\|

*mano nāsā tathopastho gandho bhūmiśca paṃcakam \|*  
*sadyena brahmaṇā vyāptaṃ paṃcabrahmamayaṃ jagat \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The mind, nose, organ of generation, smell and earth, this set of five is pervaded by the Brahman Sadya. The whole universe is identical with the five Brahmans.

## \|\| 10.14.45-46 \|\|

*yaṃtrarūpeṇopadiṣṭaḥ praṇavaśśivavācakaḥ \|*  
*samaṣṭiḥ paṃcavarṇānāṃ biṃdvādyaṃ yaccatuṣṭayam \|\| 45 \|\|*

*śivopadiṣṭamārgeṇa yaṃtrarūpaṃ vibhāvayet \|*  
*praṇavamparamaṃ mantrādhirājaṃ śivarūpiṇam \|\| 46 \|\|*

45-46. Praṇava expressive of Śiva is taught through yantra. It is the composite of five letters. The four viz, Bīndu etc. constitute the form of yantra in the Śaiva cult. Praṇava is the highest Mantra—the very Śiva himself.

## Chapter 15 - The idol of Śiva for worship

## \|\| 10.15.1 \|\|

*īśvara uvāca \|*  
*tataḥ paraṃ pravakṣyāmi sṛṣṭipaddhatimuttamām \|*  
*sadāśivānmaheśādicatuṣkasya varānane \|\| 1 \|\|*

Subrahmaṇya said:—

1\. Henceforth I shall explain, O good-faced lady, the mode of creation for the set of four, Maheśa etc. from Sadāśiva.

## \|\| 10.15.2 \|\|

*sadāśivassamaṣṭissyādākāśadhipatiḥ prabhuḥ \|*  
*asyaiva vyaṣṭitāpannammaheśādicatuṣṭayam \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Sadāśiva the lord of ether is the composite. The set of four, Maheśa etc. is the individualistic form.

## \|\| 10.15.3 \|\|

*sadāśivasahasrāṃśānmaheśasya samudbhavaḥ \|*  
*puruṣānanarūpatvādvāyoradhipatiśca saḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Maheśa is a thousandth part of Sadāśiva. It has the form of Puruṣa for its face. It is the lord of wind too.

## \|\| 10.15.4 \|\|

*māyāśaktiyuto vāme sakalaśca kriyādhikaḥ \|*  
*asyaiva vyaṣṭirūpaṃ syādīśvarādicatuṣṭayam \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. He is associated with Māyā Śakti on his left. He is Saguṇa having much activity. The set of four Īśvara etc. is his own Vyaṣṭi.

## \|\| 10.15.5 \|\|

*īśo viśveśvaraḥ paścātparameśastataḥ param \|*  
*sarveśvara itīdantu tirodhācakramuttamam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. This set of four comprising Īśvara, Viśveśvara, Parameśvara and Sarveśvara is the excellent Tirobhāvacakra.

## \|\| 10.15.6 \|\|

*tirobhāvo dvidhā bhinna eko rudrādigocaraḥ \|*  
*anyaśca dehabhāvena paśuvargasya santateḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Tirobhāva is twofold. One concerns Rudra, the other the individual souls in view of their physical bodies.

## \|\| 10.15.7 \|\|

*bhogānuraṃjanaparaḥ karmasāmyakṣaṇāvadhi \|*  
*karmasāmye sa ekaḥ syādanugrahamayo vibhuḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The latter remains till equality in activity is achieved. When this is achieved, there remains only the lord of the form of blessing.

## \|\| 10.15.8 \|\|

*tatra sarveśvarā yāste devatāḥ parikīrttitāḥ \|*  
*parabrahmātmakāḥ sākṣānnirvikalpā nirāmayāḥ \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. There all the Īśvaras who are glorified as the deities, are identical with the great Brahman itself, free from alternatives and ailments.

## \|\| 10.15.9 \|\|

*tirobhāvātmakaṃ cakraṃ bhavecchāntikalāmayam \|*  
*maheśvarādhiṣṭhitaṃ ca padametadanuttamam \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The Tirobhāva Cakra consists of the Śāntikalā. This excellent region is presided over by Maheśvara.

## \|\| 10.15.10 \|\|

*etadeva padaṃ prāpyaṃ maheśapadasevinām \|*  
*māheśvarāṇāṃ sālokyakramādeva vimuktidam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. This region is attainable by those who serve Maheśa etc. It yields Sālokya liberation to the devotees of Maheśvara.

## \|\| 10.15.11 \|\|

*maheśvarasahasrāṃśādrudramūrtirajāyata \|*  
*aghoravadanākārastejastattvādhipaśca saḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. The image Rudra is born of a thousandth part of Maheśvara. He has Aghora for his face. He is the lord of the principle of fire.

## \|\| 10.15.12 \|\|

*gaurīśaktiyuto vāme sarvvasaṃhārakṛtprabhuḥ \|*  
*asyaiva vyaṣṭirūpaṃ syācchivādyatha catuṣṭayam \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. He is associated with Gaurīśakti on his left. He is the cause of dissolution of the universe; of him alone is the Vyaṣṭi born—the set of four, Śiva etc.

## \|\| 10.15.13 \|\|

*śivo haro mṛḍabhavau viditaṃ cakramadbhutam \|*  
*saṃhārākhyaṃ mahādivyaṃ paramaṃ hi munīśvara \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. This set of four consists of Śiva, Hara, Mṛda and Bhava. This wheel of dissolution, O sage, is wonderful and divine.

## \|\| 10.15.14-16 \|\|

*sa saṃhārastridhā prokto budhairnityādibhedataḥ \|*  
*nityo jīvasuṣuptyākhyo vidhernaimittikaḥ smṛtaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

*vilayastasya tu mahāniti vedanidarśitaḥ \|*  
*jīvānāṃ janmaduḥkhādiśāṃtānāmuṣitātmanām \|\| 15 \|\|*

*viśrāṃtyarthaṃ muniśreṣṭha karmaṇāṃ pākahetave \|*  
*saṃhāraḥ kalpitastredhā rudreṇāmitatejasā \|\| 16 \|\|*

14-16. Dissolution has been classified into three. The first Nitya is the daily slumber of living beings. The second is the conditioned dissolution of Brahmā. The third is the final dissolution. The three have been indicated by the Vedic texts. O excellent sage, the dissolution has been classified into three by the illustrious Rudra for the maturing of the activities of individual souls and for their rest. The souls released from their recurring births are ultimately placed in the great Ātman itself.

## \|\| 10.15.17 \|\|

*rudrasyaiva tu kṛtyānāṃ trayametadudāhṛtam \|*  
*saṃhṛtavapi sṛṣṭyādikṛtyānāṃ pañcakaṃ vibhoḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Thus I have mentioned the three activities of Rudra in respect of dissolution. The five activities of the lord in respect of creation shall also be explained to you.

## \|\| 10.15.18 \|\|

*mune tatra bhavādyāste devatāḥ parikīrttitāḥ \|*  
*parabrahmasvarūpāśca lokānugrahakārakāḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. O sage, the deities Bhava etc. are identical with the great Brahman, being the cause of bliss.

## \|\| 10.15.19 \|\|

*saṃhārākhyamidaṃ cakraṃ vidyārūpakalāmayam \|*  
*adhiṣṭhitaṃ ca rudreṇa padametannirāmayam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. This wheel of dissolution has Vidyārūpa Kalā. This healthy region is presided over by Rudra himself.

## \|\| 10.15.20 \|\|

*etadeva padaṃ prāpyaṃ rudrārādhanakāṃkṣiṇām \|*  
*rudrāṇāṃ taddhi sālokyakramātsāyujyadammune \|\| 20 \|\|*

20 This region is attainable by those who desire the propitiation of Rudras. O sage, it yields the Sāyujya of Rudras gradually through Sālokya.

## \|\| 10.15.21 \|\|

*rudramūrttessahasrāṃśādviṣṇoścaivābhavajjaniḥ \|*  
*sa vāmadevacakrātmā vāritattvaikanāyakaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. Viṣṇu was born from a thousandth part of Rudra. In the form of Vāmadeva wheel he is the presiding deity of the principle of water.

## \|\| 10.15.22 \|\|

*ramāśāktiyuto vāme sarvvarakṣākaro mahān \|*  
*caturbhujo'raviṃdākṣaḥ śyāmaśśaṃkhādicihnabhṛt \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He is associated with Lakṣmī Śakti on the left. He is the protector of all. He has four arms. He has eyes as splendid as the lotus. He is dark-complexioned and bears the characteristics of Śaṅkha etc.

## \|\| 10.15.23 \|\|

*asyaiva vāsudevādicatuṣkaṃ vyaṣṭitāṃ gatam \|*  
*upāsanaratānāṃ vai vaiṣṇavānāṃ vimuktidam \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Of him alone is the set of lour Vāsudevas in the Vyaṣṭi form. It bestows liberation on the devotees of Viṣṇu engaged in worship through meditation.

## \|\| 10.15.24 \|\|

*vāsudevo'niruddhaśca tatassaṃkarṣaṇaḥ paraḥ \|*  
*pradyumnaśceti vikhyātaṃ sthiticakramanuttamam \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. This set of four consists of Vāsudeva, Aniruddha, Saṃkarṣaṇa and Pradyumna. This excellent wheel of sustenance is well known.

## \|\| 10.15.25 \|\|

*sthitissṛṣṭasya jagatastatkartrā saha pālanam \|*  
*ārabdhakarmabhogāntaṃ jīvānāṃ phalabhoginām \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. Sustenance means the protection of the existing universe along with its creator until the completion of the pleasures of the souls, the reapers of the fruit.

## \|\| 10.15.26 \|\|

*viṣṇorevedamākhyātaṃ kṛtyaṃ rakṣāvidhāyinaḥ \|*  
*sthitāvapi tu sṛṣṭyādi kṛtyānāṃ paṃcakaṃ vibhoḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Protection is the activity of Viṣṇu. In the sustenance also the lord has five activities, viz; creation etc,

## \|\| 10.15.27 \|\|

*tatra pradyumnamukhyāste devatāḥ parikīrtitāḥ \|*  
*nirvikalpā nirātaṃkā muktānaṃdakarāssadā \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. The deities of whom Pradyumna is the chief are absolute and without distress. They cause bliss to liberated souls.

## \|\| 10.15.28 \|\|

*sthiticakramidaṃ brahmanpratiṣṭhārūpamuttamam \|*  
*janārdanādhiṣṭhitaṃ ca paramaṃ padamucyate \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O Brahmin, this wheel of sustenance which is permanent is presided over by Viṣṇu. It is the highest abode.

## \|\| 10.15.29 \|\|

*evadeva padaṃ prāpyaṃ viṣṇupādābjasevinām \|*  
*vaiṣṇavānāṃ cakramidaṃ sālokyādipadapradam \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. This region is attainable by those who serve the lotus-feet of Viṣṇu. This wheel yields Sālokya etc. to the devotees of Viṣṇu.

## \|\| 10.15.30 \|\|

*viṣṇoreva sahasrāṃśātsaṃbabhūva pitāmahaḥ \|*  
*sadyojātamukhātmā yaḥ pṛthivītattvanāyakaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Brahmā was born of a thousandth part of Viṣṇu.

He has the face of Sadyojāta and is the presiding deity of the principle of earth.

## \|\| 10.15.31 \|\|

*vāgdevīsahito vāme sṛṣṭikarttā jagatprabhuḥ \|*  
*caturmukho raktavarṇo rajorūpasvarūpavān \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. He is accompanied by the goddess of speech on his left. He is the creator and lord of the universe. He has four faces. He is red in complexion and his form is possessed of Rajas attribute.

## \|\| 10.15.32 \|\|

*hiṇyagarbhādyasyaiva vyaṣṭirūpaṃ catuṣṭayam \|*  
*hiraṇyagarbhotha virāṭ puruṣaḥ kāla eva ca \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. Hiraṇyagarbha, Virāṭ, Puruṣa and Kāla, the four constitute his personal form.

## \|\| 10.15.33 \|\|

*sṛṣṭi cakramidaṃ brahma putrādiṛṣisevitam \|*  
*sarvvakāmārthadaṃ brahmanparivārasukhapradam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. O Brahmin, this wheel of creation is the cause of Brahma’s sons and other sages. It bestows desires and happiness to the devotee.

## \|\| 10.15.34-35 \|\|

*sṛṣṭistu saṃhṛtasyāsya jīvasya prakṛtau bahiḥ \|*  
*ānīya karmabhogārtha sādhanāṃgaphalaissaha \|\| 34 \|\|*

*saṃyojanamitīdaṃ tu kṛtyaṃ paitāmahaṃ viduḥ \|*  
*jagatsṛṣṭikriyāvijñā yāvadvyūhaṃ sukhāvaham \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. Those who have the knowledge of creation know this to be the function of Brahman. Creation is the process of re-unification of the soul with the body previously annihilated—the body which along with the means, ancillaries and results comes out of Prakṛti for gradual enjoyment. It is pleasing as long as it holds good.

## \|\| 10.15.36 \|\|

*jagatsṛṣṭāvapi mune kṛtyānāṃ ca paṃcakaṃ vibhoḥ \|*  
*asti kālodayastatra devatāḥ parikīrttitāḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. O sage, in the creation of the universe there are five functions of the lord. The present time etc. are the deities there.

## \|\| 10.15.37 \|\|

*nivṛttirūpamākhyātaṃ sṛṣṭicakramidaṃ budhaiḥ \|*  
*pitāmahādhiṣṭhitaṃ ca padametaddhi śobhanam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. This wheel of creation is in the form of turning round. This charming region is presided over by Brahman.

## \|\| 10.15.38 \|\|

*etadeva pradaṃ prāpyaṃ brahmārpitadhiyāṃ nṛṇām \|*  
*paitāmahānāmetaddhi sālokyā divimuktidam \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. This region is attainable by those who have dedicated their minds to Brahmā. This indeed bestows Sālokya and other liberations on the devotees of Brahmā.

## \|\| 10.15.39 \|\|

*asminnapi catuṣke tu cakrāṇāṃ praṇavo bhavet \|*  
*maheśādikramādeva gauṇyā vṛttyā sa vācakaḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Even in regard to the set of four wheels the Praṇava indicates Maheśa etc.

## \|\| 10.15.40 \|\|

*idaṃ khalu jagaccakraṃ śrutiviśrutavaibhavam \|*  
*pañcāraṃ cakramiti ha stauti śrutiridammune \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. O sage, this wheel of universe is glorified by the Śrutis. The Vedas eulogise it as the wheel with five spokes.

## \|\| 10.15.41 \|\|

*ekameva jagaccakraṃ śambhośśaktivijṛṃbhitam \|*  
*sṛṣṭyādipaṃcāṃvayavaṃ paṃcāramiti kathyate \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Although the wheel of the universe with its five constituents: creation etc. has five spokes, it develops and increases by Śiva’s energy.

## \|\| 10.15.42 \|\|

*alātacakrabhramivadavicchinnalayodayam \|*  
*parito vartate yasmāttasmāccakramitīritam \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. It is called a “wheel” because it moves round and round with incessant dissolution and recreation like the whirling fire-brand.

## \|\| 10.15.43-44 \|\|

*sṛṣṭyādipṛthusṛṣṭitvātpṛthutvenopadṛśyate \|*  
*hiraṇmayasya devasya śambhoramitatejasaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

*śaktikāryamidaṃ cakraṃ hiraṇyajyotirāśritam \|*  
*salilenāvṛtamidaṃ salilaṃ vahnināvṛtam \|\| 44 \|\|*

43-44. Because of the immensity of creation it is called Pṛthu, the resultant of the Śakti of lord Śiva of immense splendour and of golden features. The wheel is dependent on golden splendour and is surrounded by water encompassed by fire.

## \|\| 10.15.45 \|\|

*āvṛto vāyunā vahnirākāśenāvṛtaṃ mahat \|*  
*bhūtādinā tathākāśo bhūtādirmahatāvṛtaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Fire is surrounded by wind; wind by ether; ether by the primordial element and that by intellect.

## \|\| 10.15.46 \|\|

*avyaktenāvṛtastadvanmahānityevamāstikaiḥ \|*  
*brahmāṇḍamiti saṃproktamācāryyairmunisattama \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Intellect is encompassed by the unmanifest. O sage, the universe is thus explained by the learned preceptors.

## \|\| 10.15.47 \|\|

*uktāni saptāvaraṇānyasya viśvasya guptaye \|*  
*cakrāddaśaguṇādhikyaṃ salilasya vidhīyate \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. Such are the seven sheaths which protect the universe. The expanse of water is ten times more massive than the wheel.

## \|\| 10.15.48 \|\|

*uparyupari cānyonyamevaṃ daśaguṇādhikam \|*  
*brahmāṇḍamiti vijñeyaṃ taddvijairmunināyaka \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. The world above is ten times more than the one below. O leading sage, the universe shall be known thus by those who desire to know that.

## \|\| 10.15.49 \|\|

*imamarthamurīkṛtya cakrasāmīpyavarttanāt \|*  
*salilasya ca tanmadhye iti prāha śrutissvayam \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. It is by accepting this sense that the Śruti says “In the middle of water”. Of course the earth rotates as the wheel.

## \|\| 10.15.50 \|\|

*anugrahatirobhāvasaṃhṛtisthitisṛṣṭibhiḥ \|*  
*karotyavirataṃ līlāmekaśśaktiyutaśśivaḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. That Śiva alone accompanied by Śakti performs incessant sports through blessing, disappearance, dissolution, sustenance and creation.

## \|\| 10.15.51 \|\|

*bahuneha kimuktena mune sāraṃ vadāmi te \|*  
*śiva evedamakhilaṃ śaktimāniti niścitam \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. O sage, of what avail is much talk? I shall tell you the essence. Śiva endowed with Śakti is all this. It is definite conclusion.

## Chapter 16 - Śiva’s principle

## \|\| 10.16.1 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śrutvopadiṣṭaṃ guruṇā vedārthaṃ munipuṃgavaḥ \|*  
*paramātmani saṃdigdhaṃ paripapraccha sādaram \|\| 1 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

1\. On hearing this truthful purport enunciated, by the preceptor the leading sage asked him about his doubt concerning the great Ātman.

## \|\| 10.16.2-3 \|\|

*vāmadeva uvāca \|*  
*jñānaśaktidhara svāminparamānandavigraha \|*  
*praṇavārthāmṛtaṃ pītaṃ śrīmukhakhjātparisrutam \|\| 2 \|\|*

*dṛḍhaprajñaśca jāto'smi saṃdeho vigato mama \|*  
*kiṃcidanyanmahāsena pṛcchāmi tvāṃ śṛṇu prabho \|\| 3 \|\|*

Vāmadeva said:—

2-3. O lord endowed with the power of knowledge and of the form of great bliss, I have heard the sweet meaning of Praṇava from your glorious lotus-like mouth and nourished intellect. My doubts have gone. O lord, Mahāsena, I ask you something else. Please listen.

## \|\| 10.16.4 \|\|

*sadāśivādikīṭāṃtarūpasya jagataḥ sthitiḥ \|*  
*strīpuṃrūpeṇa sarvatra dṛśyate na hi saṃśayaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. Surely this universe from Sadāśiva to a worm is reflected in male and female forms everywhere.

## \|\| 10.16.5 \|\|

*evaṃ rūpasya jagataḥ kāraṇaṃ yatsanātanam \|*  
*strīrūpaṃ tatkimāhosvitpuruṣo vā napuṃsakam \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. Is the eternal cause of the universe in the form of a woman, or of a man or a eunuch?

## \|\| 10.16.6 \|\|

*uta miśraṃ kimanyadvā na jātastatra nirṇayaḥ \|*  
*bahudhā vivadantīha vidvāṃsaśśāstramohitāḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. Or is īt a mixture of both or something else? There has been no decision so far. Deluded by sacred scriptures scholars argue on this point in various ways.

## \|\| 10.16.7 \|\|

*jagatsṛṣṭividhāyinyaḥ śrutayo jagatā saha \|*  
*viṣṇubrahmādayo devāḥ siddhāśca na vidanti hi \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The Vedas expatiating on the creation of the universe itself, Viṣṇu, Brahmā and other gods and the Siddhas do not know it.

## \|\| 10.16.8-10 \|\|

*yathaikyabhāvaṃ gaccheyuretadanyacca vedaya \|*  
*jānāmīti karomīti vyavahāraḥ pradṛśyate \|\| 8 \|\|*

*sa hi sarvātmasaṃsiddho vivādo nātra kasyacit \|*  
*sarvadehendriyamanobudhyahaṃkārasaṃbhavaḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

*āhosvi dātmanorūpaṃ mahānatrāpi saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*dvayametaddhi sarvveṣāṃ vivādāspadamadbhutam \|\| 10 \|\|*

8-10. Please explain so that the conflicting views may be resolved into one truthful interpretation. We say—“I know,” “I do”. This is accepted by all. There is no dispute about this. But I have some doubts. “Is there a form of the Ātman or is the Ātman identical with the body, sense-organs, mind, intellect and ego? The two are the points of dispute among many.

## \|\| 10.16.11-12 \|\|

*utpāṭyājñānasaṃbhūtaṃ saṃśayākhyaṃ viṣadrumam \|*  
*śivādvaitamahākalpavṛkṣabhūmiryathābhavet \|\| 11 \|\|*

*cittaṃ mama yathā deva bodhyo'smi kṛpayā tava \|*  
*kṛpātastava deveśa dṛḍhajñānī bhavā myaham \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. O lord, uproot this poisonous tree of doubt arising from ignorance. I shall be enlightened by you so that my mind may become the fertile ground for the growth of monistic knowledge of the Śaivite cult. O lord of gods, by your benign favour I shall become wise.

## \|\| 10.16.13 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*śrutvaivaṃ muninā pṛṣṭaṃ vaco vedāntanirvṛtam \|*  
*rahasyaṃ prabhurāhedaṃ kiṃcitprahasitānanaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

13\. On hearing the enquiry of the sage full of Vedāntic topics, the lord smiled a little and unfolded the secret.

## \|\| 10.16.14 \|\|

*subrahmaṇya uvāca \|*  
*etadeva mune guhyaṃ śivena paribhāṣitam \|*  
*ambāyāḥ śṛṇvato devyā vāmadeva mamāpi hi \|\| 14 \|\|*

Subrahmaṇya said:—

14\. O sage, this very secret was narrated by Śiva while goddess Ambā and I listened.

## \|\| 10.16.15 \|\|

*tasyāḥ stanyaṃ tadā pītvā saṃtṛpto'smi muhurmuhuḥ \|*  
*śrutavānniścalaṃ tadvai niścitaṃ me vicāritam \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. Then I was fully satiated with the drink of her breast-milk. I listened to it and I came to a decisive thought.

## \|\| 10.16.16 \|\|

*tatte vadāmi dayayā vāmadeva mahāmune \|*  
*mahadguhyaṃ ca paramaṃ suta tvaṃ śṛṇu sāṃpratam \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. O sage, I shall sympathetically explain the same to you. O son, it is the greatest secret. Please listen to it now.

## \|\| 10.16.17 \|\|

*karmāsti tattvādārabhya śāstravādassuvistaraḥ \|*  
*yathāvivekaṃ śrotavyo jñāninā jñānado mune \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The arguments in the philosophical text books are extensive beginning with those on Karmans and the principle of existence. But O sage, those arguments shall be listened to with discrimination by a wise person. Then it may yield wisdom.

## \|\| 10.16.18 \|\|

*tvayopadiṣṭā ye śiṣyāstatra ko vā bhavatsamaḥ \|*  
*kapilādiṣu śāstreṣu bhramaṃtyadyāpi te'dhamāḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. You have taught many disciples. But among them who is like you? Those base persons are rotting in delusion even now, wrangling in the philosophical texts propounded by Kapila and others.

## \|\| 10.16.19 \|\|

*te śaptā munibhiḥ ṣaḍbhiśśivanindā parāḥ purā \|*  
*na śrotavyā hi tadvārttā te'nyathāvādino yataḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. They indulged in insulting Śiva and hence were cursed by the seven sages. Their views should not be heard because they explain false notions.

## \|\| 10.16.20-22 \|\|

*anumānaprayogasyāpyavakāśo na vidyate \|*  
*paṃcāvayavayuktasya sa tu dhūmasya darśanāt \|\| 20 \|\|*

*parvvatasyāgnimadbhāvaṃ vadaṃtyatrāpi suvrata \|*  
*pratyakṣasya prapaṃcasya darśanālaṃbanaṃ tvataḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

*jñātavyaḥ parameśānaḥ paramātmā na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*strīpuṃrūpamayaṃ viśvaṃ pratyakṣeṇaiva dṛśyate \|\| 22 \|\|*

20-22. There is no scope for inference that consists of the five syllogisms. On seeing smoke they say that there is fire too. But here, O sage, the world is the object of perception. Its cause and support, the great Ātman Śiva must be known directly. The universe in the form of male and female is directly perceived.

## \|\| 10.16.23-24 \|\|

*ṣaṭkośarūpaḥ piṇḍo hi tatra cādyatrayambhavet \|*  
*mātraṃśajaṃ punaścānyatpitraṃśajamiti śrutiḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

*evaṃ sarvaśarīreṣu strīpuṃbhāvavido janāḥ \|*  
*paramātmanyapi mune strīpuṃbhāvaṃ vidurbudhā \|\| 24 \|\|*

23-24. The physical body consists of six sheaths. The first three are born of the parts of the mother and the other three of the parts of the father. So says the Śruti.

## \|\| 10.16.25 \|\|

*saccidānadarūpatvaṃ vadati brahmaṇaḥ śrutiḥ \|*  
*asannivarttakaḥ śabdaḥ sadātmeti nigadyate \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. The Śruti speaks of the Brahman as having the form of existence, knowledge and bliss. The word ‘existence’ is used in order to exclude the ‘non-existent’.

## \|\| 10.16.26-27 \|\|

*nivarttanaṃ jagattvasya cicchabdena vidhīyate \|*  
*triliṃgavarttī sacchabdaḥ puruṣotra vidhīyatām \|\| 26 \|\|*

*prakāśavācī sa bhavetsatprakāśa iti sphuṭam \|*  
*jñānaśabdasya paryyāyaścicchabdaḥ strītvamāgataḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

26-27. The exclusion of the insentient is effected by the word sentient. Of course the word Cit is used in all the three genders but it signifies here a male being. The word Cit in the feminine gender is a synonym of the word knowledge. If it is taken to mean ‘Light’, it is clear that it illuminates.

## \|\| 10.16.28 \|\|

*prakāśaścicca mithunaṃ jagatkāraṇatāṃ gatam \|*  
*saccidātmanyapi tathā jagatkāraṇatāṃ gatam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The pair Light and Cit is the cause of the universe. Similarly the Sat and the Cit are the causes of the universe.

## \|\| 10.16.29 \|\|

*ekatraiva śivaśśaktiriti bhāvo vidhīyate \|*  
*tailavarttyādimālinyātprakāśasyāpi varttate \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. In the self-same individual the feeling of Śiva and Śakti exists. If there is dirt in the oil or the wick there may be dirt in the light too.

## \|\| 10.16.30 \|\|

*mālinyamaśivatvaṃ ca citāgnyādiṣu dṛśyate \|*  
*evaṃ vivarttakatvena śivatvaṃ śruticoditam \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. Thus dirt and inauspiciousness is seen in the funeral fire also. Śivatva is due to illusion there.

## \|\| 10.16.31 \|\|

*jīvāśritāyāścicchakterdaurbalyaṃ vidyate sadā \|*  
*tannivṛtyarthamevātra śaktitvaṃ sārvakālikam \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. There is weakness in the power of Cit in the individual soul. It is to exclude it that the conception of Śakti is ever present.

## \|\| 10.16.32 \|\|

*balavāñchaktimāṃśceti vyavahāraḥ pradṛśyate \|*  
*loke vede ca sasataṃ vāmadeva mahāmune \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. O sage, people say in the world: he is strong, he is powerful and in the Vedas too there is a similar expression.

## \|\| 10.16.33 \|\|

*evaṃ śivatvaṃ śaktitvaṃ paramātmani darśitam \|*  
*śivaśaktyostu saṃyogādānaṃdassatatoditaḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. Thus the Śivatva and Śaktitva is present in the great Ātman. The bliss from the union of Śiva and Śakti is ever rising.

## \|\| 10.16.34 \|\|

*ato mune tamuddiśya munayaḥ kṣīṇakalmaṣāḥ \|*  
*śive manassamādhāya prāptāśśivamanāmayam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Hence, O sage, sinless ascetics aiming at bliss fix their minds in Śiva and attain untainted auspiciousness.

## \|\| 10.16.35 \|\|

*sarvātmatvaṃ tayorevaṃ brahmetyupaniṣatsu ca \|*  
*gīyate brahmaśabdena bṛṃhidhātvarthagocaram \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The word Brahman in the Upaniṣads signifies Ātman collectively. It can be traced to the root Bṛh to increase or grow.

## \|\| 10.16.36 \|\|

*bṛṃhaṇatvaṃ bṛhattvaṃ ca śaṃbhvākhyavigrahe \|*  
*paṃcabrahmamaye viśvapratītirbrahma śabditā \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Growing in size and massiveness is ever present in Śiva constituting the five Brahmans. The word Brahman denotes the universe.

## \|\| 10.16.37 \|\|

*pratilomātmake haṃse vakṣyāmi praṇavodbhavam \|*  
*tava snehādvāmadeva sāvadhānatayā śṛṇu \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. O Vāmadeva, due to my affection for you I shall explain the origin of the Praṇava when the word Haṃsaḥ is reverted. Listen attentively.

## \|\| 10.16.38 \|\|

*vyaṃjanasya sakārasya hakārasya ca varjanāt \|*  
*omityeva bhavetsthūlo vācakaḥ paramātmanaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. If the consonants Sa and Ha are removed it becomes Oṃ. It is the expression of the great Ātman.

## \|\| 10.16.39 \|\|

*mahāmantrassa vijñeyo munibhistattvadarśibhiḥ \|*  
*tatra sūkṣmo mahāmantrastaduddhāraṃ vadāmi te \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. It shall be known as the great Mantra by the expert sages. This great Mantra is subtle. I shall tell you its analysis.

## \|\| 10.16.40 \|\|

*ādye tripaṃcarūpe ca svare ṣoḍaśake triṣu \|*  
*mahāmantro bhavedādau sa sakāro bhavedyadā \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. The great Mantra is formed with “sa” and the sixteenth vowel (ḥ) = “saḥ” and “a” + “ha” with the fifteenth vowel (“ṃ”) = “aham” = (“so'ham”)

## \|\| 10.16.41 \|\|

*haṃsasya pratilomaḥ syātsakārārthaśśivaḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*śaktyātmako mahāmantravācyaḥ syāditi nirṇayaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. It is the inverted form of Haṃsa. The meaning of the letter ‘Sa’ is Śiva. The conclusion is, the Śakti form is expressed by the great Mantra.

## \|\| 10.16.42-43 \|\|

*gurūpadeśa kāle tu sohaṃśaktyātmakaśśivaḥ \|*  
*iti jīvaparo bhūyānmahāmantrastadā paśuḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*śaktyātmakaśśivāṃśaśca śivaikyācchivasāmyabhāk \|*  
*prajñānaṃ brahmavākye tu prajñānārthaḥ pradṛśyate \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. At the instruction of the preceptor, the great mantra is Śiva in the form of Śakti. Hence the great mantra is the individual soul too. The individual soul is in the form of Śakti and is a part of Śiva. Due to the identity with Śiva it attains equality with Śiva. In the passage (of the Vedas) “Prajñānam Brahma” the meaning of “Prajñānam” is seen.

## \|\| 10.16.44 \|\|

*prajñānaśabdaścaitanyaparyyāyassyānna saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*caitanyamātmeti mune śivasūtraṃ pravarttitam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. Undoubtedly the word “Prajñānam” is a synonym of consciousness. O sage, there is the aphorism.

## \|\| 10.16.45 \|\|

*caitanyamiti viśvasya sarvajñānakriyātmakam \|*  
*svātantryaṃ tatsvabhāvo yaḥ sa ātmā parikīrttitaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Caitanya is the freedom in respect of the knowledge and activity of every thing in the universe. He whose nature is this is glorified as Ātman.

## \|\| 10.16.46-47 \|\|

*ityādiśivasūtrāṇāṃ vārtikaṃ kathitaṃ mayā \|*  
*jñānaṃ baṃdha itīdaṃ tu dvitīyaṃ sūtramīśituḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

*jñānamityātmanastasya kiṃcijjñānakriyātmakam \|*  
*ityāhādyapadeneśaḥ paśuvargasya lakṣaṇam \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. Thus a gloss on the aphorisms of Śiva has been mentioned by me. In another aphorism of Śiva viz; “Jñānaṃ Bandhaḥ” the lord speaks of the nature of individual souls. There the word Jñāna refers to vague knowledge and activity.

## \|\| 10.16.48-49 \|\|

*etaddvayaṃ parāśakteḥ prathamaṃ spaṃdatāṃ gatam \|*  
*etāmeva parāṃ śaktiṃ śvetāśvataraśākhinaḥ \|\| 48 \|\|*

*svābhāvikī jñānabalakriyā cetyastuvanmudā \|*  
*jñānakriyecchārūpaṃ hi śaṃbhordṛṣṭitrayaṃ viduḥ \|\| 49 \|\|*

48-49. These two (knowledge and activity) are the first throbbing of the great Śakti. It is this Parā Śakti that the Śvetāśvataras eulogised joyously saying “Power of knowledge and activity is natural” They know the three eyes of Śiva constitute knowledge, will and activity.

## \|\| 10.16.50 \|\|

*etanmanomadhyagaṃ sadindriyajñānagocaram \|*  
*anupraviśya jānāti karoti ca paśuḥ sadā \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. When these reaching the centre of the mind become the object of perception on the part of sense-organs the individual soul enters these, then knows and acts.

## \|\| 10.16.51 \|\|

*tasmādātmana evedaṃ rūpamityeva niścitam \|*  
*prapaṃcārthaṃ pravakṣyāmi praṇavai kyapradarśanam \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. Hence these are the features of the Ātman alone. I shall now explain then meaning of the creation indicating the identity with the Praṇava.

## \|\| 10.16.52 \|\|

*oṃmitīdaṃ sarvamiti śrutirāha sanātanī \|*  
*tasmādvetītyupakramya jagatsṛṣṭiḥ prakrīrtitā \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. The eternal Śruti says “Om is this all.” Beginning with “verily from it”, the creation of the universe is glorified.

## \|\| 10.16.53 \|\|

*tasyāḥ śrutestu tātparyaṃ vakṣyāmi śrūyatāmidam \|*  
*tava snehādvāmadeva vivekārthavijṛṃbhitam \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. I shall mention the purport of the Vedic passage. Listen to that, O Vāmadeva, due to affection for you I say this. It is developed by the sense of discrimination.

## \|\| 10.16.54-55 \|\|

*śivaśaktisamāyogaḥ paramātmeti niścitam \|*  
*parāśaktestu saṃjātā cicchaktistu tadudbhavā \|\| 54 \|\|*

*ānandaśaktistajjāsyādicchāśaktistadudbhavā \|*  
*jñānaśaktistato jātā kriyāśa ktistu paṃcamī \|*  
*etābhya eva saṃjātā nivṛttyādyāḥ kalā mune \|\| 55 \|\|*

54-55. It is certain that the union of Śiva and Śakti is the great Ātman. From Parāśakti is born the citśakti. Born of it is the power of bliss. The power of will is born of that. From this is born the power of knowledge and from this is born the power of activity, the fifth one. O sage, the Kalās—Nivṛtti etc. are born of these Śaktis.

## \|\| 10.16.56-57 \|\|

*cidānandasamutpannau nādabindū prakīrttitau \|*  
*icchāśaktermakārastu jñānaśaktestu paṃcamaḥ \|\| 56 \|\|*

*svaraḥ kriyāśaktijāto hyakārastu munīśvara \|*  
*ityuktā praṇavotpattiḥ paṃcabrahmodbhavaṃ śṛṇu \|\| 57 \|\|*

56-57. Nāda and Bindu are born of cit and Ānanda Śaktis. O sage, the letter ‘ma’ is born of Icchāśakti. The fifth vowel ‘U’ is born of Jñāna Śakti and the letter ‘A’ is born of Kriyāśakti. The origin of the Praṇava has been mentioned thus. Now listen to the origin of the five Brahmans.

## \|\| 10.16.58 \|\|

*śivādīśāna utpannastatastatpuruṣodbhavaḥ \|*
