*tato'ghorastato vāmassadyojātodbhavastataḥ \|\| 58 \|\|*

58\. From Śiva is born Īśāna; from Īśāna the Puruṣa, from Puruṣa Aghora, from him Vāmadeva and from him Sadyojāta.

## \|\| 10.16.59-60 \|\|

*etasmānmātṛkādaṣṭatriṃśanmātṛsamudbha vaḥ \|*  
*īśānācchāntyatītākhyā kalā jātātha pūruṣāt \|*  
*utpadyate śāntikalā vidyā'ghorasamudbhavā \|\| 59 \|\|*

*pratiṣṭhā ca nivṛttiśca vāma sadyodbhave mate \|*  
*īśāccicchaktimukhato vibhormithunapañcakam \|\| 60 \|\|*

59-60. From these mātrās (of the praṇava) are born the thirty eight Kalās, Śāntyatītakalā is born of Īśana; Śāntikalā of Puruṣa; Vidyākalā of Aghora; Pratiṣṭhā and Nivṛtti of Vāmadeva and Sadyojāta. There are five pairs such as Īśāna and cit-śakti etc.

## \|\| 10.16.61 \|\|

*anugrahādikṛtyānāṃ hetuḥ pañcakamiṣyate \|*  
*tadvidbhirmunibhiḥ prājñairvaratattvapradarśibhiḥ \|\| 61 \|\|*

61\. This set of five is the cause of activities such as Anugraha etc. as mentioned by the sages who know the principles.

## \|\| 10.16.62-63 \|\|

*vācyavācakasambandhānmithunatvamupeyuṣi \|*  
*kalāvarṇasvarūpe'sminpañcake bhūtapañcakam \|\| 62 \|\|*

*viyadādi kramādāsīdutpannammunipuṅgava \|*  
*ādyaṃ mithunamārabhya pañcamaṃ yanmayaṃ viduḥ \|\| 63 \|\|*

62-63. O sage, the five dements beginning with the ether are born of this set of five letters of the Praṇava which constitute pairs based on the relationship of the word and its meaning.

## \|\| 10.16.64 \|\|

*śabdaikaguṇa ākāśaḥ śabdasparśaguṇo marut \|*  
*śabdasparśarūpaguṇapradhāno vahnirucyate \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. Ākāśa has the only attribute of sound. The wind has the two: sound and touch. The fire has three: sound, touch and colour.

## \|\| 10.16.65 \|\|

*śabdasparśarūparasaguṇakaṃ salilaṃ smṛtam \|*  
*śabdsparśarūparasagandhāḍhyā pṛthivī smṛtā \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. The water has four: sound, touch; colour and taste. The earth has five: sound, touch, colour, taste and smell.

## \|\| 10.16.66 \|\|

*vyāpakatvañca bhūtānāmidameva prakīrtitam \|*  
*vyāpyatvaṃ vaiparītyena gandhādikramato bhavet \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. This is the Vyāpakatva (pervasiveness) of the Bhūtas. The Vyāpakatva is in the inverse order beginning with smell.

## \|\| 10.16.67 \|\|

*bhūtapañcakarūpo'yamprapañcaḥ parikīrtyate \|*  
*virāṭ sarvasamaṣṭyātmā brahmāṇḍamiti ca sphuṭam \|\| 67 \|\|*

67\. The five Bhūtas constitute the universe. Virāṭ is the composite form of all. The universe is thus created.

## \|\| 10.16.68 \|\|

*pṛthivītattvamārabhya śivatattvāvadhi kramāt \|*  
*nilīya tattvasaṃdohe jīva eva vilīyate \|\| 68 \|\|*

68\. It begins with the element of the earth and ends with the element of Śiva. After merging with each other they merge in the individual soul ultimately.

## \|\| 10.16.69 \|\|

*saṃśaktikaḥ punassṛṣṭau śaktidvārā vinirgataḥ \|*  
*sthūlaprapañcarūpeṇa tiṣṭhatyāpralayaṃ sukham \|\| 69 \|\|*

69\. Accompanied by the energy it comes out again for the purpose of creation. It appears in the form of the gross cosmos and functions till the period of dissolution.

## \|\| 10.16.70 \|\|

*nijecchayā jagatsṛṣṭamudyuktasya maheśituḥ \|*  
*prathamo yaḥ parispandaśśiva tattvantaducyate \|\| 70 \|\|*

70\. The primeval oozing of Śiva who attempts to create the universe at his will is called Śivatattva.

## \|\| 10.16.71 \|\|

*eṣaivecchāśaktitatvaṃ sarvakṛtyānuvartanāt \|*  
*jñānakriyāśaktiyugme jñānādhikye sadāśivaḥ \|\| 71 \|\|*

71\. In view of its conformity with the activities this alone becomes the principle of his power of will. Between the two Śaktis of knowledge and activity if the knowledge predominates, the principle is Sadāśiva.

## \|\| 10.16.72 \|\|

*maheśvaraṃ kriyodreke tattvaṃ viddhi munīśvara \|*  
*jñānakriyāśaktisāmyaṃ śuddhavidyātmakaṃ matam \|\| 72 \|\|*

72\. O sage, know that as Maheśvara Tattva when the activity is predominant. If there is equality between the two Śaktis know that as the form of pure knowledge.

## \|\| 10.16.73-74 \|\|

*svāṅgarūpeṣu bhāveṣu māyātattvavibhedadhīḥ \|*  
*śivo yadā nijaṃ rūpaṃ paramaiśvaryyapūrvakam \|\| 73 \|\|*

*nigṛhya māyayāśeṣapadārthagrāhako bhavet \|*  
*tadā puruṣa ityākhyā tatsṛṣṭvetyabhavacchrutiḥ \|\| 74 \|\|*

73-74. After withdrawing his form of great glory with the intellect comprehending the difference between the illusion and reality among various emotions in the form of his own part when Śiva becomes the recipient of all objects through illusion, he is called Puruṣa, the creator. The Vedic text says “Tatsṛṣṭvā” (after creating it).

## \|\| 10.16.75 \|\|

*ayameva hi saṃsārī māyayā mohitaḥ paśuḥ \|*  
*śivajñānavihīno hi nānākarmavimūḍhadhīḥ \|\| 75 \|\|*

75\. He is transformed as the individual soul deluded by Māyā and devoid of the knowledge of Śiva. His intellect is deluded by different activities.

## \|\| 10.16.76 \|\|

*śivādabhinnaṃ na jagadātmānaṃ bhinnamityapi \|*  
*jānato'sya paśoreva moho bhavati na prabho \|\| 76 \|\|*

76\. It is the individual soul that is deluded and not the lord. Because the former considers himself different from Śiva.

## \|\| 10.16.77 \|\|

*yathaindrajālikasyāpi yogino na bhavedbhramaḥ \|*  
*guruṇā jñāpitaiśvaryaśśivo bhavati ciddhanaḥ \|\| 77 \|\|*

77\. As the magician so also the Yogin has no illusion. Śiva is of perfect knowledge and this is revealed by the preceptor.

## \|\| 10.16.78-79 \|\|

*sarvakartṛtvarūpā ca sarvajatvasvarūpiṇī \|*  
*pūrṇatvarūpānnityatvavyāpakatva svarūpiṇī \|\| 78 \|\|*

*śivasya śaktayaḥ pañca saṃkucadūpabhāskarāḥ \|\| 79 \|\|*

78-79. The five Śaktis of Śiva are: activity, knowledge, completeness, eternality and pervasiveness. Even in their shrinking forms they shine always.

## \|\| 10.16.80-81 \|\|

*api saṃkocarūpeṇa vibhāṃtya iti nityaśaḥ \|*  
*paśoḥ kalākhya vidyeti rāgakālau niyatyapi \|*  
*tattvapañcakarūpeṇa bhavatyatra kaleti sā \|\| 80 \|\|*

*kiṃcitkartṛtttvahetussyātkiṃcittattvaikasādhanam \|*  
*sā tu vidyā bhavedrāgo viṣayeṣvanuraṃjakaḥ \|\| 81 \|\|*

80-81. There is a set of five Tattvas of the individual soul, viz, Kalā, Vidyā, Rāga, Kāla and Niyati. Kalā is the cause of doing anything. Vidyā is the means of certain Tattvas. Rāga is the attachment to the sense-objects.

## \|\| 10.16.82 \|\|

*kālo hi bhāvabhāvānāṃ bhāsānāṃ bhāsanātmakaḥ \|*  
*kramāvacchedako bhūtvā bhūtādiriti kathyate \|\| 82 \|\|*

82\. Kāla is the illuminator of positive and negative appearances. Being a separating factor it is called Bhūtādi.

## \|\| 10.16.83 \|\|

*idantu mama kartavyamidanneti niyāmikā \|*  
*niyatissyādvibhośśaktistadākṣepātpatetpaśuḥ \|\| 83 \|\|*

83\. Niyati means the factor that discriminates and ordains. “This shall be done and this shall not be done by me.” It is a Śakti of the lord. Sotting it at nought the individual soul falls.

## \|\| 10.16.84 \|\|

*etatpaṃcakamevāsya svarūpā vārakatvataḥ \|*  
*pañcakañcukamākhyātamantaraṃgaṃ ca sādhanam \|\| 84 \|\|*

84\. This set of five is called “the five sheaths” in view of its nature of enveloping the form. It is the innermost and most essential means.

## Chapter 17 - The non-dualistic (advaita) nature of Śiva

## \|\| 10.17.1-2 \|\|

*vāmadeva uvāca \|*  
*niyatyadhastātprakṛteruparisthaḥ pumāniti \|*  
*pūrvatra bhavatā proktamidānīṃ kathamanyathā \|\| 1 \|\|*

*māyayā saṃkucadrūpastadadhastāditi prabho \|*  
*iti me saṃśayaṃ nātha chettumarhasi tattvataḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

Vāmadeva said:—

1-2. You have mentioned before that the Puruṣa is placed beneath the Niyati and is above Prakṛti. How is it that you say now that it shrinks in form through Māyā and is beneath it. O lord, it behoves you to clear this doubt of mine.

## \|\| 10.17.3 \|\|

*śrīsubahmaṇya uvāca \|*  
*advaitaśaivavādo'yaṃ dvaitanna sahate kvacit \|*  
*dvaitaṃ ca naśvaraṃ brahmādvaitamparamanaśvaram \|\| 3 \|\|*

Subrahmaṇya said:—

3\. This is the Śaivite philosophy of non-dualism. Nowhere does it brook duality. Duality perishes and the non-dualism remains imperishable.

## \|\| 10.17.4 \|\|

*sarvajñassarvakartā ca śivassarveśvaro'guṇaḥ \|*  
*tridevajanako brahmā saccidānandavigrahaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. The omniscient, omnipotent, Śiva, the Nirguṇa lord of all, the progenitor of the three deities, the Brahman, is formed of existence-knowledge-bliss.

## \|\| 10.17.5 \|\|

*sa eva śaṃkaro devassvecchayā ca svamāyayā \|*  
*saṃkucadrūpa iva sanpuruṣassaṃbabhūva ha \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. That lord Śiva himself, out of his own will, through his own Māyā appears shrinking in size and becomes Puruṣa.

## \|\| 10.17.6 \|\|

*kalādi pañcakenaiva bhoktṛtvena prakalpitaḥ \|*  
*prakṛtisthaḥ pumāneṣa bhuṅkte prakṛtijānguṇān \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. He is conceived as the enjoyer through the set of five—Kalās etc. Thus the Puruṣa stationed in Prakṛti enjoys the Guṇas born of Prakṛti.

## \|\| 10.17.7 \|\|

*iti sthānadvayāntasthaḥ puruṣo na virodhakaḥ \|*  
*saṃkucannijarūpāṇāṃ jñānādīnāṃ samaṣṭimān \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. The presence in two places of the Puruṣa is not incompatible. He is the composite of Jñana etc. of both shrinking and intrinsic forms.

## \|\| 10.17.8-9 \|\|

*sattvādiguṇasādhyaṃ ca budhyāditritayātmakam \|*  
*cittamprakṛtitattvaṃ tadāsītsattvādikāraṇāt \|\| 8 \|\|*

*sāttvikādivibhedena guṇāḥ prakṛtisambhavāḥ \|*  
*guṇebhyo buddhirutpannā vastuniścayakāriṇī \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. The principle of the mind Prakṛti consists of Buddhi, Manas and Ahaṃkāra achievable through the Guṇas Sattva etc. The Guṇas are born of the Prakṛti, the Sāttvaic from Sattva and so on. From the Guṇas the intellect takes its origin. It is the cause of decisiveness in the objects.

## \|\| 10.17.10 \|\|

*tato mahānahaṅkārastato buddhīndriyāṇi ca \|*  
*jātāni manaso rūpaṃ syātsaṃkalpavikalpakam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. From Buddhi the principle of intellect is born and from it ego. From the ego the sense-organs of knowledge. The mind has two aspects—thought and doubt.

## \|\| 10.17.11-12 \|\|

*buddhīndriyāṇi śrotraṃ tvak cakṣurjihvā ca nāsikā \|*  
*śabdaḥ sparśaśca rūpaṃ ca raso gandhaśca gocaraḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

*buddhīndriyāṇāṃ kathitaḥ śrotrādikramatastataḥ \|*  
*vaikārikādahaṃkārāttanmātrāṇyabhavankramāt \|\| 12 \|\|*

11-12. The sense-organs are ears, skin, eyes, tongue and nose and the qualities particularly sensed through those organs are sound, touch, colour, taste and smell. The Tanmātras are born from the Vaikārika Ahaṃkāra.

## \|\| 10.17.13 \|\|

*tāni proktāni sūkṣmāṇi munibhi stattvadarśibhiḥ \|*  
*karmendriyāṇi jñeyāni svakāryyasahitāni ca \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. They are called subtle principles by the sages. Now understand the organs of activity and their functions.

## \|\| 10.17.14 \|\|

*viprarṣe vākkarau pādau pāyūpasthau ca tatkriyāḥ \|*  
*vacanādānagamanavisarggānandasaṃjñitāḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. O sage, they are the organs of speech, hand, feet, anus, penis and the vaginal passage. Their functions are speaking, handling, moving, evacuation and the sexual pleasure.

## \|\| 10.17.15 \|\|

*bhūtādikādahaṃkārāttanmātrāṇyabhavankramāt \|*  
*tāni sūkṣmāṇi rūpāṇī śabdādīnāmiti sthitiḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The subtle elements originated in order from the ego, are the cause of the elements. Their subtle forms are sound etc.

## \|\| 10.17.16 \|\|

*tebhyaścākāśavāyvagnijalabhūmijaniḥ kramāt \|*  
*vijñeyā muniśārdūla pañcabhūtamitīṣyate \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. From them are born in order the ether, wind, fire, water and the earth. O sage, they are known as the five elements.

## \|\| 10.17.17 \|\|

*avakāśapradānaṃ ca vāhakatvañca pāvanam \|*  
*saṃrambho dhāranteṣāṃ vyāpārāḥ parikīrtitāḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. Their functions are giving space, blowing, cooking, force and supporting.

## \|\| 10.17.18 \|\|

*vāmadeva uvāca \|*  
*bhūtasṛṣṭiḥ purā proktā kalādibhyaḥ kathampunaḥ \|*  
*anyathā procyate skanda saṃdeho'tra mahānmama \|\| 18 \|\|*

Vāmadeva said:—

18\. O Skanda, you have mentioned before that the elements were created from Kalās etc. How is it that it is being mentioned in another manner? I have great doubt here.

## \|\| 10.17.19 \|\|

*ātmatattvamakārassyādvidyā syādustataḥ param \|*  
*śivatattvammakārassyādvāmadeveti ciṃtyatām \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. “The letter ‘A’ is the Ātmatattva; the letter ‘U’ is ‘Vidyā’; the letter ‘Ma’ is Śivatattva. O Vāmadeva let this be thought over thus.

## \|\| 10.17.20 \|\|

*bindunādau tu vijñeyau sarvatattvārthakāvubhau \|*  
*tatratyā devatāyāśca tā mune śṛṇu sāmpratam \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. “The Bindu and Nāda have essence of all the principles. O sage, listen now to what the deities therein arc.

## \|\| 10.17.21 \|\|

*brahmā viṣṇuśca rudraśca maheśvarasadāśivau \|*  
*te hi sākṣācchivasyaiva mūrtayaḥ śrutiviśrutāḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. They are Brahmā, Viṣṇu, Rudra, Maheśvara and Sadāśiva. They are the images of Śiva himself well known in the Vedas

## \|\| 10.17.22 \|\|

*ityuktambhavatā pūrvamidānīmucyate'nyathā \|*  
*tanmātrebhyo bhavantīti sandeho'tra mahānmama \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. This is what you have said before. Now you say in another manner that they are born of the subtle elements. I have a great doubt in this matter.

## \|\| 10.17.23 \|\|

*kṛtvā tatkaruṇāṃ skanda saṃśayaṃ chettumarhasi \|*  
*ityākarṇya munervākyaṃ kumāraḥ pratyabhāṣata \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Therefore, O Skanda, it behoves you to favour me by clearing the doubt. On hearing the words of the sage, Kumāra replied thus.

## \|\| 10.17.24-26 \|\|

*śrīsubrahmaṇya uvāca \|*  
*tasmādveti samārabhya bhūtasṛṣṭikrame mune \|*  
*tāñchṛṇuṣva mahāprājña sāvadhānatayā da rāt \|\| 24 \|\|*

*jātāni pañca bhūtāni kalābhya iti niścitam \|*  
*sthūlaprapañcarūpāṇi tāni bhūtapatervapuḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

*śivatattvādi pṛthvyantaṃ tattvānāmudayakrame \|*  
*tanmātrebhyo bhavantīti vaktavyāni kramānmune \|\| 26 \|\|*

Subrahmaṇya said:—

24-26. O intelligent sage, listen with respect and attention. O sage, in accordance with the Vedic text “Tasmād Vai Etasmād” etc. it is certain that the five Bhūtas are born of the Kalās. They are in the form of the gross cosmos. This explains the body of the lord of Bhūtas (Śiva). In the order of the coming up of the Tattvas from Śiva Tattva to the principle of the earth, O sage, they shall be mentioned as originating from the subtle elements in order.

## \|\| 10.17.27 \|\|

*tanmātrāṇāṃ kalānāmapyaikyaṃ syādbhūtakāraṇam \|*  
*aviruddhatva mevātra viddhi brahmāvidāṃvara \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. There shall be identity between the subtle elements and the Kalās as causes of the Bhūtas. O excellent among those who know the Brahman, know that there is no mutual antagonism here.

## \|\| 10.17.28 \|\|

*sthūlasūkṣmātmake viśve candrasūryyādayo grahāḥ \|*  
*sanakṣatrāśca saṃjātāstathānye jyotiṣāṃ gaṇāḥ \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. In the cosmos both subtle and gross are born the planets: moon, sun stars, etc.

## \|\| 10.17.29-32 \|\|

*brahmaviṣṇumaheśādidevatā bhūtajātayaḥ \|*  
*indrādayo'pi dikpālā devāśca pitaro'surāḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

*rākṣasā mānuṣāścānye jaṃgamatvavibhāginaḥ \|*  
*paśavaḥ pakṣiṇaḥ kīṭāḥ pannagādi prabhedinaḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

*tarugulmalatauṣadhyaḥ parvatāścāṣṭa viśrutāḥ \|*  
*gaṃgādyāssaritassapta sāgarāśca maharddhayaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*yatkiṃcidvastujātantatsarvamatra pratiṣṭhitam \|*  
*vicāraṇīyaṃ sadbudhyā na bahirmunisattama \|\| 32 \|\|*

29-32. The deities Brahmā, Viṣṇu, Maheśa and others, the various living beings, Indra and other guardians of the quarters, gods, Pitṛs Asuras, Rākṣasas, human and other mobile beings, beasts, birds, worms, serpents, trees, hedges, creepers and herbs, the eight mountains\[2\], the seven rivers Gaṅgā and others, the oceans of great prosperity whatever object is there in the world all are established here.

## \|\| 10.17.33 \|\|

*strīpuṃrūpamidaṃ viśvaṃ śivaśaktyātmakaṃ budhaiḥ \|*  
*bhavādṛśairupāsyaṃ syācchivajñānaviśāradaiḥ \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. This universe of the form of female and male, of the form of Śiva and Śakti shall deserve attention of learned men like you, experts in the Śaiva knowledge.

## \|\| 10.17.34 \|\|

*sarvaṃ brahmetyupāsīta sarvaṃ vai rudra ityapi \|*  
*śrutirāha mune tasmātprapañcātmā sadāśivaḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. O sage, the Śruti says “Think that everything is Brahman, everything is Rudra.” Thus Sadāśiva is the Ātman of the universe.

## \|\| 10.17.35 \|\|

*aṣṭatriṃśatkalānyāsasāmarthyādvaitabhāvanā \|*  
*sadāśivo'hameveti bhāvi tātmā guruḥ śivaḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Due to the concept of thirty-eight Kalās the concept of duality is ever present but he who has purified the Ātman by the concept “I am Sadāśiva” becomes the preceptor Śiva.

## \|\| 10.17.36 \|\|

*evaṃ vicārī sacchiṣyo gurussyātsa śivassvayam \|*  
*prapañcadevatāyaṃtramaṃtrātmā na hi saṃśayaḥ \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Such a disciple becomes the preceptor Śiva himself. He attains the form of the Yantra, mantra and the cosmic deity itself.

## \|\| 10.17.37 \|\|

*ācāryya rūpayā vipra saṃchinnākhilabandhanaḥ \|*  
*śiśuḥ śivapadāsakto gurvātmā bhavati dhuvam \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. O Brahmin, the disciple whose bondage is severed due to the favour of the preceptor and who is engaged in Śiva’s worship becomes a lofty soul indeed.

## \|\| 10.17.38 \|\|

*yadasti vastu tatsarvaṃ guṇa prādhānyayogataḥ \|*  
*samastaṃ vyastamapi ca praṇavārthampracakṣate \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. They say that whatever exists whether a composite unit or an analysed piece is the meaning of the Praṇava in view of the predominence of the Guṇas.

## \|\| 10.17.39 \|\|

*rāgādidoṣarahitaṃ vedasāraḥ śivo diśaḥ \|*  
*tubhyamme kathitamprītyā'dvaitajñānaṃ śivapriyam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Out of affection I have explained the non-dualistic principle to you. It is pleasing to Śiva, it is devoid of aberrations; it is the essence of the Vedas.

## \|\| 10.17.40-41 \|\|

*yo hyanyathaitanmanute madvaco madagarvitaḥ \|*  
*devo vā mānavassiddho gandharvo manujo'pi vā \|\| 40 \|\|*

*durātmanastasya śiraśchiṃdyāṃ samatayāddhruvam \|*  
*sacchaktyā ripukālāgnikalpayā na hi saṃśayaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

40-41. If anyone whether a god or a human being, Siddha or Gandharva, out of arrogance considers this statement otherwise, I shall cut off his head evenly with my good trident which is like a black fire to the enemies.

## \|\| 10.17.42 \|\|

*bhavāneva mune sākṣācchivādvaitavidāṃvaraḥ \|*  
*śivajñānopadeśe hi śivācārapradarśakaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. O sage, you yourself are the most excellent of those who know the non-dualistic nature of Śiva. You are the guide for good behaviour on the occasion of the instruction of Śaivite knowledge.

## \|\| 10.17.43 \|\|

*yaddehabhasmasamparkātsaṃchinnāghavrajo'śuciḥ \|*  
*mahāpiśācaḥ samprāpya tvatkṛpātassatāṃ gatim \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. An impious ghost got his sins removed by the contact with the ashes on your body. By your grace he attained the supreme goal.

## \|\| 10.17.44 \|\|

*śivayogīti saṃkhyātatriloka vibhavo bhavān \|*  
*bhavatkaṭākṣasamparkātpaśu paśupatirbhavet \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. You are a Śiva yogin, an asset to the three worlds. At your benign glance even the individual soul becomes the supreme soul.

## \|\| 10.17.45 \|\|

*tava tasya mayi prekṣā lokāśikṣārthamādarāt \|*  
*lokopakārakaraṇe vicarantīha sādhavaḥ \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. It is for instructing the world that a noble soul as yourself look up to me with respect. Indeed the saints wander here and there for helping the wide world.

## \|\| 10.17.46-47 \|\|

*idaṃ rahasyamparamaṃ pratiṣṭhitamatastvayi \|*  
*tvamapi śraddhayā bhaktyā praṇaveṣveva sādaram \|\| 46 \|\|*

*upaviśya ca tānsarvānsaṃyojya parameśvare \|*  
*śivācāraṃ grāhayasva bhūtirudrākṣamiśritam \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. This great secret is founded in you. You too, abide by the Praṇavas alone with respect, faith and devotion and unite them with Parameśvara helping people grasp Śaivite conduct including Bhasma and Rudrākṣa.

## \|\| 10.17.48 \|\|

*tvaṃ śivo hi śivācārī samprāptādvaitabhāvataḥ \|*  
*vicaraṃlokarakṣāyai sukhamakṣayamāpnuhi \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. You are Śiva indeed. You follow Śaivite conduct and have understood the concept of non-dualism Wandering about for the welfare of the world you will attain an everlasting happiness.

## \|\| 10.17.49 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*śrutvedamadbhutamataṃ hi ṣaḍānanoktaṃ vedāntaniṣṭhitamṛṣistu vinamramūrttiḥ \|*  
*bhūtvā praṇamya bahuśo bhuvi daṇḍavattatpādāravindaviharanmadhupatvamāpa \|\| 49 \|\|*

Sūta said:—

49\. After hearing this wonderful cult of Vedānta, explained by Kārttikeya the sage became very humble. He bowed many times and prostrated before him. He behaved like a bee hovering round his lotus-like feet.

## Chapter 18 - The procedure of initiating a disciple

## \|\| 10.18.1 \|\|

*\|\| śaunaka uvāca \|*  
*śrutvā vedāntasāraṃ tadrahasyamparamādbhutam \|*  
*kimpṛṣṭavānvāmadevo maheśvarasutaṃ tadā \|\| 1 \|\|*

Śaunaka said:—

1\. On hearing that extremely wonderful secret, the essence of the Vedānta, what did Vāmadeva ask of the son of Maheśvara?

## \|\| 10.18.2 \|\|

*dhanyo yogī vāmadevaḥ śivajñānaratassadā \|*  
*yatssambandhātkathotpannā divyā paramapāvanī \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. Blessed indeed is the Yogin Vāmadeva, who is ever engaged in Śaivitc activities and about whom this divine and highly sacred story has come about.

## \|\| 10.18.3 \|\|

*iti śrutvā munīnāntadvacanampremagarbhitam \|*  
*sūtaḥ prāha prasannastāñchivāsaktamanā budhaḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. On hearing these words, pregnant with love, of the sages, the delighted Sūta, the scholar whose mind is attached to Śiva, spoke to them.

## \|\| 10.18.4 \|\|

*sūta uvāca \|*  
*dhanyā yūyaṃ mahādevabhaktā lokopakārakāḥ \|*  
*śṛṇudhvammunayassarve saṃvādaṃ ca tayoḥ punaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. You are all blessed. You are the devotees of lord Śiva You help all the worlds, O sages, you hear further their conversation.

## \|\| 10.18.5 \|\|

*śrutvā maheśatanayavacanaṃ dvaitanāśakam \|*  
*advaitajñānajanakaṃ santuṣṭo'bhūnmahānmuniḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. On hearing the words of Kārttikeya, that quell dualism and produce non-dualistic knowledge the sage became delighted.

## \|\| 10.18.6 \|\|

*natvā stutvā ca vividhaṃ kārtikeyaṃ śivātmajam \|*  
*punaḥ papraccha tattvaṃ hi vinayena mahāmuniḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. After bowing to Kārttikeya, son of Śiva and eulogising him in various ways the great sage asked him again regarding the principles, with great humility.

## \|\| 10.18.7 \|\|

*vāmadeva uvāca \|*  
*bhagavansarvatattvajña ṣaṇmukhāmṛtavāridhe \|*  
*gurutvaṃ kathameteṣāṃ yatīnāmbhāvitātmanām \|\| 7 \|\|*

Vāmadeva said:—

7\. O lord Kārttikeya, O ocean of nectar, O knower of all principles, how can the ascetics of purified souls attain the position of a preceptor?

## \|\| 10.18.8 \|\|

*jīvānāṃ bhogamokṣādisiddhissidhyati yadvaśāt \|*  
*pāramparyyaṃ vinā naiṣā mupadeśādhikāritā \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. They cannot have the authority to instruct without a tradition following which the individual souls attain worldly pleasures and salvation.

## \|\| 10.18.9 \|\|

*evaṃ ca kṣaurakarmāṃgaṃ snānañca kathamīdṛśam \|*  
*iti vijñāpaya svāminsaṃśayaṃ chettumarhasi \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. Similarly how is the ceremonial ablution an ancillary to the rite of shaving? O lord, herein I intimate to you this doubt of mine. It behoves you to clear it.

## \|\| 10.18.10 \|\|

*iti śrutvā kārtikeyo vāmadevavacaḥ smaran \|*  
*śivaṃ śivāṃ ca manasā vyācaṣṭumupacakrame \|\| 10 \|\|*

Subrahmaṇya said:—

10\. On hearing the words of Vāmadeva Kārttikeya began to explain after thinking upon Śiva and Śivā.

## \|\| 10.18.11 \|\|

*śrīsubrahmaṇya uvāca \|*  
*yogapaṭṭampravakṣyāmi gurutvaṃ yena jāyate \|*  
*tava snehādvāmadeva mahadgopyaṃ vimuktidam \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. I shall explain Yogapaṭṭa whereby one attains the state of a preceptor. It is a great secret that yields salvation and O Vāmadeva, it is due to my affection to you that I mention it to you.

## \|\| 10.18.12-15 \|\|

*vaiśākhe śrāvaṇemāsi tathāśvayuji kārtike \|*  
*mārgaśīrṣe ca māghe vā śuklapakṣe śubhe dine \|\| 12 \|\|*

*paṃcamyāṃ paurṇamāsyāṃ vā kṛtaprābhātikakriyaḥ \|*  
*labdhānujñastu guruṇā snātvā niyatamānasaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

*paryyaṃkaśaucaṃ kṛtvā tadvāsasāṃgaṃ pramṛjya ca \|*  
*dviguṇaṃ doramābadhya vāsasī paridhāya ca \|\| 14 \|\|*

*kṣālitāṃghrirdvirācamya bhasma sadyādima ntrataḥ \|*  
*dhārayeddhi samādāya samuddhūlanamārgataḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

12-15. During any of these months Vaiśākha, Śrāvaṇa, Āśvina, Kārttika, Mārgaśīrṣa or Māgha, on an auspicious day in the bright half, either on the fifth or on the full moon day the disciple shall finish his daily morning duties. Taking permission from the preceptor he shall take his bath. With restrained mind he shall sit on his hams. With a cloth he shall wipe off the limbs. He shall wear two cloths after tying the waist-band with twice-spun thread. He shall then wash his feet and perform Ācamana twice. After dusting his body with Bhasma he shall wear Tripuṇḍra marks with Sadyādi mantra.

## \|\| 10.18.16-17 \|\|

*gṛhītahasto guruṇā sānukūlena vai mune \|*  
*sacchiṣyaḥ sāñjalissvābhyāṃ hastābhyāmprāṅmukho yathā \|\| 16 \|\|*

*tathopaveṣṭitastiṣṭhenmaṃḍape samalaṃkṛte \|*  
*gurvāsanavare śuddhe cailājinakuśottare \|\| 17 \|\|*

16-17. O sage, the preceptor shall grasp the disciple with his hands and make him sit facing east on a well decorated platform on a pure excellent seat—a cloth, a deerskin or Darbha grass. The disciple shall be wrapped in the cloth.

## \|\| 10.18.18 \|\|

*atha deśika ādāya śaṃkhaṃ sādhāramastrataḥ \|*  
*viśodhya tasya purataḥ sthāpayetsānukūlataḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Then he shall take a conch with its stand and purify it with Astra mantra. He shall then place it in front of him on a favourable position.

## \|\| 10.18.19 \|\|

*sādhāraṃ śaṅkhamapi ca sampūjya kusumādibhiḥ \|*  
*niḥkṣipeda stravarmabhyāṃ śodhitaṃ tatra sajjalam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. After worshipping the conch with its pedestal with flowers he shall pour pure water into it sanctified by Astra and Varma mantras.

## \|\| 10.18.20 \|\|

*āpūrya pūrvavatpūjya ṣaḍaṃgoktakrameṇa ca \|*  
*praṇavena punastadvai saptadhaivābhimantrayet \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. After filling it, it shall be worshipped again in the manner mentioned in Ṣaḍaṅga Pūjā. Then he shall repeat the Praṇava seven times.

## \|\| 10.18.21 \|\|

*abhyarcya gandhapuṣpādyairdhūpadīpau pradarśya ca \|*  
*saṃrakṣāstreṇa taṃ śaṃkhaṃ varmaṇāthāvaguṇṭhayet \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. He shall worship it with scents and flowers and show lights and incense. He shall then cover up the conch with Astra and Varma mantras.

## \|\| 10.18.22-23 \|\|

*dhenuśaṃkhākhyamudre ca darśayedatha deśikaḥ \|*  
*punassvapurataśśaṃkhaṃ dakṣiṇe deśa uttame \|\| 22 \|\|*

*pūjyārghyoktavidhānena sundarammaṇḍalaṃ śubham \|*  
*kuryyātsampūjayettañca sugandhakusumā dibhiḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

22-23. He shall then show the mystic gestures of the cow and conch. In a pure spot to the south of the conch and in front of him he shall make a beautiful and auspicious Maṇḍala according to the rules of worship and worship it with sweet scents and flowers.

## \|\| 10.18.24-25 \|\|

*sādhāraṃ śodhitaṃ śuddhaṃ ghaṭantantupariṣkṛtam \|*  
*dhūpitaṃ sthāpitaṃ śuddhavāsitodaprapūritam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*pañcatvakpañcapatraiśca mṛttikābhiśca pañcabhiḥ \|*  
*militaṃ ca sugandhena lepayettammunīśvara \|\| 25 \|\|*

24-25. He shall place a pot on its pedestal. It shall be pure and scrubbed well. Threads should be neatly tied round it. It shall be fumigated with incense and filled with pure fragrant water. Five barks, five sprouts and five types of clay shall be mixed with sweet smelling substances and smeared over it, O great sage.

## \|\| 10.18.26 \|\|

*vastrāmradaladūrvāgranārikelasumaistataḥ \|*  
*taṃ ghaṭaṃ vastubhiścānyaissaṃkuryātsamalaṃkṛtam \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. That pot shall be decorated and embellished with cloths, mango leaves, Dūrvā grass, coconut flowers and other articles.

## \|\| 10.18.27 \|\|

*vinyasetpañcaratnāni ghaṭe tatra munīśvara \|*  
*hiraṇyañcāpi teṣāṃ vābhāve bhaktyā pravinyaset \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O great sage, he shall put five gems into the pot or if they are not available he shall put gold into it.

## \|\| 10.18.28 \|\|

*nīlākhyaratnaṃ ca tathā ratne māṇikyahemanī \|*  
*pravālagomedake ca pañcaratnamidaṃ smṛtam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. The five gems are: amethyst, ruby, gold ingot, coral and onyx.

## \|\| 10.18.29 \|\|

*nṛmlaskamiti samprocya glūmityante 'tha deśikaḥ \|*  
*samyagvidhānataḥ prītyā sānukūlaḥ samarcayet \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. After uttering the mystic syllables “Nṛmlaskam” and “Glūm” in the end he shall perform worship in a favourable manner in accordance with the rules.

## \|\| 10.18.30 \|\|

*ādhāraśaktimārabhya yajanoktavidhānataḥ \|*  
*pañcāvaraṇamārgeṇa devamāvāhya pūjayet \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. He shall invoke the lord along the path of five coverings in the manner prescribed for sacrifices, beginning with Ādhāra Śakti and then worship.

## \|\| 10.18.31 \|\|

*nivedya pāyasānnañca tāṃbūlādi yathā purā \|*  
*nāmāṣṭakārcanāntaṃ ca kṛtvā tamabhimantrayet \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. He shall offer milk pudding and cooked rice as Naivedya and the betel leaves as before. After the adoration with the repetitions of eight names, the other mantras shall be chanted.

## \|\| 10.18.32-33 \|\|

*praṇavāṣṭottaraśataṃ brahmabhiḥ pañcabhiḥ kramāt \|*  
*sadyādīśāntamapyastraṃ rakṣitaṃ varmaṇā punaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

*avaguṃṭhya pradarśyātha dhūpadīpau ca bhaktitaḥ \|*  
*dhenuyonyākhyamudre ca samyaktatra pradarśayet \|\| 33 \|\|*

32-33. Praṇava shall be repeated hundred and eight times. The five Brahmans beginning with Sadyojāta and ending with Īśāna shall be meditated upon. It shall be covered with Astra and Varma mantras. Incense and lights shall be waved with devotion. He shall then show the mystic gestures of cow and the vaginal passage clearly.

## \|\| 10.18.34 \|\|

*tataśca deśikastasya darbhairācchādya mastake \|*  
*maṇḍalastheśadigbhāge caturasraṃ prakalpayet \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. The preceptor shall then cover the vessel on the top with the Darbha grass. He shall then make a square to the northeast of the Maṇḍala.

## \|\| 10.18.35 \|\|

*taduparyyāsanaṃ ramyaṃ kalpayitvā vidhānataḥ \|*  
*tatra saṃsthāpayecchiṣyaṃ śiśuṃ sānukūlataḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. Then a beautiful seat shall be put over the square in accordance with the rules. He shall then make the boy disciple sit on it in a comfortable posture.

## \|\| 10.18.36 \|\|

*tataḥ kumbhaṃ samutthāya svastivācanapūrvakam \|*  
*abhiṣiṃcedguruḥ śiṣyaṃ prādakṣiṇyena mastake \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The preceptor shall raise the vessel. Repeating “Svasti” he shall pour water on the head of the disciple making a circle with the inverted pot.

## \|\| 10.18.37 \|\|

*praṇavaṃ pūrvamuccāryya saptadhā brahmabhi stataḥ \|*  
*pañcabhiścābhiṣekānte śaṃkhodenābhiveṣṭayet \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. After uttering the Praṇava once first and then seven times with the names of five Brahmans he shall encircle him at the end of the ablution with the water from the conch.

## \|\| 10.18.38 \|\|

*cārudīpaṃ pradarśyātha vāsasā parimṛjya ca \|*  
*nūtanaṃ dorakaupīnaṃ vāsasī paridhāpayet \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. After the rite of waving the charming light he shall wipe him with a towel. The disciple shall be made to wear a new waistband, a loin-cloth and two wearing cloths.

## \|\| 10.18.39-40 \|\|

*kṣālitāṃghrirdvirācamya dhṛtabhasmaguruśśiśum \|*  
*hastābhyāmavalaṃbyātha hastau maṃḍapamadhyataḥ \|\| 39 \|\|*

*tadaṃgeṣu samālipya tadbhasma vidhinā guruḥ \|*  
*āsane saṃpraveśyātha kalpite sthāpayetsukham \|\| 40 \|\|*

39-40. The feet shall be washed and Ācamana performed twice. Holding the ashes in his hands the preceptor shall smear it over the body of the disciple who is led into the Maṇḍapa and comfortably seated.

## \|\| 10.18.41 \|\|

*pūrvābhimukhamātmīyatattvajñānābhilāṣiṇam \|*  
*svasanastho gururbrūyādamalātmā bhaveti tam \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. The preceptor shall be seated with the disciple facing the cast and desirous of spiritual knowledge. The preceptor shall tell him—“Be pure in soul.”

## \|\| 10.18.42-43 \|\|

*guruśca paripūrṇo'smi śiva ityacalasthitiḥ \|*  
*samādhimācaretsamyaṅmuhūrttaṃ gūḍhamānasaḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*paścādunmīlya nayane sānukūlena cetasā \|*  
*sāṃjaliṃ saṃsthitaṃ śuddhaṃ paśyecchiṣyamanākulaḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. He shall sit steady in a trance for a short while thinking “I am the preceptor—Śiva. He shall open his eyes with the mind favourably disposed towards him and without excitement glance at the pupil who is sitting with palms joined in reverence.

## \|\| 10.18.44 \|\|

*svahastambhasitāliptaṃ vinyasya śiśumastake \|*  
*dakṣaśrutāvupadiśeddhaṃsassohamiti sphuṭam \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. With his hand smeared with the ashes he shall touch the head of the pupil and instruct in his right ear clearly “Haṃsaḥ Soham.”

## \|\| 10.18.45 \|\|

*tatrādyāhaṃpadasyārthaḥ śaktayātmā sa śivassvayam \|*  
*sa evāhaṃ śivosmīti svātmānaṃ saṃvibhāvaya \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. “The first Ham means Śakti-Soul. Saḥ means Śiva. I am he alone.” Thus he shall reveal the meaning.

## \|\| 10.18.46-47 \|\|

*ya ityaṇorarthatattvamupadiśya tato vadet \|*  
*avāṃtarāṇāṃ vākyānāmarthatātparyamādarāt \|\| 46 \|\|*

*vākyāni vacmi te brahmansāvadhānamatiśśṛṇu \|*  
*tāni dhāraya citte hi sa brūyāditi saṃsphuṭam \|\| 47 \|\|*

46-47. After instructing him in the meaning of “Yaḥ Aṇu’ he shall express clearly the meaning and purport of the statements in the middle. He shall tell him clearly, “O Brahmin, I shall tell you the statements. Listen and retain them in your mind.”

## Chapter 19 - The rules of Yogapaṭṭa

## \|\| 10.19.1 \|\|

*prajñānaṃ brahmavākyārthaḥ pūrvameva prabodhitaḥ \|*  
*ahaṃpadasyārthabhūtaḥ śaktyātmā parameśvaraḥ \|\| 1 \|\|*

Subrahmaṇya said:—

Now the great statements: perfect knowledge is Brahman; I am Brahman; thou art that. This Ātman is Brahman. All this is pervaded by the lord. I am the vital breath. Ātman is perfect knowledge; what is here is there; what is there is here. It is other than what is known; verily it is other than what is unknown too. This is your soul, the immanent and the deathless one. He who is in this Puruṣa and he who is in the sun both are the same. I am the great Brahman, the greatest, greater than the greatest. I am myself (the Brahman) characterised by bliss, since I am the master of the Vedas and the Śāstras. Brahman is stationed in all living beings. Undoubtedly I am that alone. I am the vital breath of the elements: of the earth, of the waters, of the fire, of the wind, of the ether and of the three Guṇas. I am all. I am the Ātman of all. I transmigrate. I am without a second because I have everything in my Ātman—past, present and future. Indeed all this is Brahman. I am all. I am the liberated. He who is this is I; I am he; I am Haṃsa; I am he. This shall be meditated upon always and everywhere.

1\. The meaning of the statement—“Perfect knowledge is Brahman” has already been made known. Parameśvara the Śakti-soul is the meaning of the word Aham.

## \|\| 10.19.2 \|\|

*akāraḥ sarvavarṇāgryaḥ prakāśaḥ paramaḥ śivaḥ \|*  
*hakāro vyomarūpaḥ syācchaktyātmā saṃprakīrtitaḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. ‘A’ is the foremost among all letters. It is the greatest splendour ‘Śiva’. The letter ‘Ha’ is of the form of firmament. It is glorified as the Śakti-soul.

## \|\| 10.19.3 \|\|

*śivaśaktyostu saṃyogādānandaḥ satatoditaḥ \|*  
*brahmeti śivaśaktyostu sarvātmatvamiti sphuṭam \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. Bliss is ever-rising due to the union of Śiva and Śakti. The word Brahman indicates the state of being Ātman of all, of Śiva and Śakti.

## \|\| 10.19.4 \|\|

*pūrvamevopadiṣṭaṃ tatsohamasmīti bhāvayet \|*  
*tattvamityatra taditi saśabdārthaḥ prabodhitaḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. That has been taught before. The devotee shall meditate “I am he” “That you are;” in this the meaning of “That” has already been explained.

## \|\| 10.19.5-6 \|\|

*anyathā so'hamityatra viparītārthabhāvanā \|*  
*ahaṃśabdastu puruṣastaditi syānnapuṃsakam \|*  
*evamanyonyavairudhyādanvayo nabhavettayoḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

*strīpuṃrūpasya jagataḥ kāraṇaṃ cānyathā bhavet \|*  
*sa tattvamasi ityevamupadeśārthabhāvanā \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. Otherwise there is a likelihood of opposite conception in “I am he” whereas the word “I” is in the masculine, the word “That” is in the neuter. Hence due to the incompatibility they cannot be interpreted together. The cause of the universe, of male and female forms, cannot be otherwise. The concept of the meaning of the instruction is: “Thou art that.”

## \|\| 10.19.7 \|\|

*ayamātmeti vākye ca puṃrūpaṃ padayugmakam \|*  
*īśena rakṣaṇīyatvādīśāvasyamidaṃ jagat \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. In the statement “This Ātman is Brahman” both the words are in the masculine. All this worthy of protection is pervaded by the lord.

## \|\| 10.19.8-9 \|\|

*prajñānātmā yadeveha tadamutreti cintayet \|*  
*yaḥ sa eveti vidvadbhissiddhāntibhirihocyate \|\| 8 \|\|*

*uparisthitavākye ca yo'mutra sa iha sthitaḥ \|*  
*iti pūrvavadevārthaḥ puruṣo viduṣāṃ mataḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

8-9. “The Ātman is the perfect knowledge: what is here is there; he who is here is there.” The concept is very well interpreted by the scholars.

## \|\| 10.19.10-12 \|\|

*anyadeva tadviditādatho \|\| aviditādapi \|*  
*asminvākye phalasyāpi vaiparītyavibhāvanā \|\| 10 \|\|*

*yathāsyāttadvadevātra vakṣyāmi śrūyatāṃ mune \|*  
*ayathāviditāchabdo pūrvavadviditāditi \|\| 11 \|\|*

*pravṛttissyāttadviditāttathaivāviditātparam \|*  
*anyadeva hi saṃsiddhyai na bhavediti niścitam \|\| 12 \|\|*

10-12. In the statement “It is other than what is known and what is unknown”, there is a likelihood of adverse interpretation and conception in regard to the fruit of salvation. I shall explain, O sage, how this misinterpretation shall be prevented. “What is known” means “what is known not properly.” What is unknown” means “What is not known before.” Otherwise the expression may not be conducive to any meaning.

## \|\| 10.19.13-14 \|\|

*eṣa ta ātmāṃtaryāmī yo'mṛtaśca śivassvayam \|*  
*yaścāyampuruṣe śaṃbhuryaścāditye vyavasthitaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

*sa cā'sau seti pārthakyaṃ naikaṃ sarvaṃ sa īritaḥ \|*  
*sopādhidvayamasyārtha upacārāttathocyate \|\| 14 \|\|*

13-14. “This is your soul, the immanent, and the immortal one.” It is Śiva himself, who is in the Puruṣa, or in the sun. Here the two conditions indicate that there is no separateness in the lord.

## \|\| 10.19.15-17 \|\|

*taṃ śambhunāthaṃ śrutayo vadanti hi hiraṇmayam \|*  
*hiraṇya bāhava iti sarvāṃgasyo palakṣalam \|\| 15 \|\|*

*anyathā tatpatitvaṃ tu na bhavediti yatnataḥ \|*  
*ya eṣontariti śaṃbhuśchāndogye śrūyate śivaḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

*hiraṇyaśmaśruvāṃstadvaddhiraṇyamayakeśavān \|*  
*nakhamārabhya keśāgrā sarvatrāpi hiraṇmayaḥ \|\| 17 \|\|*

15-17. Verily the Vedas mention lord Śiva as golden. “Golden-armed” refers to his limbs being golden. Otherwise the lordship cannot be his. In the Chāndogya also in the passage “He who is in Śiva” is mentioned with golden moustache, golden tresses. Everywhere from the nail till the hair he is golden.

## \|\| 10.19.18 \|\|

*ahamasmi paraṃ brahma parāparaparātparam \|*  
*iti vākyasya tātparyaṃ vadāmi śrūyatāmidam \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. I shall tell you me purport of the statement “I am the great Brahman”. Please listen.

## \|\| 10.19.19 \|\|

*ahaṃpadasyārthabhūtaḥ śaktyātmā śivaīritaḥ \|*  
*sa evāsmīti vākyārtha yojanā bhavati dhruvam \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. The meaning of ‘Aham’ is Śakti-soul. Śiva is indicated thereby. “I am he alone”. The statement can be construed thus.

## \|\| 10.19.20-21 \|\|

*sarvotkṛṣṭaśca sarvātmā parabrahma sa īritaḥ \|*  
*paraścāthāparaśceti parātparamiti tridhā \|\| 20 \|\|*

*rudro brahmā ca viṣṇuśca proktāḥ śrutyaiva nānyathā \|*  
*tebhyaśca paramo devaḥ paraśabdena bodhitaḥ \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. He is mentioned as the great Brahman, most sublime and the soul of all: “Para, Apara, Parātpara.” The words Rudra, Brahmā and Viṣṇu are mentioned in the Vedas. The lord is greater than these. This is indicated by the word ‘Para’.

## \|\| 10.19.22 \|\|

*vedaśāstra gurūṇāṃ ca vākyābhyā savaśācchiśoḥ \|*  
*pūrṇānandamayaśśaṃbhuḥ prādurbhūto bhaveddhṛdi \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. By pondering over and practising the words of the Vedas, Śāstras and the preceptor, Śiva, the blissful lord appears in the heart of the pupil.

## \|\| 10.19.23 \|\|

*sarvabhūtasthitaśśambhussa evāhaṃ na saṃśayaḥ \|*  
*tattvajātasya sarvasya prāṇosmyahamahaṃ śivaḥ \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Śiva is stationed in all living beings. Undoubtedly I am he. I am the vital breath of all the Taitvas. I am Śiva.

## \|\| 10.19.24-26 \|\|

*ityuktvā punarapyāha śivastattvatrayasya ca \|*  
*prāṇosmītyatra pṛthvyādiguṇāntagrahaṇānmune \|\| 24 \|\|*

*ātmatattvāni sarvāṇi gṛhītānīti bhāvaya \|*  
*punaśca sarvagrahaṇaṃ vidyātattvaśivātmanoḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

*tattvayoścāsmyahaṃ prāṇāssarvasssarvātmako hyaham \|*  
*jīvasya cāntaryāmitvājjīvo'haṃ tasya sarvadā \|\| 26 \|\|*

24-26. After mentioning the Tattvas the Śāstras mention the Tattvas and Guṇas severally. The statement “I am the vital breath of all”, O sage, includes the principles in entirety. By saying “I am all” he means “I am the vital breath of the Vidyātattva and the Tattvas of Śiva and Ātman”. Hence “I am the Ātman of all”. Since the individual soul is immanent as well, I am his Jīva always.

## \|\| 10.19.27 \|\|

*yadbhūtaṃ yacca bhavyaṃ yadbhavipyatsarvameva ca \|*  
*manmayatvādahaṃ sarvaḥ sarvo vai rudra ityapi \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. What is past, what is yet to be, what is present-all these are identical with me. Hence I am all. All is Rudra.

## \|\| 10.19.28 \|\|

*śrutirāha mune sā hi sākṣācchivamukhodgatā \|*  
*sarvātmā paramairebhirguṇairnityasamanvayāt \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. O sage, the Śruti says that it has originated from Śiva’s mouth. “I am the soul of all”, because the great qualities are ever present in me.

## \|\| 10.19.29 \|\|

*svasmātparātmavirahādadvitīyo'hameva hi \|*  
*sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahmeti vākyārthaḥ pūrvvamīritaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. “I am without a second” because there is no other Ātman distinct from the self. The statement “indeed all this is Brahman” has been explained before.

## \|\| 10.19.30 \|\|

*pūrṇo'haṃ bhāvarūpatvānnityamukto'hameva hi \|*  
*paśavo matprasādena muktā madbhāvamāśritāḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. I am complete because of my positive aspect. I am ever liberated. All the souls resorting to my nature are liberated due to my grace.

## \|\| 10.19.31 \|\|

*yo'sau sarvātmakaśśambhusso'haṃ sa śivo'smyaham \|*  
*iti vai sarvavākyārtho vāmadeva śivoditaḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. “I am he who is Śiva, the soul of all and identical with all. I am Haṃsa. I am Śiva.” This is the meaning of all statements, O Vāmadeva, as mentioned by Śiva.

## \|\| 10.19.32 \|\|

*itīśaśrutivākyābhyāmupadiṣṭārthamādarāt \|*  
*sākṣācchivaikyadaṃ puṃsāṃ śiśogururupādiśet \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The preceptor shall communicate to the pupil the meaning as declared by Śiva and explained in the Vedīc passages. It establishes the identity of men with Śiva Himself.

## \|\| 10.19.33 \|\|

*ādāya śaṃkhaṃ sādhāramastramantreṇa bhasmanā \|*  
*śodhya tatpuratassthāpya caturasre samarcite \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. He shall then take the conch with the pedestal and purify it with Bhasma and the Astra mantra. He shall put it before the disciple on the square and worship it.

## \|\| 10.19.34 \|\|

*omityabhyarcya gandhādyairastraṃ vastropaśobhitam \|*  
*vāsitaṃ jalamāpūrya sampūjyomiti mantrataḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. He shall worship it with scents repeating Om. It shall be filled with scented water invoked by Astra mantra and filtered with a cloth. It shall be worshipped with the Praṇava Om.

## \|\| 10.19.35-36 \|\|

*saptadhaivābhimaṃtryātha praṇavena punaśca tam \|*  
*yastvantaraṃ kiṃcidasti kurute tyatibhītibhāk \|\| 35 \|\|*

*ityāha śrutisattattvaṃ dṛḍhātmā gatabhīrbhava \|*  
*ityābhāṣya svayaṃ śiṣyaṃ devaṃ dhyāyansamarcayet \|\| 36 \|\|*

35-36. It shall be worshipped repeating Om seven times. He who makes the slightest slip has to face the greatest terror. So says the Śruti. He shall then address the disciple himself, “Be steady-minded and fearless”. Meditating on the lord he shall continue the worship.

## \|\| 10.19.37 \|\|

*śiṣyāsanaṃ samprapūjya ṣaḍutthāpanamārgataḥ \|*  
*śivāsanaṃ ca saṃkalpya śivamūrtiṃ prakalpayet \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. He shall worship the seat, of the disciple with the rite of “Ṣaḍutthāpana.” After conceiving Śiva’s seat he shall install an idol of Śiva.

## \|\| 10.19.38-39 \|\|

*pañca brahmāṇi vinyasya śiraḥ pādāvasānakam \|*  
*muṇḍavatkrakalābhedaiḥ praṇavasya kalā api \|\| 38 \|\|*

*aṣṭatriṃśanmaṃtrarūpā śśiṣyadehe'tha mastake \|*  
*samāvāhya śivaṃ mudrāḥ sthāpanīyāḥ pradarśayet \|\| 39 \|\|*

38-39. He shall fix on the head and the body of the disciple, the five Brahmans, beginning with the head and ending with the feet. He shall also fix the Kalās of the Praṇava too in the forms of the thirty-eight mantras as distinguished by the trunk, face and Kalās. After invoking Śiva he shall show the mystic gestures of the Kalās.

## \|\| 10.19.40 \|\|

*tataścāṅgāni vinyasya sarvajñānītyanukramāt \|*  
*kalpayedupacārāṃśca ṣoḍaśāsanapūrvakān \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. He shall then perform the Nyāsa of the limbs. The sixteen forms of ancillary services beginning with the offering of the seat shall also be performed.

## \|\| 10.19.41 \|\|

*pāyasānnañca naivedyaṃ samarpyo magnijāyayā \|*  
*gaṇḍūṣācamanārghyādi dhūpadīpādikaṃ kramāt \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Milk pudding shall be offered as Naivedya repeating Om Svāhā. The rites of offering water for gargling and Ācamana, Arghya, incense, lights etc. shall also be performed.

## \|\| 10.19.42-45 \|\|

*nāmāṣṭakena sampūjya brāhmaṇairvedapāragaiḥ \|*  
*japedbrahmavidāpnoti bhṛgurvai vāruṇistataḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*yo devānāmupakramyaḥ yaḥ paraḥ sa maheśvaraḥ \|*  
*ityaṃtaṃ tasya purataḥ kahlārādivirnitām \|\| 43 \|\|*

*ādāya mālāmutthāya śrīvirūpākṣa nirmite \|*  
*śāstre paṃcāśike rūpe siddhiskandhaṃ japecchanaiḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

*khyātiḥ pūrṇohamityaṃtaṃ sānukūlena cetasā \|*  
*deśikastasya śiṣyasya kaṇṭhadeśe samarpayet \|\| 45 \|\|*

42-45. After worshipping with the eight names he shall perform the Japa along with brahmins well-versed in the Vedas. Passages from the Śrutis: “The knower of Brahman attains.” “Bhṛgu the son of Varuṇa” “He who of gods” and ending with “He who is the greatest is Maheśvara” shall be recited. Taking a garland of Kalhāra flowers he shall stand up and recite the “Siddhi Skandha” section in the Pañcāśika Text of Śrīvirūpākṣa. Thereafter thinking thus “This is my fame. I am complete” and feeling favourably disposed towards him the preceptor shall put the garland round the neck of the disciple.

## \|\| 10.19.46 \|\|

*tilakaṃ vandanenātha sarvāṅgālepanaṃ punaḥ \|*  
*svasampradāyānuguṇaṃ kārayecca yathāvidhi \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. The caste mark on the forehead and the application of the unguent over the body shall be carried out in accordance with his cultural custom.

## \|\| 10.19.47-49 \|\|

*tataśca deśikaḥ prītyā nāmaśrīpādasaṃjñitam \|*  
*chatrañca pādukāṃ dadyāddūrvākalpavikalpanam \|\| 47 \|\|*

*vyākhyātatvañca karmmādigurvāsanaparigraham \|*  
*anugṛhya gurustasmai śiṣyāya śivarūpiṇe \|\| 48 \|\|*

*śivohamasmīti sadā samādhistho bhaveti tam \|*  
*samprocyātha svayaṃ tasmai namaskāraṃ samācaret \|\| 49 \|\|*

47-49. The preceptor shall assign a name to him prefixed with the title Śripāda. He shall offer him an umbrella and sandals. He shall delegate to him the power of oratory, authorise him to accept scat in front since the disciple has now become Śiva himself. He shall tell him to be conscious that he is Śiva. The preceptor shall make obeisance in accordance with the tradition in the cult.

## \|\| 10.19.50 \|\|

*sampradāyānuguṇyena namaskuryustathāpare \|*  
*śiṣyastadā samutthāya namaskuryādguruntathā \|*  
*gurorapi guruṃ tasya śiṣyāṃśca svagurorapi \|\| 50 \|\|*

50\. The disciple shall then stand up and prostrate before his preceptor, the preceptor’s preceptor and the other disciples of his preceptor.

## \|\| 10.19.51 \|\|

*evaṃ kṛtanamaskāraṃ śiṣyandadyādguruḥ svayam \|*  
*suśīlaṃ yatavācaṃ taṃ vinayāvanataṃ sthitam \|\| 51 \|\|*

51\. When the disciple has finished the customary obeisance he shall stand humbly and silently like a well behaved disciple. The preceptor shall tell him like this.

## \|\| 10.19.52 \|\|

*adyaprabhṛti lokānāmanugrahaparo bhava \|*  
*parīkṣya vatsaraṃ śiṣyamaṃgīkuru vidhānataḥ \|\| 52 \|\|*

52\. “From now onwards be engaged in blessing the worlds. Accept a disciple only after testing him for a year. Accept him duly.

## \|\| 10.19.53 \|\|

*rāgādidoṣānsaṃtyajya śivadhyānaparo bhava \|*  
*satsampradāyasaṃsiddhaissaṃgaṃ kuru na cetaraiḥ \|\| 53 \|\|*

53\. Eschew the faults of lust etc. and be devoted to meditation on Śiva. Associate with those who have inherited good tradition and not with others.

## \|\| 10.19.54 \|\|

*anabhyarcya śivaṃ jātumā bhuṃkṣvāprāṇa saṃkṣayam \|*  
*gurubhaktiṃ samāsthāya sukhī bhava sukhī bhava \|\| 54 \|\|*

54\. Till the end of your life never take food without worshipping Śiva. Abiding by your devotion to the preceptor be happy, be happy.”

## \|\| 10.19.55 \|\|

*iti kramādguruvaro dayālurjñānasāgaraḥ \|*  
*sānukūlena cittena samaṃ śiṣyaṃ samācaret \|\| 55 \|\|*

55\. In this manner the excellent preceptor, sympathetic and scholarly, shall be favourably inclined towards the disciple.

## \|\| 10.19.56 \|\|

*tava snehānmayāyaṃ vai vāmadeva munīśvara \|*  
*yogapaṭṭaprakāraste prokto guhyataro'pi hi \|\| 56 \|\|*

56\. O Vāmadeva, O great sage, out of affection for you the mode of the consecration of the disciple has been mentioned to you though it is a great secret.

## \|\| 10.19.57 \|\|

*ityuktvā ṣaṇmukhastasmai kṣaurasnānavidhikramam \|*  
*vaktumārabhata prītyā yatīnāṃ kṛpayā śubham \|\| 57 \|\|*

57\. After saying this Kārttikeya narrated the procedure of hair-cutting and ablution in detail intending to favour the ascetics (anxious to hear him).

## Chapter 20 - Rules for hair-cutting and ablution

## \|\| 10.20.1 \|\|

*subrahmaṇya uvāca \|*  
*kṣaurasnānavidhiṃ vakṣye vāmadeva mahāmune \|*  
*yasya sadyo vidhānena śuddhissyādyatinaḥ parā \|\| 1 \|\|*

Subrahmaṇya said:-

1\. O sage Vāmadeva I shall now explain the rules of hair-cutting and ablution. These if performed immediately are conducive to great purity of the ascetic.

## \|\| 10.20.2 \|\|

*yogapaṭṭaprakārasya vidhimprāpya munīśvara \|*  
*sa śiṣyassyādvratī pūrṇaḥ kṣaurakarmmodyato bhavet \|\| 2 \|\|*

2\. O sage, after the consecration as a disciple he shall observe the other rites and get ready for the tonsorial ceremony.

## \|\| 10.20.3 \|\|

*guruṃ natvā viśeṣeṇa labdhānujñastato guroḥ \|*  
*śirassaṃkṣālya cācamya savāsāḥ kṣauramācaret \|\| 3 \|\|*

3\. He shall bow to the preceptor and secure permission from him. After washing the head and performing the Ācamana he shall have the tonsure while putting on cloth.

## \|\| 10.20.4 \|\|

*kṣālayedvasanaṃ paścānmṛdambhobhiḥ kṣurādikam \|*  
*taddhastau ca mṛdālipya kṣālayeti mṛdaṃ dadet \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. After that he shall wash the cloth, the razor etc. with water and clay. The barber also shall be given clay and asked to wash his hands.

## \|\| 10.20.5-6 \|\|

*sthāpitamprokṣitantoyaiśśivaṃ śivamitīrayan \|*  
*svanetre pihite caivānāmāṃguṣṭhābhimaṃtrite \|\| 5 \|\|*

*astreṇonmīlya saṃdṛśya kṣurā dikṣau rasādhanam \|*  
*abhimantrya dvādaśātha prokṣayedastramaṃtrataḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

5-6. Repeating “Śivam” “Śivam” he shall sprinkle every object with water. He shall then close his eyes with the ring-finger and the thumb. Repeating the Astra mantra he shall open his eyes and look at the razor and other implements of tonsorial rite. Repeating the mantra twelve times he shall sprinkle them with the Astra mantra.

## \|\| 10.20.7 \|\|

*kṣuraṃ gṛhītvā tāreṇa dakṣabhāge nikṛntayet \|*  
*keśāṃśca kāṃści dagreṣu vaptvā sarvaṃ ca vāpayet \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. He shall take the razor and cut off a patch of hair on the right side. Then a few hair on the front, then the entire hair shall be shaved off.

## \|\| 10.20.8 \|\|

*pṛthivyāṃ parṇamādāya vikṣipenna bhuvaḥ sthale \|*  
*śmaśrūṇi hastapādasthanakhāni ca nikṛṃtayet \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. The hair shaven off shall be put on a leaf and not allowed to fall on the ground. The beards, the moustache also shall be shaven. Nails of the hands and feet shall be pared.

## \|\| 10.20.9-10 \|\|

*bilvāśvatthatulasyādisthāne saṃgṛhya mṛtikām \|*  
*dviṣaṭvāraṃ nimajyāpsu tīraṃ gatvopaviśya ca \|\| 9 \|\|*

*śuddhe deśe tu saṃsthāpya mṛdaṃ tredhā vibhajya ca \|*  
*evampunastridhā kṛtvā prokṣyāstreṇābhimantrayet \|\| 10 \|\|*

9-10. He shall take clay from the root of Bilva, Aśvattha or Tulasi. He shall plunge into water twelve times and come to the bank. He shall place the clay on a pure spot of ground and divide it into three parts. Each part shall be divided into three. It shall be sprinkled with water with the Astra mantra.

## \|\| 10.20.11 \|\|

*tatraikāṃ mṛdamādāya dāpayitvānyapāṇinā \|*  
*karau dvādaśadhā lipya pratyekaṃ kena kṣālayet \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. Taking a lump of clay he shall wipe off his hands with it twelve times. Each of the hands shall be separately washed with water.

## \|\| 10.20.12 \|\|

*punarekāmpādayośca mukhe cānyāṃ kare kramāt \|*  
*saṃlipyākṣālya cāmbhobhiḥ punaśca jalamāviśet \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. Another lump of clay shall be used similarly for the feet, another lump for the face, another for the arms and so on. After smearing and washing he shall enter water again.

## \|\| 10.20.13 \|\|

*anyāṃ mṛdambhāgayitvā śirasi dvādaśa kramāt \|*  
*ālipya mṛdamāsyāntanimajya ca punaḥ punaḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Another lump of clay shall be divided into twelve and applied over the beard. He shall then plunge into the water again.

## \|\| 10.20.14 \|\|

*tīraṃ gatvā tu gaṃḍūṣānṣoḍaśācamanaṃ dvidhā \|*  
*prāṇānāyamya ca punaḥ praṇavaṃ dvyaṣṭasaṃkhyayā \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. He shall come to the bank, gargle sixteen times, perform Ācamana twice and Prāṇāyāma sixteen times repeating the Praṇava.

## \|\| 10.20.15 \|\|

*mṛdamanyāṃ punastredhā vibhajya ca tadekayā \|*  
*kaṭiśaucaṃ pādaśaucaṃ vidhāyācamya ca dvidhā \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. He shall divide another lump of clay into three. With one part he shall purify the hips and feet and perform Ācamana twice.

## \|\| 10.20.16 \|\|

*praṇavenātha ṣoḍaśa prāṇānāyamya vāgyataḥ \|*  
*punaranyāṃ svorudeśe tridhā vinyasya comiti \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. Repeating the Praṇava he shall perform Prāṇāyāma silently sixteen times. He shall take another part of the clay lump and apply it on his thighs thrice repeating “Om”.

## \|\| 10.20.17 \|\|

*prokṣyābhimaṃtrayetsapta svapāṇyostalamekadhā \|*  
*tridhālipyātha sampaśyetsūryyamūrtiṃ ca pāvanīm \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. He shall sprinkle water over it repeating Om seven times. He shall apply the clay on the palms once in the beginning and thrice afterwards. Then he shall glance at the holy disc of the sun.

## \|\| 10.20.18-19 \|\|

*svakakṣayoḥ samālipya vyatyastābhyāmathānyayā \|*  
*pāṇibhyāñca mṛdā śiṣyassumatirdṛḍhamānasaḥ \|\| 18 \|\|*

*gṛhītvānyāṃ mṛdaṃ śuddhāṃ tathāsau gurubhaktimān \|*  
*śira ārabhya pādāntaṃ vilipyādityadṛṣṭayā \|\| 19 \|\|*

18-19. With another lump of clay he shall purify the armpits by means of the hands on the opposite sides. With steady and pure mind the disciple shall take another lump of pure clay and smear his body from head to foot with devotion to the preceptor and eyes fixed in the sun.

## \|\| 10.20.20 \|\|

*samutthāya tato'sau vai daṇḍamādāya bhūtale \|*  
*svaguruṃ mantradambhaktyā saṃsmarejjñānaniṣṭhayā \|\| 20 \|\|*

20\. Fixing the staff on the ground he shall stand up and remember his preceptor who has taught him the mantra with devotion strengthened by wisdom.

## \|\| 10.20.21 \|\|

*tatassāmbaṃ maheśānaṃ śaṃkaraṃ candraśekharam \|*  
*saṃsmaredbhaktitaśśiṣya sarveśvaryapatiṃ śivam \|\| 21 \|\|*

21\. He shall devoutly remember Sāmba, Maheśāna, Śaṅkara, the moon-crested Śiva, the lord of prosperity and glory.

## \|\| 10.20.22 \|\|

*trivārampraṇametprītyā sāṣṭāṃgaṃ ca guru śivam \|*  
*pañcāṅgenaikavārañca samutthāya ca vandayet \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. He shall prostrate before the preceptor Śiva thrice with eight limbs and once with five limbs touching the ground and stand up making obeisance.

## \|\| 10.20.23 \|\|

*tīrthaṃ praviśya tanmadhye nimajyonmajya tāmmṛdam \|*  
*skandhe saṃsthāpya pūrvoktaprakāreṇa vilepayet \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. He shall enter the river again and plunge once. Coming up he shall apply the clay on the shoulders in the manner mentioned before.

## \|\| 10.20.24 \|\|

*tatrāvaśiṣṭaṃ saṃgṛhya jalamadhye praviśya ca \|*  
*viloḍya samyak tāṃ tatra sarvāṃgeṣu vilipya ca \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. The remaining clay he shall take up and dissolve it in the water and then apply it all over his body.

## \|\| 10.20.25 \|\|

*trivāramomiti procya śivapādāmbujaṃ smaran \|*  
*saṃsārāmbudhisaṃtāraṃ sadā yadvidhito hi saḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. He shall repeat ‘Om’ thrice and remember the lotus-like feet of Śiva which enable him to cross the ocean of worldly existence.

## \|\| 10.20.26 \|\|

*abhiṣicyomiti jalaṃ virajābhasmalolitam \|*  
*aṃgopamārjjanaṃ kṛtvā susnāyādbhasmanā tataḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Repeating Om he shall pour water over his body besmeared with the Bhasma of the Virajā Homa. He shall wipe the body, take his bath and apply Dharma.

## \|\| 10.20.27 \|\|

*tripuṃḍraṃ ca vidhāyātha yathoktavidhinā śubham \|*  
*yathoktāṃgeṣu sarveṣu sāvadhāna tayā mune \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. O sage, then he shall carefully apply Tripuṇḍra in accordance with the rules.

## \|\| 10.20.28 \|\|

*tataśśuddhamanā bhūtvā kuryyānmadhyaṃdinakriyāḥ \|*  
*maheśvaraṃ namaskṛtya gurūṃstīrthādikāni ca \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. After paying obeisance to Maheśvara, the preceptors, holy centres and others he shall perform the midday rites.

## \|\| 10.20.29 \|\|

*sampūjayenmaheśānaṃ bhaktyā paramayā mune \|*  
*sāmbikaṃ jñānadātāraṃ pātāraṃ tribhavasya vai \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. O sage, with great devotion he shall worship Śiva accompanied by Śivā and the bestower of perfect knowledge, the protector of the three worlds.

## \|\| 10.20.30 \|\|

*tatosau dṛḍhacetasko yatiḥ svavṛṣasaṃsthitaḥ \|*  
*bhikṣārthampravrajecchuddho vipravargeṣu sādhuṣu \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. With steady mind, abiding by his virtuous duties and pure in body, he shall go to good brahmin householders for alms.

## \|\| 10.20.31 \|\|

*tatastatra ca śuddhātmā pañcadhā parikalpitam \|*  
*bhaikṣyaṃ yathocitaṃ kuryyāddūṣitānnaṃ vivarjjayet \|\| 31 \|\|*

31\. He shall divide the alms into five parts and do with them as proper. He shall avoid defiled food.

## \|\| 10.20.32 \|\|

*śaucaṃ snānaṃ tathā bhikṣāṃ nityamekāntasevanam \|*  
*bhikṣauścatvāri karmmāṇi pañcamaṃ naiva vidyate \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. A mendicant shall perform four activities: Purificatory rites, ablution, begging for alms and resorting to isolated places for meditation. He has no fifth rite.

## \|\| 10.20.33 \|\|

*alābuṃ veṇupātraṃ ca dāravammṛṇmayantathā \|*  
*bhikṣoścattvāri pātrāṇi pañcamannaiva vidyate \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. He shall use only four types of begging bowls, that made of dried gourd, or of bamboo, or of wood or of clay. He shall not take any other type of bowl.

## \|\| 10.20.34 \|\|

*tāmbūlaṃ taijasampātraṃ retassekaṃ sitāṃbaram \|*  
*divāsvāpo hi naktānnaṃ yatīnāṃ ṣaḍvivarjitāḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. Six things are forbidden to the ascetics: chewing the betel, using the metallic vessel, emission of semen, white cloth, sleeping during the day and taking meal at night.

## \|\| 10.20.35 \|\|

*sākṣarā viparītāśca rākṣasāsta iti smṛtāḥ \|*  
*tasmādvai viparītaṃ ca karmma naivācaredyatiḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. If they observe the rules they are wise. If they do not they are Rākṣasas. The ascetic shall never indulge in activities contrary to the rules.

## \|\| 10.20.36 \|\|

*yatiḥ prayatnataḥ kuryyātkṣaurasnānaṃ ca śuddhaye \|*  
*saṃsmaranmanasā śuddhaṃ paraṃ brahma sadāśivam \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. He shall perform the post-shaving ablution assiduously for purity, remembering mentally the supreme Brahman Sadāśiva.

## \|\| 10.20.37 \|\|

*ityaiva muniśārddūla tava snehānmayākhilaḥ \|*  
*kṣaurasnānavidhiḥ proktaḥ kimbhūyaḥ śrotumicchasi \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. O sage, thus out of affection for you, the rites of post-shaving ablution I have explained to you. What else do you wish to hear?

## Chapter 21 - The duties and rites up to the tenth day after the death of ascetics

## \|\| 10.21.1-2 \|\|

*vāmadeva uvāca \|*  
*ye muktā yatayasteṣāṃ dāhakarmma na vidyate \|*  
*mṛte śarīre khananaṃ taddehasya śrutaṃ mayā \|\| 1 \|\|*

*tatkarmācakṣva suprītyā kārtikeya guro mama \|*  
*tvattonyo na hi saṃvaktā triṣu lokeṣu vidyate \|\| 2 \|\|*

Vāmadeva said:—

1-2. There are no crematory rites for the liberated ascetics. I have heard that they are buried. O Kārttikeya, my preceptor, please mention that rite lovingly. There is none in the three worlds who can explain the same.

## \|\| 10.21.3-4 \|\|

*pūrṇāhaṃ bhāvamāśritya ye muktā dehapaṃjarāt \|*  
*ye tūpāsanamārgeṇa dehamuktāḥ paraṃgataḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*teṣāṃ gativiśeṣañca bhagavañchaṃkarātmaja \|*  
*vaktumarhasi suprītyā māṃ vicāryya svaśiṣyataḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. O lord, son of Śiva, considering me as your disciple please tell me lovingly the mode of departure for those who are liberated from the corporal cage after realising perfect identity with the Brahman and who have attained their goal after liberation through the path of Upāsanā.

## \|\| 10.21.5 \|\|

*\|\|sūta uvāca \|*  
*munivijñaptimākarṇya śaktiputrassurārihā \|*  
*prāhātyantarahasyaṃ tadbhṛguṇā śrutamīśvarāt \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. On hearing the request of the sage, the son of Śakti, destroyer of demons spoke about the great secret which had been heard from Śiva by Bhṛgu.

## \|\| 10.21.6 \|\|

*\|\| subrahmaṇya uvāca \|*  
*idameva mune guhyaṃ bhṛgave śivayogine \|*  
*uktaṃ bhagavatā sākṣātsarvajñena pinākinā \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. It is this secret, O sage, that was explained to Bhṛgu, the devotee of Śiva by the omniscient Pināka-bearing lord himself.

## \|\| 10.21.7 \|\|

*vakṣye tadadya te brahmanna deyaṃ yasya kasyacit \|*  
*deyaṃ śiṣyāya śāntāya śivabhaktiyutāya vai \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. O Brahmin, I shall explain it to you. It shall not be given to any or every one indiscriminately. It shall be given to a calm disciple equipped with devotion to Śiva.

## \|\| 10.21.8 \|\|

*samādhistho yatiḥ kaścicchivabhāvena dehabhuk \|*  
*asti cetsa mahādhīraḥ paripūrṇaśśivo bhavet \|\| 8 \|\|*

8\. If there is any ascetic who has quit his body while in trance with his devotion for Śiva he becomes Śiva himself.

## \|\| 10.21.9 \|\|

*adhairyyacitto yaḥ kaścitsamādhiṃ na ca viṃdati \|*  
*tadupāyampravakṣyāmi sāvadhānatayā śṛṇu \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. If there is such a person devoid of concentration who docs not attain Samādhi, I shall mention the means of remedy for him. Listen attentively.

## \|\| 10.21.10 \|\|

*tripadārthaparijñānaṃ vedāntāgamavākyajam \|*  
*śrutvā gurormukhādyogamabhyasetsa yamādikam \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. On hearing from his preceptor the lore of Vedānta and Āgamas along with the explanation he shall practise Yoga by means of Yama, Niyama etc.

## \|\| 10.21.11 \|\|

*tatkurvansa yatissamyakchivadhyānaparo bhavet \|*  
*niyamena mune nityaṃ praṇavāsaktamānasaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. O sage, practising it regularly the ascetic shall be attached to Praṇava and be well-engaged in the meditation on Śiva.

## \|\| 10.21.12 \|\|

*dehadaurbalyavaśato yadyadhairyyadharo yatiḥ \|*  
*akāmaśca śivaṃ smṛtvā sa jīrṇāṃ svāṃ tanuṃ tyajet \|\| 12 \|\|*

12\. If he is devoid of courage due to physical weakness, he shall remember Śiva with specific desires and cast off his worn-out body.

## \|\| 10.21.13 \|\|

*sadāśivānugrahato naṃdinā preritā mune \|*  
*ātivāhikarūpiṇyo devatāḥ pañca viśrutāḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. O sage, there are five deities in the forms of messengers who by the favour of Sadāśiva are put in the charge of Nandin.

## \|\| 10.21.14-16 \|\|

*ātmahantākṛtiḥ kācijjyottiḥpuṃjavapuṣmatī \|*  
*ahno'bhimāninī kācicchuklapakṣābhimāninī \|\| 14 \|\|*

*uttarāyaṇarūpā ca paṃcānugrahatatparā \|*  
*dhūmrā tamasvinī rātriḥ kṛṣṇapakṣābhimāninī \|\| 15 \|\|*

*dakṣiṇāyanarūpeti viśrutāḥ pañca devatāḥ \|*  
*tāsāṃ vṛttiṃ śṛṇuṣvādya vāmadeva mahāmune \|\| 16 \|\|*

14-16. One of them has the shape of a self-killer (fire). Another has a mass of splendour as body. A third is the presiding deity of the day. The fourth is the presiding deity of the bright half of the month and the fifth one is in the form of the sun’s transit from capricorn to cancer. These five are engaged in blessing. Another set of five deities is also well known: Dhūmrā, Tamasvinī, Rātri, Kṛṣṇapakṣābhimāninī, Dakṣiṇāyana. O great sage Vāmadeva, now listen to their activities.

## \|\| 10.21.17 \|\|

*tāḥ paṃcadevatā jīvānkarmānuṣṭhāna tatparān \|*  
*gṛhītvā tridivaṃ yāṃti tatpuṇyavaśato mune \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The five deities, O sage, take the souls interested in performing holy rites to the heaven by their merit.

## \|\| 10.21.18 \|\|

*bhuktvā bhogānyathoktāṃśca te tatpuṇyakṣaye punaḥ \|*  
*mānuṣaṃ lokamāsādya bhajate janmapūrvavat \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. After enjoying the pleasures in heaven as mentioned, at the diminution of the merit they return to the human realm and take births as before.

## \|\| 10.21.19-20 \|\|

*tāḥ punaḥ paṃcadhā mārgaṃ vibhajyārabhya bhūtalam \|*  
*agnyādikramatāṃ gṛhyaṃ sadāśivapadaṃ yatiḥ \|\| 19 \|\|*

*ninīya vandyacaraṇau devadevasya pṛṣṭhataḥ \|*  
*tiṣṭhaṃtyanugrahākārāḥ karmmaṇyeva prayojitāḥ \|\| 20 \|\|*

19-20. The deities employed in activities divide the sphere beginning with the earth into five in the order of fire etc. They then take the ascetic to Sadāśiva’s region. Having led him to the honoured feet of the lord, the deities of blessing stand behind the lord.

## \|\| 10.21.21-22 \|\|

*samāgatamabhiprekṣya devadevassadā śivaḥ \|*  
*viraktaścenmahāmaṃtratātparyamupadiśya ca \|\| 21 \|\|*

*svasāmyaṃ ca vapurdatte gāṇapatyebhiṣicya ca \|*  
*anugṛhṇāti sarveśaśśaṃkaraḥ sarvanāyakaḥ \|\| 22 \|\|*

21-22. On seeing him come, if he is unattached, Sadā-Śiva lord of the gods teaches him the purport of the great mantra; crowns him as the chief of the Gaṇas and gives him a body similar to his. Śiva lord of all, leader of all, blesses him thus.

## \|\| 10.21.23-31 \|\|

*mṛgaṭaṃkatriśūlāgryavaradānavibhūṣitam \|*  
*trinetraṃ candraśakalaṃ gaṃgollāsijaṭādharam \|\| 23 \|\|*

*adhiṣṭhitavimānāgryaṃ sarvadaṃ sarvakāmadam \|*  
*iti śākhāviraktaścedrudrakanyāsamāvṛtam \|\| 24 \|\|*

*nṛtyagītamṛdaṃgādivādyaghoṣamanoharam \|*  
*divyāmbarasragālepa bhūṣaṇairapi bhūṣitam \|\| 25 \|\|*

*divyāmṛtaghaṭaiḥ pūrṇaṃ divyāṃbhaḥparipūritam \|*  
*sūryakoṭipratīkāśaṃ caṃdrakoṭisuśītalam \|\| 26 \|\|*

*manovegaṃ sarvagaṃ ca vimānamanugṛhya ca \|*  
*bhuktabhogasya tasyāpi bhogakautūhalakṣaye \|\| 27 \|\|*

*nipātya śaktiṃ tīvratarāṃ prakṛtyā hyati durgamām \|*  
*kāntāraṃ dagdhukāmāntānmalayānalasuprabhām \|\| 28 \|\|*

*anugṛhya mahāmaṃtratātparyamparameśvaraḥ \|*  
*pūrṇohaṃ bhāvanārūpaḥ śaṃbhura smīti niścalam \|\| 29 \|\|*

*anugṛhya samādhiśca svadāsyaspandarūpiṇīḥ \|*  
*ravyādikarmmasāmarthyarūpāḥ siddhīranargalāḥ \|\| 30 \|\|*

*āyuḥ kṣaye padmayoneḥ punarāvṛttivarjitām \|*  
*muktiṃ ca paramāṃ tasmai prayacchati jagadguruḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

23-31. The ascetic is decorated with the deer-skin, axe, trident and the gesture of offering boons. It has three eyes. His matted locks shine by the splendour of Gaṅgā and the crescent moon. He is seated on a splendid aerial chariot. He is delegated the power of bestowing all desires. If he is attached the lord blesses him with an aerial chariot having the speed of the mind, capable of going everywhere, shining like a crore of suns, cool like a crore of moons, full of vessels with celestial nectar and divine water, decorated with divine garments, garlands, unguents and ornaments charming with the sounds of dance, music, Mṛdaṅga and other instruments and surrounded by Rudra virgins. When he has enjoyed all pleasures, when his eagerness for enjoyment subsides lord Śiva blesses him imparting to him the purport of the great mantra. He blesses him with immovable trance in the form “I am Śiva, I am perfect”. He blesses him with unrestricted Siddhis capable of creating the sun etc. The Siddhis are in the form of throbbings arising from realisation that he is the slave of Śiva. The preceptor of the world bestows on him the greatest salvation devoid of return even when the life of the lotus-born deity comes to an end.

## \|\| 10.21.32 \|\|

*etadeva padaṃ tasmātsarvaiśvaryyaṃ samaṣṭimat \|*  
*muktighaṃṭāpathaṃ ceti vedāṃtānāṃ viniścayaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

32\. The attainment of this region is the accumulation of all prosperity and glory. It is the highway to salvation, the conclusion of Vedāntas.

## \|\| 10.21.33 \|\|

*mumūrṣostasya mandasya yatessatsampradāyinaḥ \|*  
*yatayaḥ sānukūlatvāttiṣṭheyuḥ parita stadā \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. When the ascetic of good, tradition is slowly dying the other ascetics shall stand around him with their attitude in conformity with their wishes.

## \|\| 10.21.34-35 \|\|

*tatassarve ca te tatra praṇavādīnyanukramāt \|*  
*upadiśya ca vākyāni tātparyaṃ ca samāhitāḥ \|\| 34 \|\|*

*varṇayeyuḥ sphuṭaṃ prītyā śivaṃ saṃsmārayansadā \|*  
*nirguṇaṃ paramajyotiḥ praṇamya vilayāvadhi \|\| 35 \|\|*

34-35. They shall recite the statements beginning with Praṇava and explain their purport with the pious mind. They shall remind him of Śiva the great Nirguṇa splendour and make obeisance to him till he expires.

## \|\| 10.21.36 \|\|

*eteṣāṃ samamevātra saṃskārakrama ucyate \|*  
*asaṃskṛtaśarīrāṇāṃ daurgatyaṃ naiva jāyate \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. The procedure of obsequies shall be followed. If their bodies are not cremated they are likely to miss the good goal.

## \|\| 10.21.37 \|\|

*saṃnyasya sarvakarmmāṇi śivāśrayaparā yataḥ \|*  
*dehaṃ dūṣayatasteṣāṃ rājño rāṣṭraṃ ca naśyati \|\| 37 \|\|*

37\. Since they have renounced all their activities and have resorted to Śiva, if the king defiles their bodies his kingdom will be ruined.

## \|\| 10.21.38 \|\|

*tadgrāmavāsinaste'pi bhaveyurbhṛśaduḥkhinaḥ \|*  
*taddoṣaparihārāya vidhānaṃ caivamucyate \|\| 38 \|\|*

38\. The people of that village too will suffer distress. The following procedure shall be taken up to avoid that default.

## \|\| 10.21.39 \|\|

*sa tu nama hariṇyāya cetyārabhya vinamradhīḥ \|*  
*nama āmīvatkebhyāntaṃ tatkāle prajapenmanum \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Humbly the king shall begin with “obeisance to Iriṇya”\[1\] and recite the mantra ending with “obeisance to Āmīvatkas”.

## \|\| 10.21.40 \|\|

*oṃmityante japandevayajanampūrayettataḥ \|*  
*tataśśāntirbhavettasya doṣasya hi munīśvara \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. Repeating Om in the end he shall complete the worship of the lord. O great sage, this procedure will quell the defect.

## \|\| 10.21.41 \|\|

*putrādayo yathā nyāyaṃ kuryyussaṃskāramuttamam \|*  
*vacmi tatkṛpayā vipra sāvadhānatayā śṛṇu \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. His sons and relatives shall perform the obsequies according to the rules.

## \|\| 10.21.42-43 \|\|

*abhyarcya snāpya śuddhodairabhyarcya kusumādibhiḥ \|*  
*śrīrudracamakābhyāṃ ca rudrasūktena ca kramāt \|\| 42 \|\|*

*śaṃkhaṃ ca purataḥ sthāpya tajjalenābhiṣicya ca \|*  
*puṣpaṃ nidhāya śirasi praṇavena pramārjayet \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. He shall be bathed with water and worshipped with flowers etc. repeating the mantras of Śrīrudra, Camaka and Rudrasūkta in order. The conch shall be placed before him and he shall be sprinkled with the water therein. A flower shall be placed on the head and his body shall be wiped repeating the Praṇava.

## \|\| 10.21.44 \|\|

*kaupīnādīni saṃtyajya punaranyāni dhārayet \|*  
*bhasmanoddhūlayettasya sarvāṃgaṃ vidhinā tataḥ \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. The loin-cloth shall be taken off and fresh one shall be tied. His body shall be dusted with the ashes in accordance with the rules.

## \|\| 10.21.45 \|\|

*tripuṇḍraṃ ca vidhānena tilakaṃ candanena ca \|*  
*viracya puṣpairmālābhiralaṃkuryyātkalevaram \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. Tripuṇḍra marks shall be applied in accordance with the rules, and a sandal-paste mark shall also be made. The body shall be decorated with flowers and garlands.

## \|\| 10.21.46 \|\|

*uraḥ kaṇḍaśirobāhuprakoṣṭhaśrutiṣu kramāt \|*  
*rudrākṣamālābharaṇairalaṃkuryyācca maṃtrataḥ \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. Repeating the requisite mantras Rudrākṣa garlands shall be worn over the chest, neck, head, arms, wrists and cars in order.

## \|\| 10.21.47 \|\|

*sudhūpitaṃ samutthāpya śikyopari nidhāya ca \|*  
*paṃcabrahmamaye ramye rathe saṃsthāpayettanum \|\| 47 \|\|*

47\. After fumigating with incense the body shall be lifted and placed on a rope-swing. It shall be placed on a charming chariot of the form of five Bahmans.

## \|\| 10.21.48 \|\|

*oṃmādyaiḥ paṃcabhirbrahmamaṃtraissadyādibhiḥ krmāt \|*  
*sugaṃdhakusumairmālyairalaṃkuryyādrathaṃ ca tam \|\| 48 \|\|*

48\. The chariot shall be decorated with fragrant flowers and garlands with the five Brahma mantras beginning with Sadyojāta preceded by Om.

## \|\| 10.21.49 \|\|

*nṛtyavādyairbrāhmaṇānāṃ vedaghoṣaiśca sarvataḥ \|*  
*grāmampradakṣiṇīkṛtya gacche tpretaṃ tamudvahan \|\| 49 \|\|*

49\. The body shall be taken round in procession around the village to the accompaniment of dances, music and chanting of Vedic mantras.

## \|\| 10.21.50-56 \|\|

*tataste yatinaḥ sarve tathā prācyāmathāpi vā \|*  
*udīcyampuṇyadeśe tu puṇyavṛkṣasamīpataḥ \|\| 50 \|\|*

*khanitvā devayajanaṃ daṇḍamātrapramāṇataḥ \|*  
*praṇavavyāhṛtibhyāṃ ca prokṣya cāstīryya ca kramāt \|\| 51 \|\|*

*śamīpatraśca kusumairuttarāgraṃ tadūrdhvataḥ \|*  
*āstīrya darbhāṃstatpīṭhaṃ cailājinakuśottaram \|\| 52 \|\|*

*praṇavena brahmabhiśca pañcagavyena tāṃ tanum \|*  
*prokṣyābhiṣicya raudreṇa sūktena praṇavena ca \|\| 53 \|\|*

*śaṃkhatoyenābhiṣicya mūrdhni puṣpaṃ viniḥkṣipet \|*  
*tadgatasyānukūlo'sau śivasmaraṇatatparaḥ \|\| 54 \|\|*

*oṃmityatha samuddhṛtya svastivācanapūrvakam \|*  
*garte yogāsane sthāpya prāṅmukhaṃ syādyathā tathā \|\| 55 \|\|*

*gaṃdhapuṣpairalaṃkṛtvā dhūpaguggulunā tataḥ \|*  
*viṣṇo havyamiti procya rakṣasveti vadandadet \|\| 56 \|\|*

50-56. The ascetics shall dig a ditch as deep as the staff in a holy spot near a holy tree on the eastern or northern side. They shall sprinkle it with water repeating the Praṇava and the Vyāhṛtis. They shall spread the leaves of the Śamī tree and flowers with their tips pointing to the north. Above them Darbha grass shall be spread. His seat whether cloth, deerskin or mat of Darbha grass shall be placed over them. The body shall be sprinkled with Pañcagavya with Praṇava and Brahma mantras. It shall be bathed with the water from the conch repeating Rudrasūkta and the Praṇava. A flower shall be placed on the head. Repeating the Svastivācana mantras and Om the body shall be lifted and placed inside the ditch in the Yogic pose facing the east. It shall be decorated with fragrant flowers and fumigated with incense and fragrant gum resin repeating the mantra “O Viṣṇu, protect the Havya”.

## \|\| 10.21.57-58 \|\|

*daṇḍaṃ dakṣiṇahaste tu vāme dadyātkamaṇḍalum \|*  
*prajāpate na tvadetānyanyo maṃtreṇa sodakam \|\| 57 \|\|*

*brahmajajñānamprathamamitimaṃtreṇa mastake \|*  
*spṛśañjaptvā rudrasūktaṃ bhuvormadhye spṛśañjapet \|\| 58 \|\|*

57-58. The staff shall be placed in his right hand and the vessel full of water in the left, repeating the mantra “Prajāpate natvadetā”.\[3\] With the mantra “Brahmaja jñānam Prathamam” etc. his head and with the Rudra Sūkta the middle of the eyebrow shall be touched.

## \|\| 10.21.59 \|\|

*māno mahāntamityādicaturbhirmastakantataḥ \|*  
*nālikereṇa nirbhidyādavaṭaṃ pūrayettataḥ \|\| 59 \|\|*

59\. The skull shall be split with a coconut repeating the four mantras beginning with “Mā no Mahāntam” etc. Then the ditch shall be filled.

## \|\| 10.21.60-61 \|\|

*paṃcabhirbrahmabhisspṛṣṭvā japetsthalamananyadhīḥ \|*  
*yo devānāmupakramya yaḥ paraḥ sa maheśvaraḥ \|\| 60 \|\|*

*iti japtvā mahādevaṃ sāṃbaṃ saṃsārabheṣajam \|*  
*sarvajñamaparādhīnaṃ sarvānugrahakārakam \|\| 61 \|\|*

60-61. Touching the place, with the mind dwelling on nothing else, the performer of the obsequies shall repeat the five Brahma mantras and the mantra beginning with “Yo devānām” and ending with “Yaḥ paraḥ sa Maheśvaraḥ” he shall worship Mahādeva, Sāmba, the panacea for the ills of the world, the omniscient, the non-dependent and the blesser of all.

## \|\| 10.21.62 \|\|

*ekāratnisamutsedhamaratnidvayavistṛtam \|*  
*mṛdā pīṭhaṃ prakalpyātha gopaye nopalepayet \|\| 62 \|\|*

62\. Then a mud platform, two Aratnis square and one Aratni high, shall be erected and smeared with cow-dung.

## \|\| 10.21.63 \|\|

*caturasraṃ ca tanmadhye gaṃdhākṣatasamanviteḥ \|*  
*sugaṃdhakusumairbilvaistulasyā ca samarcayet \|\| 63 \|\|*

63\. A mystic diagram shall be inscribed in the middle and worshipped wilh fragrant flowers, Bilva leaves and Tulasī along with scented raw grains.

## \|\| 10.21.64 \|\|

*praṇavena tato dayāddhūpadīpau payohaviḥ \|*  
*dattvā pradakṣiṇīkṛtya namaskuryyācca paṃcadhā \|\| 64 \|\|*

64\. The waving of the lights fumigating with incense, milk and the food offering shall be offered with Praṇava. The grave shall be circumambulated. They shall them prostrate five times.

## \|\| 10.21.65 \|\|

*praṇavaṃ dvādaśāvṛttvā saṃjapya praṇamettataḥ \|*  
*digvidikkramato dadyādbrahmādyampraṇavena ca \|\| 65 \|\|*

65\. After repeating the Praṇava twelve times they shall again make obeisance. To every quarter and interstice food offering shall be offered with Praṇava.

## \|\| 10.21.66 \|\|

*evaṃ daśāhaparyyaṃtaṃ vidhiste samudāhṛtaḥ \|*  
*yatīnāṃ munivaryyāthaikādaśāhavidhiṃ śṛṇu \|\| 66 \|\|*

66\. Thus I have narrated the rites up to the tenth day. O great sage, now listen to the rites of the eleventh clay for ascetics.

## Chapter 22 - The rites on the eleventh day for the ascetics

## \|\| 10.22.1 \|\|

*\|\| subrahmaṇya uvāca \|*  
*ekādaśehni samprāpte yo vidhissamudāhṛtaḥ \|*  
*taṃ vakṣye muniśārdūla yatīnāṃ snehatastava \|\| 1 \|\|*

Subrahmaṇya said:—

1\. O sage, out of affection for you I shall explain the rite for ascetics on the eleventh day after death.

## \|\| 10.22.2-3 \|\|

*sammārjya vedīmālipya kṛtvā puṇyāhavācanam \|*  
*prokṣya paścimamārabhya pūrvāntampañca ca kramāt \|\| 2 \|\|*

*maṇḍalānyuttarāśāsyaḥ kuryātsvayamavasthitaḥ \|*  
*prādeśamātraṃ saṃkalpya caturasraṃ ca madhyataḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

2-3. The performer of obsequies shall sweep the platform and smear it with cow-dung. He shall sprinkle water with Puṇyāhavācana mantras five times from the west to the east. He shall sit down facing the north and draw the mystic diagrams himself, the middle square of 18 cms in length.

## \|\| 10.22.4 \|\|

*bindutrikoṇaṣaṭkoṇavṛttākārāṇi ca kramāt \|*  
*śaṃkhaṃ ca puratasthāpya pūjoktakramamārgataḥ \|\| 4 \|\|*

4\. The Bindu, triangle, hexagon and circle shall be drawn in order. The conch shall be placed in front in accordance with the rales of worship.

## \|\| 10.22.5 \|\|

*prāṇānāyamya saṃkalpya pūjayitvā sureśvarī \|*  
*devatāḥ pañca pūrvoktā ativāhikarūpiṇīḥ \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. He shall perform the Prāṇāyāma and after due Saṃkalpa rite, be shall worship the five deities mentioned as messengers before.

## \|\| 10.22.6-8 \|\|

*saṃtyajyottarato darbhān yaśca saṃspṛśate tataḥ \|*  
*paścimādi samārabhya ṣaḍutthāsanamārgataḥ \|\| 6 \|\|*

*maṇḍalāni ca teṣvantaḥ puṣpāṇyādhāya pīṭhavat \|*  
*oṃ hrī mityuktvāgnirūpāntāmativāhikadevatām \|\| 7 \|\|*

*āvāhayāmi nama ityantaṃ sarvatra bhāvayet \|*  
*darśayetsthāpanādyāstu mudrāḥ pratyekamādarāt \|\| 8 \|\|*

6-8. He shall cast off Darbha grass to the north and not touch it again. Beginning with the west the Maṇḍalas shall be worshipped according to the rules for the rite of “Sadutthāpana.” Flowers shall be stacked within them like a pedestal with “Om Hrīm. I invoke the messenger goddess in the form of fire, obeisance”. This latter portion is common to the five. The gestures of installation shall be shown severally to each.
