## \|\| 10.22.9 \|\|

*hrāṃhrīmityādinā kuryyādāsāmaṃgāni ca kramāt \|*  
*pāśāṃkuśābhayābhīṣṭapāṇicandropalaprabhāḥ \|\| 9 \|\|*

9\. The Aṅganyāsas for the deities shall be performed with Hrām, Hrīm etc. The messenger goddesses shall have the noose, goad, gestures of fearlessness and wish in their hands. They are as lustrous as the lunar stone.

## \|\| 10.22.10 \|\|

*raktāṃgulīyakacchāyarañjitākhiladiṅmukhā \|*  
*raktāmbaradharāḥ kārapadapaṃkajaśobhitāḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

10\. They have changed (or enhanced) the hue of the quarters by the lustre of their gemset rings. They wear red garments and shine with lotus-like hands and feet.

## \|\| 10.22.11 \|\|

*trinetrollāsivadanapūrṇacandramanoharāḥ \|*  
*māṇikya mukuṭodbhāsicandralekhāvataṃsitāḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

11\. They look charming with their faces resembling the full moon and brilliant with three eyes. They are decorated with the crescent moon shining above their ruby-set coronets.

## \|\| 10.22.12 \|\|

*kuṇḍalāmṛṣṭagaṇḍāśca pīnonnatapayodharāḥ \|*  
*hārakeyūrakaṭakakāṃcīdāmamanoharāḥ \|\| 12 \|\|*

12 Their swinging ear-rings tickle their cheeks. They have plump and elevated breasts. They are charming with their necklace, shoulderlets, bangles and girdles.

## \|\| 10.22.13 \|\|

*tanumadhyāḥ pṛthuśroṇyo raktadivyāmbarāvṛtāḥ \|*  
*māṇikyamayamaṃjīrasiṃjatpadasaroruhāḥ \|*  
*pādāṃgulīyakaśroṇīrmaṃjulātimanoharāḥ \|\| 13 \|\|*

13\. Their waists are slender and buttocks large. They wear divine red garments. The ruby-set anklets jingle and tinkle in their lotus-like feet. The rings in their toes are very charming.

## \|\| 10.22.14 \|\|

*anugraheṇa mūrtena śivavatkiṃ nu sādhyate \|*  
*tasmācchaktyātmamūrtena sarvaṃ sādhyaṃ maheśavat \|\| 14 \|\|*

14\. What blessing can be secured from a departed soul? Only from a powerful soul can everything be achieved as from Maheśa.

## \|\| 10.22.15 \|\|

*sarvānugrahakartraiva svīkṛtāḥ paṃcamūrtaya \|*  
*sarvakāryyakarā divyāḥ parānugrahatatparāḥ \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. The five deities capable of all activities and eager to bless others are accepted by the lord as the bestowers of bliss.

## \|\| 10.22.16 \|\|

*evaṃ dhyātvā tu tāḥ sarvā anugrahaparāśśivāḥ \|*  
*pādayoḥ pādyametāsāṃ dadyācchaṃkhodabindubhiḥ \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. After meditating on the auspicious deities eager to bless he shall offer Pādya at their feet with drops of water from the conch.

## \|\| 10.22.17 \|\|

*hasteṣvācamanīyaṃ ca mauliṣvarghyaṃ pradāpayet \|*  
*śaṃkhoda bindubhistāsāṃ snānakarma ca bhāvayet \|\| 17 \|\|*

17\. The water for Ācamana shall be offered in their hands and Arghya on their heads. He shall conceive of their ritualistic ablution with the drops of water from the conch.

## \|\| 10.22.18 \|\|

*raktāmbarāṇi divyāni sottarīyāṇi dāpayet \|*  
*mukuṭādīnyanarghyāṇi dadyādābharaṇāni ca \|\| 18 \|\|*

18\. Beautiful garments red in colour shall be offered along with upper cloths. Coronets and ornaments shall also be offered.

## \|\| 10.22.19 \|\|

*suvāsitaṃ ca śrīkhaṇḍamakṣatāṃścātiśobhanān \|*  
*surabhīṇi manojñāni kusumāni ca dāpayet \|\| 19 \|\|*

19\. Fragrant sandal wood, auspicious Akṣatas and charming fragrant flowers shall also be offered.

## \|\| 10.22.20-21 \|\|

*dhūpaṃ ca paramāmodaṃ sājyavarti ca dīpakam \|*  
*sarvaṃ samarpayāmīti praṇavaṃ hrīmupakramāt \|\| 20 \|\|*

*namo'ntaṃcatatodadyātpāyastamadhunāplutam \|*  
*sājyaśarkarayāpūpakadalīguḍapūritam \|\| 21 \|\|*

20-21. Fragrant incense and lamp with wicks soaked in ghee shall be offered saying “I dedicalc everything.” Om should be uttered in the beginning and Namaḥ at the end. Then milk pudding with honey shall be offered along with ghee, sugar, sweet pie and plantain fruits stuffed with jaggery.

## \|\| 10.22.22 \|\|

*pratyekaṃ kadalīpatre bharitaṃ ca suvāsitam \|*  
*bhūrbhuvassvariti procya prokṣṇādīni kārayeta \|\| 22 \|\|*

22\. Different articles shall be heaped on separate plantain leaves and rendered fragrant. They shall be sprinkled with water uttering “Bhuḥ Bhuvaḥ Svaḥ”.

## \|\| 10.22.23 \|\|

*oṃ hrīmiti samuccāryya naivedyaṃ vahnijāyayā \|*  
*pānīyaṃ nama ityuktvā parampremṇā samarpayet \|\| 23 \|\|*

23\. Naivedya shall be offered with; “Om Hrīm Svāhā”. With “Namaḥ” water shall be offered with great devotion.

## \|\| 10.22.24 \|\|

*tata udvāsayetprītyā pūrvato munisattama \|*  
*sthalaṃ viśodhya gaṃḍūṣācamanārghyāṇi dāpayet \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. O excellent sage, thereafter the rite of Udvāsana shall be performed with devotion. The place shall be mopped clean beginning with the cast. Then water for gargling and Ācamana shall be offered with Arghya.

## \|\| 10.22.25 \|\|

*tāṃbūlaṃ dhūpa dīpau ca pradakṣiṇanamaskṛtī \|*  
*vidhāya prārthayedetāśśirasyaṃjalimādadhat \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. After offering Tāmbūla, incense and lights, the circumambulation and obeisance shall be performed. The deities shall be prayed with the joined palms placed on the head reverentially.

## \|\| 10.22.26 \|\|

*śrīmātarassuprasannā yatiṃ śivapadaiṣiṇam \|*  
*rakṣaṇīya mprabruvantu parameśapadābjayoḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. “May the glorious mothers be delighted. At the lotus-like feet of Parameśa let them recommend the protection of the deceased ascetic who is desirous of attaining the region of Śiva.

## \|\| 10.22.27 \|\|

*iti saṃprārthya tāssarvā visṛjya ca yathāgatam \|*  
*tāsāmprasādamuddhṛtya kanyakābhyaḥ pradāpayeta \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. After ritualistically dismissing them along the way they had come, the remanents of the Naivedya shall be distributed among the virgins.

## \|\| 10.22.28 \|\|

*gobhyo vā jalamadhye vā nikṣipennānyathā kvacit \|*  
*atraiva pārvaṇaṃ kuryyānnaikoddiṣṭaṃ yateḥ kvacit \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. It shall be given to the cows or cast off into water: There is no other way of disposal. Pārvaṇa and not Ekoddiṣṭa Śrāddha shall be performed for an ascetic.

## \|\| 10.22.29 \|\|

*atrāyaṃ pārvaṇaśrāddhe niyamaḥ procyate mayā \|*  
*taṃ śṛṇuṣva muniśreṣṭha yena śreyo bhavettataḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. I shall now tell you the rules for the Pārvaṇa for your welfare. O sage, listen.

## \|\| 10.22.30-32 \|\|

*kartā snātvā dhṛtaprāṇa upavītī samāhitaḥ \|*  
*sapavitrakarastvasyāṃ puṇyatithyāmiti bruvan \|\| 30 \|\|*

*kariṣye pārvaṇaṃ śrāddhamiti saṃkalpya cottare \|*  
*dadyāddarbhānuttamāṃśca hyāsanārthaṃ jalaṃ spṛśet \|\| 31 \|\|*

*tatropaveśayedbhaktyā sābhyaṃgaṃ kṛtamajjanān \|*  
*āhūya caturo viprāñchivabhaktāndṛḍhavratān \|\| 32 \|\|*

30-32. The performer of the Pārvaṇa shall take his bath, perform Prāṇāyāma with pious mind wearing the sacred thread as usual. Wearing the Darbha ring he shall say “On this auspicious Tithi I shall perform the Pārvaṇa Śrāddha.” In the place of Āsana Darbha grass shall be placed. After Saṃkalpa Darbhas shall be cast off to the north. Water shall be touched. He shall invite four Śaiva brahmins who are steady in their rites. The brahmins shall take oil bath after which they shall be made to sit.

## \|\| 10.22.33-34 \|\|

*viśvedevārthaṃ bhavatā prasādaḥ kriyatāmiti \|*  
*ātmane bhavatā paścādantarātmana ityapi \|\| 33 \|\|*

*paramātmana ityevaṃ procya prārthyaṃ ca tānyatiḥ \|*  
*śraddhayā caraṇanteṣāṃ kuryyādyāthārthyamādarāt \|\| 34 \|\|*

33-34. “Ye all shall represent the Viśvedevas.” After saying this he shall say “obeisance to Ātman, Antarātman and Paramātman.” Then he shall touch their feet with devotion.

## \|\| 10.22.35 \|\|

*pādau prakṣālya teṣāntu prāṅmukhānupaveśya ca \|*  
*gandhādibhiralaṃkṛtya bhojayecca śivā grataḥ \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. The feet of the brahmins shall be washed and they shall be made to sit facing the east. After decorating them with scents, sandal paste etc., they shall be led in front of Śiva

## \|\| 10.22.36-39 \|\|

*gomayenopalipyātra darbhānprāgagrakalpitān \|*  
*āstīryya saṃyataprāṇaḥ piṇḍānāṃ ca pradānakam \|\| 36 \|\|*

*kariṣya iti saṃkalpya maṇḍalatrayamarcya ca \|*  
*ātmānamantarātmānaṃ paramātmanamapyataḥ \|\| 37 \|\|*

*caturthyantaṃ vadanpaścādimaṃ piṇḍamitīrayan \|*  
*dadāmīti ca samprocya dadyātpiṇḍānsvabhaktitaḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

*kuśodakaṃ tato dadyādyathāvidhividhānataḥ \|*  
*tata utthāpya vai kuryyātpradakṣiṇanamaskṛtī \|\| 39 \|\|*

36-39. The ground shall be smeared with cow-dung. Darbha grass shall be spread with their tips to the cast. He shall perform Prāṇāyāma and the rite of saṃkalpa “I shall offer the Piṇḍa.” He shall then worship the Maṇḍala Ātman, Antarātman and the Paramātman turning the face back. “I am giving these Piṇḍas,” saying this he shall offer the Piṇḍas with devotion. Water from the Darbha grass shall be offered. Getting up he shall perform circumambulation and the Namaskāra.

## \|\| 10.22.40 \|\|

*tato dattvā brāhmaṇebhyo dakṣiṇāṃ ca yathāvidhi \|*  
*nārāyaṇabaliṃ kuryyāttasminneva sthale dine \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. After giving gifts to the Brahmins according to the rules he shall offer Nārāyaṇa Bali at the same spot on the same day.

## \|\| 10.22.41 \|\|

*rakṣārthameva sarvatra viṣṇoḥ pūjāvidhiḥ smṛtaḥ \|*  
*kuryyādviṣṇormahāpūjāṃ pāyasānnaṃ nivedayet \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. Everywhere Viṣṇu shall be worshipped for protection. He shall perform the worship of Viṣṇu and offer milk pudding as Naīvedya.

## \|\| 10.22.42-43 \|\|

*dvādaśātha samāhūya brāhmaṇānvedapāragān \|*  
*keśavādibhirabhyarcya gandhapuṣpākṣatādibhiḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

*upānacchatravastrādi dattvā tebhyo yathāvidhi \|*  
*santoṣayenmahābhaktyā vividhairvaca naiśśubhaiḥ \|\| 43 \|\|*

42-43. He shall invite twelve brahmins who are masters of the Vedas. He shall worship them in the names of Keśava etc. with scents, flowers and Akṣatas. He shall propitiate them and offer them shoes, umbrella, cloth, according to the rules. He shall speak to them sweet, auspicious words.

## \|\| 10.22.44 \|\|

*āstīryya darbhānpūrvāgrānbhūssvāhā ca bhuvassuvaḥ \|*  
*praṇavādi procya bhūmau pāyasānnaṃ baliṃ haret \|\| 44 \|\|*

44\. He shall spread Darbha grass on the ground with their tips to the east. After saying “Bhūḥ Svāhā, Bhuvaḥ Svāhā, Suvaḥ Svāhā, Om.” He shall offer milk pudding as oblation.

## \|\| 10.22.45 \|\|

*ekādaśāha suvidhirmayā prokto munīśvara \|*  
*dvādaśāhavidhiṃ vakṣye śṛṇuṣvādarato dvija \|\| 45 \|\|*

45\. O great sage, the rite of the eleventh day has been mentioned by me. O brahmin, I shall now explain the twelfth day rites.

## Chapter 23 - The twelfth day rites for Yatis

## \|\| 10.23.1-2 \|\|

*subrahmaṇya uvāca \|*  
*dvādaśāhe samutthāya prātaḥ snātvā kṛtāhnikaḥ \|*  
*śivabhaktānyatīnvāpi brāhmaṇānvā śivapriyān \|\| 1 \|\|*

*vimantrya tānsamāhūya madhyāhna cāplutāñchucīn \|*  
*vidhivadbhojayedbhaktyā svādvannairvividhaiśśubhaiḥ \|\| 2 \|\|*

Subrahmaṇya said:—

1-2. He shall get up early in the morning on the twelfth day, take his bath and perform his daily rites. He shall invite some Śaiva ascetics or brahmin devotees of Śiva. After they had taken their baths they shall be fed at midday in accordance with the rules, offering them sweet and auspicious foodstuffs of various sorts.

## \|\| 10.23.3-4 \|\|

*sannidhau parameśasya paṃcāvaraṇamārgataḥ \|*  
*pūjayettasya saṃsthāpya prāṇānāyamya vāgyataḥ \|\| 3 \|\|*

*mahāsaṃkalpamārgeṇa saṃkalpyāsmadguroriha \|*  
*pūjāṃ kariṣya ityuktvā tato darbhānuspapṛśet \|\| 4 \|\|*

3-4. In the presence of Parameśa they shall be worshipped in accordance with the Pañcāvaraṇa rites after performing Prāṇāyāma. As in the case of Mahāsaṃkalpa he shall perform the Saṃkalpa rite thus, “I shall perform the worship of the perceptor.” He shall then touch the Darbhas.

## \|\| 10.23.5 \|\|

*pādau prakṣālya cācamya svayaṃ kartā ca vāgyataḥ \|*  
*sthāpayedāsane tānvai prāṅmukhānbhasmabhūṣitān \|\| 5 \|\|*

5\. After washing his feet and performing Ācamana he shall make them sit facing east. Ashes shall be smeared all over the body.

## \|\| 10.23.6 \|\|

*sadāśivādikramato dhyāyedaṣṭau ca tatra tān \|*  
*parayā sambhāvanayetarānapi mune dvijān \|\| 6 \|\|*

6\. O sage, the eight—Sadāśiva etc. shall be meditated upon and the brahmins shall be treated with respect.

## \|\| 10.23.7 \|\|

*parameṣṭhiguruṃ dhyāyetsāṃbabuddhyā svanāmataḥ \|*  
*guruśca paramantasmātparāparaguruṃ tataḥ \|\| 7 \|\|*

7\. He shall then meditate on Parameṣṭhi Guru as Sāmba with his own name. Paramaguru and Parāt Paraguru shall also be meditated upon.

## \|\| 10.23.8-11 \|\|

*idamāsanamityuktvā cāsanāni prakalpayet \|*  
*praṇavādidvitīyāṃte svasya nāma samuccaran \|\| 8 \|\|*

*āvāhayāmi nama ityāvāhyārghodakena tu \|*  
*pādyamācamanaṃ cārghyaṃ vastragandhākṣatānapi \|\| 9 \|\|*

*dattvā puṣpairalaṃkṛtya praṇavādyaṣṭanāmabhiḥ \|*  
*sacaturthauṃnamo'ntaiśca sugandhakusumaistataḥ \|\| 10 \|\|*

*dhūpadīpau hi dattvā ca sakalārādhanaṃ kṛtam \|*  
*sampūrṇamastviti procya namaskuryātsamutthitaḥ \|\| 11 \|\|*

8-11. He shall say “Here is the seat” and offer seat. With Om as prefix his name shall be mentioned in the accusative case with the addition “I invoke, obeisance”. He shall then offer Pādya, Ācamana, Arghya, cloth, scents and Akṣatas. They shall be decorated with flowers. Their names shall be mentioned in the Dative case with Om prefixed and worshipped with fragrant flowers. After fumigating with the incense and offering lights he shall say, “The entire rites of propitiation are over. May they be perfect and complete” and perform obeisance and get up.

## \|\| 10.23.12-14 \|\|

*pātrāṇi kadalīpatrāṇyāstīryādbhiviśodhya ca \|*  
*śuddhānnapāyasāpūpasūpavyañjanapūrvakam \|\| 12 \|\|*

*dattvā padārthānkadalīnālikeraguḍānvitān \|*  
*pātrāsanāni ca pṛthagdadyātsamprocya ca kramāt \|\| 13 \|\|*

*pariṣicya ca samprokṣya viṣṇorhavyamiti bruvan \|*  
*rakṣasveti karasparśaṃ kārayitvā samutthitaḥ \|\| 14 \|\|*

12-14. Plantain leaves shall be spread and cleaned with water. Pure cooked nice, milk pudding, sweet pies, cooked dal, vegetable dishes, and other food stuffs shall be placed over them along with plantain fruits, coconuts and jaggery. Darbha grass is put under each leaf. These shall be sprinkled and the Pariṣecana, the sprinkling of foodstuffs shall be performed. He shall then say “Protect the Havya of Viṣṇu” and make them touch the articles.

## \|\| 10.23.15 \|\|

*āpośanaṃ samarpyātha prārthayettānidamprati \|*  
*sadāśivādayaḥ prītā varadāśca bhavantu me \|\| 15 \|\|*

15\. He shall stand up and offer giving water for drinking he shall request them thus, “May Sadāśiva and others be delighted with me and bestow boons on me”.

## \|\| 10.23.16 \|\|

*ye devā iti ca tato japtvedaṃ sākṣataṃ tyajet \|*  
*namaskṛtya samutthāya sarvatrāmṛtamastviti \|\| 16 \|\|*

16\. He shall then recite the mantra “Ye devā” etc. and scatter Akṣatas. He shall prostrate, stand up and say “Everywhere may there be nectar”.

## \|\| 10.23.17-23 \|\|

*uktvā prasādya ca japangaṇānāṃtvetyupa kramāt \|*  
*vedādīn rudracamakau rudrasūktaṃ ca paṃca ca \|\| 17 \|\|*

*brahmāṇi bhojanānte tu yāvanmantrāṃśca sākṣatān \|*  
*dattvottarāpośanaṃ ca hastāṃghrimukhaśodhanam \|\| 18 \|\|*

*kṛtvā cāntānsvāsaneṣu sthāpayitvā yathāsukham \|*  
*śuddhodakampradāyātha karppūrādi yathoditam \|\| 19 \|\|*

*mukhavāsaṃ dakṣiṇāṃ ca pādukāsanapatrakam \|*  
*vyajanaṃ phalakāndaṇḍaṃ vaiṇavaṃ ca pradāya tān \|\| 20 \|\|*

*pradakṣiṇanamaskāraissaṃtoṣyāśiṣamā vaheta \|*  
*punaḥ praṇamya samprārthya gurubhaktimacaṃcalām \|\| 21 \|\|*

*sadāśivādayaḥ prītā gacchantu ca yathāsukham \|*  
*ityudvāsya dvāradeśāvadhi samyaganuvrajan \|\| 22 \|\|*

*niruddhastaḥ parāvṛtya dvāsthairvipraiśca bandhubhiḥ \|*  
*dīnānāthaiśca sahito bhuktvā tiṣṭhedyathāsukham \|\| 23 \|\|*

17-23. Saying this he shall propitiate them. Then repeating the mantra “Gaṇānāṃtvā” he shall recite Rudra, Camaka, Rudrasūkta and Pañcasūkta. After the brahmins had taken their meals they shall he given as many Akṣatas as there are mantras. Water shall be offered for drinking and washing hands, mouth and feet. When they had performed Ācamana they shall be comfortably seated. Pare water shall be given again. Camphor betel leaves shall be offered and Dakṣiṇās given. Gifts of sandals, scats, leaves, fans, planks and bamboo staff shall be made. They shall be circumambulated and prostrated and their blessings received. Making obeisance again they shall be requested for unflinching devotion to the preceptor. Saying “May Sadāśiva and others go delightedly and comfortably” he shall bid farewell to them. Till the door he shall follow them. When they forbid him follow further, he shall return and take food along with the other brahmins, kinsmen and the poor. He shall then feel contented and happy.

## \|\| 10.23.24 \|\|

*vikṛtaṃ na bhavetkvāpi satyaṃ satyaṃ punaḥ punaḥ \|*  
*pratyabdameva kurvāṇo gurvārādhanamuttamam \|*  
*iha bhuktvā mahābhogāñchivalokamavāpnuyāt \|\| 24 \|\|*

24\. This is never affected. It is the truth. He who performs the rite of propitiation of the preceptor every year enjoys great pleasure here and attains Śivaloka hereafter.

## \|\| 10.23.25 \|\|

*\|\| sūta uvāca \|*  
*evaṃ kṛtānugrahamātmaśiṣyaṃ śrīvāmadevaṃ munivaryyamuktvā \|*  
*prasannadhīrjñānivaro mahātmā kṛtvā parānugrahamāśu devaḥ \|\| 25 \|\|*

25\. After saying this to Vāmadeva his disciple blessed by himself, the wise lord Subrahmaṇya delighted in mind said:—

## \|\| 10.23.26 \|\|

*yannaimiṣāraṇyamunīśvarāṇāṃ proktaṃ purā vyāsamunīśvareṇa \|*  
*tasmādasāvādigururbhavāṃstu dvitīya āryyo bhuvane prasiddhaḥ \|\| 26 \|\|*

26\. Since formerly this was mentioned to the sages of the Naimiṣa forest by the sage Vyāsa he is our first preceptor. You are the second one famous in the world.

## \|\| 10.23.27 \|\|

*śrutvā munīndro bhavato mukhājjātsanatkumāraḥ śivabhaktipūrṇaḥ \|*  
*vyāsāya vaktā sa ca śaivavaryyaśśukāya vaktā bhavitā ca pūrṇaḥ \|\| 27 \|\|*

27\. Hearing this from the lotus-like mouth of yours the great sage Sanatkumāra, full of devotion to Śiva, shall mention this to Vyāsa. That excellent devour of Śiva will mention this to Śuka.

## \|\| 10.23.28 \|\|

*pratyekaṃ muniśārdūlaṃ śiṣyavargacatuṣṭayam \|*  
*vedādhyayanasaṃvṛttaṃ dharmasthāpanapūrvakam \|\| 28 \|\|*

28\. For every leading sage there will be four disciples. They will be masters of Vedic study and establish virtuous sacred rites.

## \|\| 10.23.29 \|\|

*vaiśampāyana eva syātpailo jaiminireva ca \|*  
*sumantuśceti catvāro vyāsaśiṣyā mahaujasaḥ \|\| 29 \|\|*

29\. The four disciples of Vyāsa are Vaiśampāyana, Paila, Jaimini and Sumantu.

## \|\| 10.23.30 \|\|

*agastyaśca pulastyaśca pulahaḥ kratureva ca \|*  
*tava śiṣyā mahātmāno vāmadeva mahāmune \|\| 30 \|\|*

30\. O sage Vāmadeva, your disciples are Agastya, Pulastya, Pulaha and Kratu.

## \|\| 10.23.31-32 \|\|

*sanakaśca sanandaśca sanātanamunistataḥ \|*  
*sanatsujāta ityete yogivaryāḥ śivapriyāḥ \|\| 31 \|\|*

*sanatkumāraśiṣyāste sarvavedārthavittamāḥ \|*  
*guruśca paramaścaiva parātparagurustataḥ \|*  
*parameṣṭhiguruścaite pūjyāssyuśśukayoginaḥ \|\| 32 \|\|*

31-32. Sanaka, Sananda, Sanātanamuni, and Sanatsujāta, these great yogins, favourites of Śiva, the knowers of the Vedas are the disciples of Sanatkumāra. Thus these preceptors, great preceptors etc. are worthy of being worshipped by the yogin Śuka.

## \|\| 10.23.33 \|\|

*idaṃ praṇavavijñānaṃ sthitaṃ vargacatuṣṭaye \|*  
*sarvotkṛṣṭanidānaṃ ca kāśyāṃ sanmuktikāraṇam \|\| 33 \|\|*

33\. This perfect knowledge of Praṇava is well established in the four classes of disciples. Being the cause of great glory it is the cause of salvation at Kāśī.

## \|\| 10.23.34 \|\|

*etanmaṇḍalamadbhutaṃ paraśivādhiṣṭhāna rūpaṃ sadāvedāntārthavicārapūrṇamatibhiḥ pūjyaṃ yatīndraiḥ param \|*  
*vedādipravibhāgakalpitamahākāśādināpyāvṛtantvatsaṃtoṣakaraṃ tathāstu jagatāṃ śreyaskaraṃ śrīpradam \|\| 34 \|\|*

34\. This Maṇḍala as the seat of the great Śiva is wonderful. It is worshipped by the great sages conversant with the essence of Vedānta. It is enveloped by the ether as mentioned in the Vedas. May it be conducive to your satisfaction and the welfare of the world. May it yield glory and prosperity.

## \|\| 10.23.35 \|\|

*idaṃ rahasyamparamaṃ śivoditaṃ vedāntasiddhātaviniścitamparam \|*  
*mattaśśrutaṃ yadbhavatā tato mune bhavanmatamprājñatamā vadaṃti \|\| 35 \|\|*

35\. This is the secret par excellence as mentioned by Śiva. It is the conclusion of the Vedānta tenet. O sage, since it has been heard by you from me people call it as your tenet.

## \|\| 10.23.36 \|\|

*tasmādanenaiva pathā gataśśivaṃ śivohamasmīti śivo bhavedyatiḥ \|*  
*pitāmahādipravibhāgamuktaye nadyo yathāsindhumimāḥ prayānti \|\| 36 \|\|*

36\. Hence an ascetic who treads along this path attains Śiva like the rivers that fall into the sea. By the repeated practice of the meditation “I am Śiva”, the ascetic becomes Śiva. He can grant salvation even to the class of beings Brahma etc.

## \|\| 10.23.37-38 \|\|

*śrīsūta uvāca \|*  
*evammunīśvarāyaitadupadiśya sureśvaraḥ \|*  
*saṃsmṛtya caraṇāmbhoje pitro ssarvvasurārcite \|\| 37 \|\|*

*kailāsaśikharamprāpa kumāraśśikharāvṛtam \|*  
*rājitamparamāścaryyadivyajñānaprado guruḥ \|\| 38 \|\|*

37-38. After teaching this to, the sage the lord of gods remembered the lotus-like feet of his parents worshipped by the gods. Kumāra reached Kailāsa of many peaks, Kumāra the preceptor who bestows perfect knowledge.

## \|\| 10.23.39 \|\|

*vāmadevo'pi sacchiṣyaissaṃvṛtaśśikhivāhanam \|*  
*sampraṇamya jagāmāśu kailāsamparamādbhutam \|\| 39 \|\|*

39\. Accompanied by his disciples and making obeisance to the peacock-vehicled lord, Vāmadeva too immediately left for the wonderful mountain Kailāsa.

## \|\| 10.23.40 \|\|

*gatvā kailāsaśikharamprāpyeśanikaṭammuniḥ \|*  
*dadarśa mokṣadammāyānāśañcaraṇamīśayoḥ \|\| 40 \|\|*

40\. After reaching the summit of Kailāsa the sage approached Śiva. He saw the feet of the lord and the goddess—the feet that bestow salvation and destroy illusion.

## \|\| 10.23.41 \|\|

*bhaktyā cārpitasarvāṃgo vismṛtya svakalevaram \|*  
*papāta sannidhau bhūyo bhūyo natvā samu tthitaḥ \|\| 41 \|\|*

41\. With great devotion he dedicated himself to him. He prostrated again and again and then got up.

## \|\| 10.23.42 \|\|

*tato bahuvidhaiḥ stotrairvedāgamarasotkaṭaiḥ \|*  
*tuṣṭāva parameśānaṃ sāṃbikaṃ sasutaṃ muniḥ \|\| 42 \|\|*

42\. Then the sage, with various Vedic and Śāstric hymns eulogised Śiva together with Ambā and their sons.

## \|\| 10.23.43 \|\|

*nidhāya caraṇatmbhojandeva devyossvamūrddhani \|*  
*pūrṇānugrahamāsādya tatraiva nyavasatsukham \|\| 43 \|\|*

43\. He put the lotus-like feet of the lord and the goddess on his head. With their blessings he stayed there comfortably.

## \|\| 10.23.44-45 \|\|

*bhavanto'pi viditvaivampraṇavārthammaheśvaram \|*  
*vedaguhyaṃ ca sarvasvantāra kambrahma muktidam \|\| 44 \|\|*

*atraiva sukhamāsīnāḥ śrīviśveśvarapādayoḥ \|*  
*sāyujyarūpāmatulāmbhajadhvammuktimuttamām \|\| 45 \|\|*

44-45. All of you too shall learn the meaning of the Praṇava Maheśvara, the secret of the Vedas, of the Tāraka Brahman, the bestower of salvation. Remaining here itself comfortably you will attain the excellent salvation, the unequalled Sāyujya with the feet of Śrīviśveśvara.

## \|\| 10.23.46 \|\|

*ahaṃ gurupadāmbhojasevāyai bādarāśramam \|*  
*gamiṣye bhavatāmbhūyassatsambhāṣaṇamastu me \|\| 46 \|\|*

46\. I shall go to Badarikāśrama for serving the lotus like feet of my preceptor. Let us aspire for the opportunity of having excellent talk again.
